### [1] [Benchmarking Machine-Learning Top Taggers from JetClass to a Full-Simulation $Z'\to t\bar{t}$ Search: Transfer, Extrapolation, Sensitivity and Inference Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15274)
*Manisha Kumari,Amal Sarkar*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究在真实CMS模拟数据（含pileup）上系统评估七种机器学习顶夸克标记器（包括ParT、UParT、GloParT等粒子Transformer）对Z′→tt̄共振搜寻的物理增益，发现ROC曲线下面积相近的模型在发现显著性上可差达3倍，粒子Transformer将质量探测上限提升400 GeV，且其优势随共振质量增大而增强；pileup影响微弱，轻量CNN在低计算成本下具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习顶夸克标记器多在理想化快模拟（无pileup、窄pT窗口）下比较，其基准排名无法反映其在真实搜索中的实际物理增益，亟需在真实探测器模拟与完整实验条件下进行端到端性能评估。

Method: 在JetClass统一训练七种标记器（BDT、图像CNN、ParticleNet、ParT/UParT/GloParT），随后在Geant4级2016 CMS开放数据（含真实pileup）的Z′→tt̄搜寻中进行零样本迁移应用（no retraining），覆盖m_Z′ = 1.2–2.5 TeV，重点分析jet-level discovery significance、mass reach、pileup鲁棒性及计算成本。

Result: 粒子Transformer（ParT/UParT/GloParT）在完全合并的顶夸克喷注中展现出最优质量探测上限（+400 GeV），且其优势随m_Z′增大而增强；AUC相近的标记器在发现显著性上差异可达3倍；pileup对所有标记器影响<5%；小型CNN在计算成本降低约10×前提下保持竞争性鉴别力；各组间性能差距在重训练与统计误差范围内稳定。

Conclusion: 标记器选择不应仅依赖基准AUC分数，而应综合考量其运动学覆盖范围（尤其高pT外推能力）、分析工作点、发现灵敏度增益及计算开销；粒子Transformer因优异的高动量泛化性与稳定性，成为TeV尺度tt̄共振搜寻的首选架构。

Abstract: Searches for heavy resonances decaying to \(t\bar{t}\) test physics beyond the Standard Model at the TeV scale, and their reach depends on how well boosted top jets are identified. Machine-learning top taggers are usually compared on fast-simulation samples without pileup and in a narrow jet-\(p_T\) window, so a benchmark ranking does not directly show how much each tagger adds to a real search. This study trains seven taggers under identical conditions on JetClass. They are a boosted decision tree, two image-based networks, ParticleNet, and three particle transformers (ParT, UParT, and GloParT). The taggers are then applied without retraining to a \(Z'\to t\bar{t}\) search with \(m_{Z'}\) between 1.2 and 2.5~TeV, using 2016 CMS Open Data simulated with Geant4 and realistic pileup. The overall benchmark order of the taggers is preserved. However, taggers with similar area under the ROC curve (AUC) differ by up to a factor of three in jet-level discovery significance. The particle transformers extend the mass reach of the search by \(400~\mathrm{GeV}\), and their lead grows with resonance mass. This gain is seen in fully merged top jets and is associated with stable performance at jet \(p_T\) above the training range. Pileup has only a minor effect on all taggers. A small convolutional network achieves competitive discrimination at a much lower computational cost. The gaps between tagger groups hold under retraining and within statistical uncertainties. The results show that a tagger must be chosen on its kinematic coverage, the working point of the analysis, its discovery sensitivity and its cost, and not on its benchmark score alone.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文首次对Λc+→nKs0π+衰变进行了振幅分析，利用BESIII实验在4.600–4.699 GeV质心能量下采集的4.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，识别出四个中间共振态：Λ(1830)⁰、Λ(2000)⁰、N(1440)⁺和N(1650)⁺，并测量了其联合分支比与衰变不对称参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解粲重子Λc⁺的非轻子衰变机制，检验QCD启发模型与SU(3)味对称性预言，特别是通过多体末态振幅分析提取激发超子与核子共振态的参与强度及自旋-宇称信息。

Method: 基于最大似然拟合的不变质量与角度分布联合振幅分析（Dalitz-plot + helicity formalism），使用BESIII探测器重建的Λc⁺→nKs0π⁺事例，约束中间共振态的相对相位、模量与自旋密度矩阵元，并采用迭代拟合处理干涉效应。

Result: 首次观测到Λ(1830)⁰与Λ(2000)⁰在Λc⁺→nKs0π⁺中的显著贡献；测得四个共振道的乘积分支比（10⁻³量级）及对应的衰变不对称参数α，其中Λ(1830)⁰π⁺与Λ(2000)⁰π⁺通道呈现强正负极化不对称，暗示J^P=3/2⁻与1/2⁻等不同自旋宇称组分主导。

Conclusion: Λc⁺→nKs0π⁺衰变中存在显著的重子激发态共振贡献，Λ(1830)⁰与Λ(2000)⁰的高统计显著性支持其作为Λ(1116)的轨道激发态解释，而N(1440)⁺/N(1650)⁺信号揭示了s-channel与u-channel图幅的竞争，为Λc⁺衰变中重子谱与QCD色相干机制提供新约束。

Abstract: We perform the first amplitude analysis of the charmed baryon hadronic decay $\Lambda_c^+\to n K^0_{\mathrm{S}} \pi^+$. The analysis uses $e^+ e^-$ annihilation data corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 4.5~$\rm{fb}^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII spectrometer at center-of-mass energies between 4.600 GeV and 4.699~GeV. The amplitude analysis identifies four intermediate resonances $\Lambda(1830)^{0}$, $\Lambda(2000)^{0}$, $N(1440)^{+}$ and $N(1650)^{+}$. We measure the products of branching fractions: $$\mathcal{B}(\Lambda_{c}^{+} \rightarrow \Lambda(1830)^{0}\pip)\cdot \mathcal{B}(\Lambda(1830)^{0} \rightarrow n \Ks) = (2.4\pm0.7\pm0.5\pm0.1)\times 10^{-3}, \mathcal{B}(\Lambda_{c}^{+} \rightarrow \Lambda(2000)^{0}\pip)\cdot \mathcal{B}(\Lambda(2000)^{0} \rightarrow n \Ks) = (5.4\pm1.7\pm1.1\pm0.3)\times 10^{-3}, \mathcal{B}(\Lambda_{c}^{+} \rightarrow N(1440)^{+}\Ks)\cdot \mathcal{B}(N(1440)^{+} \rightarrow n \pip) = (4.0\pm2.0\pm1.5\pm0.2)\times 10^{-3}, \mathcal{B}(\Lambda_{c}^{+} \rightarrow N(1650)^{+}\Ks)\cdot \mathcal{B}(N(1650)^{+} \rightarrow n \pip) = (5.2\pm1.1\pm0.8\pm0.3)\times 10^{-3},$$ where the first uncertainties are statistical, the second are systematic, and the third are related to the normalizing branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(\Lambda_c^+\to n K^0_{\mathrm{S}} \pi^+)$. In addition, we measure the decay asymmetry parameters: $$\alpha_{\Lambda(1830)^{0}\pip} = \phantom{-}0.93^{+0.07}_{-0.72}\pm0.07,\alpha_{\Lambda(2000)^{0}\pip} = -0.91^{+0.56}_{-0.09}\pm0.14,\alpha_{N(1440)^{+}\Ks} = -0.13\pm0.43\pm0.35,\alpha_{N(1650)^{+}\Ks} = -0.42\pm0.45\pm0.29.$$

</details>


### [3] [Adaptive Matrix-Based Denoising Algorithm Using Local Spatial Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15544)
*Zhaoli Guo,Qingyu Li,Baolin Zhang,Jiarui Zhao,Aonan Zhu,Liang Peng †,Huilin Li †*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部空间支撑的自适应矩阵去噪算法，用于高亮度对撞机设施中高事例率、高本底条件下的高层触发（HLT）在线数据处理；在STCF主漂移室（MDC）上验证表明，该算法在多种物理道模拟中对离散点簇本底抑制率超90%，真实击中保留率高于98%，且具有低延迟、高精度与高吞吐特性，适用于规则结构探测器的通用在线去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满足高亮度对撞机（如STCF）在高事例率与高本底环境下高层触发（HLT）系统对在线数据处理的严苛需求，特别是上游去噪模块所需的低延迟、高精度与强鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一种基于局部空间支撑的自适应矩阵去噪算法，其滤波准则仅依赖于探测器击中点之间的局部空间关系，不预设轨迹参数或特定物理过程模型；在STCF主漂移室（MDC）上实现并测试。

Result: 在OSCAR模拟的五种带电末态物理道中，离散点簇本底抑制率>90%（低至中等本底），真实击中保留率>98%；即使探测器效率降至90%，保留率仍>98%；平均处理时间低且可预测，满足HLT上游去噪模块性能要求。

Conclusion: 该算法具备通用性、鲁棒性与实时性，为高亮度对撞机中规则结构探测器（如MDC、TPC等）的在线去噪提供了一种不依赖轨道拟合或物理模型的新型研究范式。

Abstract: To address the online data-processing requirements of the high-level trigger (HLT) under high-event-rate and high-background conditions in high-luminosity collider facilities, this paper proposes an adaptive matrix-based denoising algorithm using local spatial support. The algorithm is implemented and tested in the main drift chamber (MDC) of the Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF). Based on tests with five charged-final-state physics channels simulated using the Offline Software of the Super Tau Charm Facility (OSCAR), the rejection rate for discrete point-cluster backgrounds exceeds 90% at low to moderate background levels, while the true-hit retention ratio remains above 98%. Even when the detector efficiency is reduced to 90%, the true-hit retention ratio remains above 98%. These results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm satisfies the stringent online-processing constraints of an HLT upstream denoising module, including low and predictable average processing time, high precision, and high throughput. Because its filtering criterion is derived from local spatial relationships among detector hits rather than predefined track parameters or a model of a specific physical process, the proposed algorithm provides a general research approach for online denoising in regularly structured detectors used in high-luminosity collider facilities.

</details>

*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验在13.6 TeV质子-质子对撞数据中首次观测到稀有衰变η → μ⁺μ⁻π⁺π⁻，并以更高精度测量了η → μ⁺μ⁻μ⁺μ⁻相对于η → μ⁺μ⁻的分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验轻强子物理中的手征微扰论（ChPT）预言及轻夸克味改变过程的幺正性；探索可能的新物理效应（如暗光子、轻标量粒子）对稀有η衰变的贡献。

Method: 基于CMS探测器2022–2023年采集的62.6 fb⁻¹ 13.6 TeV pp碰撞数据，采用高精度μ子识别与动量重建、四体末态不变质量拟合、背景抑制（主要为η → γγ → 4μ/2μ2π混合事例及组合本底）、以及数据驱动与模拟联合的系统误差评估方法。

Result: 首次观测到η → μ⁺μ⁻π⁺π⁻衰变，测得分支比相对值为(2.89 ± 0.48_stat +0.42/-0.29_syst) × 10⁻³；η → μ⁺μ⁻μ⁺μ⁻/η → μ⁺μ⁻分支比为(0.86 ± 0.06_stat +0.09/-0.08_syst) × 10⁻³，统计精度提升44%。

Conclusion: 结果与NLO ChPT预言基本一致，但对NNLO修正和新物理耦合强度（如暗光子γ′–η混合角）构成更强约束；为后续高统计量Run 3分析奠定基准。

Abstract: An observation of the rare decay $\eta$ $\to$ $\mu^+\mu^-\pi^+\pi^-$ is reported from proton-proton collision data at a center-of-mass energy of 13.6 TeV, collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC in 2022 and 2023, and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 62.6 fb$^{-1}$. In addition, the branching fraction ratio of $\eta$ $\to$ $\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-$ relative to $\eta$ $\to$ $\mu^+\mu^-$ is measured with a precision improved by 44% compared with the previous measurement. The measured branching fraction ratios are $\mathcal{B}(\eta \to\mu^+\mu^-\pi^+\pi^-)/\mathcal{B}(\eta\to\mu^+\mu^-)$ = [2.89 $\pm$ 0.48 (stat) $^{+0.42}_{-0.29}$ (syst)]$\times$ 10$^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{B}(\eta\to\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-)/\mathcal{B}(\eta\to\mu^+\mu^-)$ = [0.86 $\pm$ 0.06 (stat) $^{+0.09}_{-0.08}$ (syst)]$\times$10$^{-3}$.

</details>


### [5] [Multi-approach Standard Model Effective Field Theory analyses of top-quark associated production in leptonic final states at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16347)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中采集的140 fb⁻¹数据，对含顶夸克的联合产生过程（如tt̄γ、tt̄Z等）进行SMEFT全局拟合，同时约束33个维度六算符的Wilson系数，未发现显著偏离标准模型的信号；比较了两种分析策略，表明针对单个过程优化的复杂方法在多数系数上具有更优灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC Run 2高精度数据下，亟需统一、自洽地解读多种顶夸克关联产生道以提升SMEFT新物理探针的全局约束能力，并评估不同分析策略对Wilson系数灵敏度的影响。

Method: 采用基于似然函数的全局拟合框架，覆盖9类顶夸克关联产生过程（tt̄γ, tt̄Z, ..., tHW），统一处理探测器层级的SMEFT效应；对比两类策略：(i) 基于末态物体计数的通用事件分类法，(ii) 针对各过程正交优化的专用区域法；对33个维度六SMEFT算符（含top-boson、四夸克、top-lepton及复数算符贡献）作联合约束。

Result: 所有33个Wilson系数的95% CL限制被给出，无统计显著偏离SM；第二类（专用区域）策略在绝大多数系数上给出更紧致约束；首次实现对tZq、tWZ、tHq、tHW等稀有道与主流tt̄X道的SMEFT协同拟合。

Conclusion: SMEFT全局解释框架可行且稳健；分析策略选择显著影响参数敏感度，未来高精度SMEFT研究应优先采用过程特异化、正交区域驱动的方法；当前结果为TeV尺度新物理提供了迄今最严格的多道联合约束之一。

Abstract: Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) interpretations of measurements of top-quark associated production processes in leptonic final states are presented using the full Run 2 proton-proton collision dataset recorded by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. A simultaneous likelihood fit is performed across final states sensitive to $t\bar{t}\gamma$, $t\bar{t}Z$, $t\bar{t}W$, $t\bar{t}H$, $t\bar{t}t\bar{t}$, $tZq$, $tWZ$, $tHq$, and $tHW$ production, with harmonised reconstructed final-state objects and a coherent treatment of detector-level SMEFT effects across all contributing processes. Two complementary analysis strategies are investigated: an approach employing a more generic event categorisation based on counting reconstructed objects, and a more complex approach combining orthogonal regions optimised to specifically identify individual processes, as employed by previous dedicated measurements. Constraints are obtained on the Wilson coefficients of dimension-six SMEFT operators affecting top-quark interactions, including top-boson, four-quark and top-lepton operators, together with complex operator contributions. The results do not show a significant deviation from the Standard Model expectation and 95% confidence interval limits are placed in a simultaneous fit to 33 SMEFT Wilson coefficients. The comparison of the two analysis strategies provides new insight into the design of increasingly complex SMEFT interpretations. The second, more complex, approach targeting individual processes results in the best sensitivity to the majority of Wilson coefficients.

</details>


### [6] [Light-Meson Spectroscopy at COMPASS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16397)
*David Spülbeck*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了对衍射产生末态ωπ⁰π⁻、Kₛ⁰K⁻和π⁻π⁺π⁻η的最新研究，重点提取π₁(1600)（自旋异号1⁻⁺混合介子候选者）的共振参数，并在b₁π、f₁π、η′π等衰变道中确认其存在；同时发现一个超出夸克模型预期的奇异-sector 0⁻超数态；首次基于事例的双Regge模型似然拟合被应用于高质量区η⁽′⁾π数据，以刻画非共振双Regge交换过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验QCD预言的自旋异号混合介子（特别是π₁(1600)），探索超出传统夸克模型的激发谱结构，并厘清非共振双Regge机制在轻介子产生中的作用。

Method: 对COMPASS实验中衍射产生的多体末态进行部分波分析（PWA），结合事件级最大似然拟合，分别提取共振参数与双Regge交换振幅；其中双Regge拟合为首个基于事例的实现。

Result: 在多个衰变道（b₁(1235)π, f₁(1285)π, η′π）中确立π₁(1600)的1⁻⁺量子数及质量、宽度；发现一个新0⁻奇异态（Jᴾ=0⁻），其存在无法由标准夸克模型解释；首次完成η⁽′⁾π高质区双Regge交换的事件级似然拟合，约束了非共振背景。

Conclusion: π₁(1600)作为最轻混合介子候选者的证据进一步增强；奇异sector出现超数态暗示可能存在四夸克态、分子态或胶球混杂成分；双Regge模型的成功拟合表明非共振机制在高能衍射过程中起主导作用，为未来光谱学分析提供了关键基准。

Abstract: We report on our latest findings from studying the diffractively produced $\omega\pi^0\pi^-$, $K_S^0K^-$, and $\pi^-\pi^+\pi^-\eta$ final states and determining the associated resonance parameters. A particular focus is on the signal of the lightest hybrid meson candidate with spin-exotic quantum numbers $J^{PC}=1^{-+}$, i.e. the $\pi_1(1600)$, which we observe in different decay channels including $b_1(1235)\pi$, $f_1(1285)\pi$, and $\eta^\prime\pi$. Furthermore, we highlight the observation of a supernumerary state with respect to predictions of quark models in the strange sector with $J^P=0^-$. In addition, the analysis of the non-resonant double-Regge exchange process using $\eta^{(\prime)}\pi$ data in the high-mass region is discussed. It is the first event-based likelihood fit of a double-Regge model and improves our understanding of non-resonant production mechanisms in light-meson spectroscopy.

</details>


### [7] [Performance of the ATLAS tau trigger in Run 3 and future prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16454)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ATLAS实验在LHC Run 3中用于识别强子衰变τ轻子的触发算法与选择策略，基于13.6 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据评估了其性能：单τ触发效率达90–98%，蒙特卡洛模拟的效率校正因子为0.95–1.05（不确定度2–3%），并讨论了面向2026年数据的触发级τ识别改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在高亮度LHC运行（Run 3）中高效、准确地遴选含强子衰变τ轻子的物理事例（如H→ττ、BSM新物理信号），需优化触发级τ识别算法以平衡效率、率宽与背景抑制。

Method: 基于ATLAS探测器2022–2023年采集的√s = 13.6 TeV pp碰撞数据，采用数据驱动方法标定τ触发效率；利用富含强子型τ末态的数据样本推导MC模拟效率的校正因子；并通过2026年测试链验证新型触发算法性能。

Abstract: The algorithms and selections used at the trigger level in the ATLAS experiment to select events with hadronically decaying tau leptons are described. The performance of the hadronic tau trigger is presented using proton-proton collision data recorded by the ATLAS experiment in Run 3 at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV at the Large Hadron Collider. In the plateau region of the efficiency curve for a given single tau trigger, efficiency varies between 90-98%. Correction factors for the simulated tau trigger efficiency in Monte Carlo samples were derived using 2022 and 2023 data enriched with hadronically decaying tau lepton final states, and these range from 0.95-1.05 with an uncertainty of 2-3% over a wide range of the transverse momentum of the hadronic tau candidates. In addition, potential improvements and their performance for the identification of hadronic tau candidates at trigger level are discussed using data recorded in 2026, having been implemented in selected tau trigger test chains.

Abstract: The Poisson $\sigma$-model (PSM) over a closed worldsheet $\Sigma$ is reinterpreted as a theory of connections on a trivial principaloid bundle $P_0 = \Sigma \times G \to \Sigma$ with the symplectic structure groupoid $(G,\omega)$ integrating the Poisson target $(M,\Pi)$, and that of sections of the shadow bundle $F_0 = \Sigma \times M \to \Sigma$ - as defined in 2503.09886 [math.DG]. The flat directions of the PSM are explained as linearisations of automorphisms of $P_0$, modelled on maps from $\Sigma$ into the group of lagrangean bisections of $G$, which induce Poisson automorphisms of $(M,\Pi)$. The gauge anomaly of the ensuing field theory is derived and demonstrated to vanish for prequantisable $\omega$. A general anomaly-cancellation mechanism is proposed, which couples the bare model - rewritten as the holonomy of a connection-dependent trivial gerbe on $F_0$ - to a background gerbe over $M$ endowed with a $G$-equivariant structure. The coupling takes the form of the tensor product of the two gerbes and the extended PSM is defined as the holonomy of the product gerbe. An essential generalisation of the theory is then proposed - to shadows $F$ of principaloid bundles $P$ of arbitrary topology, in keeping with the interpretation of the gauging as descent to the orbistack $M//G$. The generalisation uses local product gerbes depending on local connections over trivialisations of $F$. The $G$-equivariant structure is proven to ensure a 2-stacky descent of the local gerbes to a global one over $F$. Its holonomy is taken as the definition of the generalised PSM. A cohomological discussion of obstructions against the descent is provided. Finally, a reformulation of the generalised PSM as a field theory with a topological gauge-symmetry defect is given, in which the $G$-equivariant structure is employed - alongside the transition cocycle of $P$ - as the datum of the defect.

</details>

Abstract: We construct the canonical boundary algebras of Jackiw--Teitelboim gravity coupled to free bosonic matter. For a nonempty finite or countable family of species with lowest weights greater than one half, the two boundary algebras are mutual commutants and nonhyperfinite type $\mathrm{II}_\infty$ factors. Their nonzero finite corners are nonhyperfinite type $\mathrm{II}_1$ factors. The construction uses bounded heat-regularized fields and a Hilbert algebra, which gives a faithful normal semifinite trace without a path-integral argument. The weight restriction is sharp: elementary point fields remain unbounded after positive heat regularization when $0<d\leq1/2$. For one massless scalar, specified bounded observables and normal states have a high energy limit described by a hyperfinite continuous core. We explain why this limit does not preserve the nonhyperfiniteness of the exact algebra.

Abstract: We construct two-parameter Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators for the transfer matrices of noncompact open $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ BC-type spin chains with general boundary interactions, treating both one-boundary and two-boundary models. The $\mathbb{Q}$-operators are defined as traces of general monodromy matrices over infinite-dimensional auxiliary spaces carrying principal series representations of $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$. At specific values of parameters the two-parameter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators degenerate into simpler one-parameter Baxter operators. We provide both an algebraic derivation based on local operator identities and a diagrammatic proof using Feynman diagrammatic techniques. Finally, we apply the quantum Separation of Variables (SoV) method to the spectral problem of the one-boundary transfer matrix. We derive the system of separated difference equations for the transfer matrix eigenfunctions and show that, under the assumption of kernel factorization, it reduces to a one-dimensional difference equation coinciding precisely with the Baxter equation for eigenvalues of appropriately normalized one-parameter Baxter $Q$-operator.

Abstract: The paper presents a brief overview of recent advances in the mathematical theory of evolution equations for quantum many-particle systems. The structure of expansions representing non-perturbative solutions to the Cauchy problem for hierarchies of fundamental evolution equations for many quantum particles is outlined. It has been shown that the concept of cumulants of operator semigroups forms the basis for non-perturbative representation of solutions to hierarchies of evolution equations that describe the state and observables. It is proven that the constructed dynamics of correlations underlie the description of the evolution of the state, both for finitely and infinitely many particles, governed by the BBGKY hierarchy for reduced density operators or by the hierarchy of nonlinear evolution equations for reduced correlation operators. In fact, it is demonstrated that the behavior of particle correlations governs the dynamics of states in quantum systems with an infinite number of particles.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机Lotka-Volterra系统中相互作用与观测统计量（如丰度波动）之间的根本关系，特别是为何实验观测到的Gamma分布丰度波动常被误读为无相互作用，以及如何从时序数据中提取真实相互作用结构。

Method: 基于stochastic GENERIC框架构建metriplectic结构；利用log-canonical Poisson双矢量显式构造几何结构；通过涨落-耗散关系导出乘性噪声；运用Gamma分布性质与矩阵不等式分析稳态测度；推导Cov(x, log x)的Lyapunov型恒等式并估计其下界。

Result: 1) Metriplectic可表示性当且仅当Volterra耗散条件成立；2) 稳态为Gamma分布乘积，适用于任意满足条件的非对称A（含捕食-猎物）；3) 稳态分布仅依赖平衡点与C/k，不唯一确定A；4) 零滞后log-丰度协方差导数等于A(C/k)⁻¹，其反对称部分即Poisson结构，决定定态熵产；5) 精确Lyapunov恒等式AK+KᵀAᵀ+Q=0成立，且种群独立当且仅当噪声metriplectically兼容；6) 给出interspecies cross-covariance的显式下界，数值模拟显示该下界近乎紧致。

Conclusion: Gamma分布的丰度波动不能作为缺乏种间相互作用的证据；真实的相互作用结构完全编码于时序关联中，尤其是log-abundance协方差的动力学特征；metriplectic几何框架为解析随机生态动力学提供了统一的热力学与统计物理基础。

Abstract: We show that a Lotka--Volterra system with an interior equilibrium admits a metriplectic structure, with a Volterra function $V_C$ as energy and an entropy variable for the environment, if and only if Volterra's dissipativity condition $CA+A^{\top}C\le0$ holds, and we construct it explicitly with a log-canonical Poisson bivector. The interactions are, however, visible in time: the zero-lag derivative of the log-abundance covariance equals $A(C/k)^{-1}$, and its antisymmetric part is the Poisson part, which carries all the stationary entropy production, computed in closed form. Finally, for general noise we prove an exact Lyapunov-type identity $AK+K^\top A^\top+Q=0$ for $K=\mathrm{Cov}(x,\log x)$ at any stationary solution. It implies that the populations are independent if and only if the noise is metriplectically compatible, and gives an explicit lower bound on interspecies cross-covariances in terms of the distance of $Q$ to the compatible family. Abstract: We investigate the interplay between physics beyond the Standard Model and modified spacetime geometries in shaping the photon and neutrino spectra from evaporating black holes. We implement three representative supersymmetric (SuSy) benchmarks and a composite dynamics (CoDy) scenario in BlackHawk. We consider both Schwarzschild black holes and regular black holes geometries, namely polymerized, Hayward, and Simpson--Visser black holes. New degrees of freedom at the TeV scale become accessible at Hawking temperatures corresponding to black hole masses of approximately $M_{\rm BH}\sim 10^{10}\,\mathrm{g}$. We show that physics beyond the Standard Model produces distinctive signatures in both the instantaneous secondary-photon and neutrino spectra. Radiative decays generate narrow spectral features with approximately temperature-independent relative amplitudes, while multistep decay cascades produce broad enhancements that become increasingly pronounced at higher temperatures. In contrast, compressed mass spectra exhibit no localized features. Crucially, we show that the effects of new particle physics and modified spacetime geometries can become intertwined in specific energy regimes, altering the resulting spectral signatures. Our results demonstrate that photon and neutrino spectra provide complementary probes of new particle physics and black hole geometry, with predicted spectral enhancements extending into the energy ranges accessible to current and future high-energy observatories.

Abstract: Continuous spin particles (CSPs) are massless particles characterized by a dimensionful spin scale, $\rho$, which, when non-zero, changes their helicity under Lorentz boosts. Since CSPs reduce to ordinary particles when $\rho \rightarrow 0$, the photon or graviton could be CSPs and only appear to behave as ordinary particles due to a small value of $\rho$. Therefore, it is important to determine what value of $\rho$ is experimentally allowed. Recent work has shown that the physical effects of CSPs scale as powers of $\rho/\omega$ when $\rho \ll \omega$, where $\omega$ is a frequency scale, making low-frequency probes particularly beneficial for highlighting these phenomena. Pulsar timing arrays are uniquely suited for this application as they have exceptional sensitivity to low, nanohertz frequency gravitational waves, as demonstrated by their recent evidence for a stochastic gravitational wave background. Using standard Bayesian data analysis techniques, we find that the NANOGrav 15-year data set constrains the graviton spin scale to $\rho_\text{g} < 1.23 \times 10^{-23} \, \text{eV} $ at the 95% credible level.

For an incoming electron the $(B+L)$-violating channel is anti-nucleon synthesis, and we determine the branching ratios in two limits: the two channels are equally likely at high energy, and only the helicity flip remains at low energy, below the anti-nucleon threshold. In between we model the energy dependence. For a quark in a slow proton the $(B+L)$-violating channel is catalysed proton decay, which has no threshold and occurs with a probability close to one. Callan's statistical interpretation of the fractional state emerges in a sharper form: the state is not a superposition of two final states with equal weights, but a single state that decays into them with energy-dependent probabilities, equal only at high energy.

Abstract: Femtoscopic measurements in heavy-ion collisions indicate that the pion source is Lévy-stable rather than Gaussian, while the source radii still follow the hydrodynamic scaling $R^2\propto 1/m_T$. This yields a criterion for power-law source tails with two routes: either the outer flow is ultra-relativistic and the temperature grows at least linearly (Hubble-type solutions), or the flow is slow, the temperature uniform, and the proper acceleration falls off as $1/r$. The acceleration of $\lambda$-PF generates no power-law tail, but with a Hubble-type dressing its source tail is Lévy-stable in a range of parameters, generalising the Hubble-type Lévy source to accelerating cores. Thus Lévy-like sources and the hydrodynamic $m_T$ scaling are compatible in perfect-fluid hydrodynamics, but hydrodynamics does not create the power-law tail: it is either inherited from a power-law pressure, requiring a $1/r$ acceleration, or put in through the dressing. We take on this challenge with a triple threat. First, we perform an exact two-loop calculation in the full theory. Second, we derive the logarithmically enhanced terms from a general argument, according to which the singlet rescales the Higgs couplings to the electroweak gauge bosons and thereby spoils the cancellation of ultraviolet divergences between Higgs and Goldstone contributions. Abstract: The kinematic phase space is fundamental to all particle scattering processes across experiments - from lepton and hadron colliders to dark matter searches. This paper provides the first comprehensive analytic treatment of $2 \to 3$ phase space and cross section distributions that uses a non-constant matrix element and keeps all particle masses non-zero. This is fundamental for an accurate description of crucial configurations for Beyond Standard Model searches, e.g. Vector Boson Fusion. Using Gram Determinants as our primary language for Lorentz-invariant kinematics, we derive exact, closed-form expressions for differential cross section distributions and explicitly construct the complete physical phase space boundaries. This paper serves as both a pedagogical introduction and a practical reference for the analytic computation of realistic $2 \to 3$ processes, showing how the $m\neq0, |{\cal M}|\neq 1$ problem is computationally tractable through proper kinematic methods. However, as in the supervised case, the no free lunch theorem assures that the performance of unsupervised models depends on the specific signal hypothesis and their inductive biases, and the successful identification of a given signal by a particular algorithm does not guarantee comparable performance for other signals. In this study, we take a step toward a more model-independent unsupervised and semi-supervised search strategy by combining classifiers into ensembles. Our goal is addressing two complementary aspects of unsupervised ensemble learning - overall performance and robustness of the classification task from a thorough assessment of ensemble models. Curiously, the same coupling that fits the LZ data also yields the observed dark matter abundance at a mass of about 300 GeV, and is consistent with collider searches and indirect detection. The scenario is testable, predicting further events at lower energies, which the full LZ run will probe and a next-generation detector such as XLZD will decisively test.

Abstract: In this work we compare the Higgs Effective Field Theory (HEFT) predictions with the Two Higgs Doublet Model (2HDM) predictions for triple Higgs production via $WW \rightarrow hhh$ scattering, under the hypothesis of very heavy Higgs bosons $H$, $A$, and $H^\pm$, and assuming the lightest one $h$ has the observed mass in the experiments, $m_h \simeq 125$ GeV. We will also compare these solutions with the corresponding solutions found for the case of double Higgs production. The solutions presented here for the matched coefficients summarize the non-decoupling effects from the heavy modes, $H$, $A$ and $H^\pm$ in the scattering process $WW \rightarrow hhh$ and provide the most relevant contributions to the cross section $\sigma(WW \rightarrow hhh)$ that do not decrease as inverse powers of the heavy masses. In the last part, we will also discuss the numerical predictions of the cross section $\sigma(WW \rightarrow hhh)$ in terms of the HEFT-matched coefficients. The focus of this discussion is set on the big gap between the HEFT-matched prediction and the SM prediction due to the non-decoupling behaviour occurring whenever misalignment is assumed in the 2HDM, i.e. for $c_{\beta-\alpha} \neq 0$. Abstract: The sum of large Pomeron loops in QCD, based on the $t$-channel unitarity and on the solution of the Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) equation in the saturation region, has been combined with the AGK (Abramovsky, Gribov and Kancheli) cutting rules to obtain multiplicity distributions with KNO (Koba, Nielsen and Olesen) scaling and an entropy equal to the logarithm of the gluon structure function. The KNO functions that follow from the Gaussian representation of the asymptotic series have log-normal and not Gaussian tails, $\ln\Psi_{\rm BK}\simeq-\frac{1}{2}\ln^2\xi$ and $\ln\Psi_{\rm dd}\simeq-\frac{1}{4}\ln^2\xi$, and we give them in closed form in terms of the Lambert function. The fitted saturating form connects the nearly saturated light-flavor contribution with increasing attraction in the heavy-flavor region. In the common saturation limit, the leading chromoelectric contribution acts as a common mass shift for each constituent and can be absorbed into effective constituent masses; the residual pair term accounts for the flavor dependence away from saturation. With the chromomagnetic interaction included, this single parameter set reproduces the ground-state meson and baryon spectra and yields predictions for multiply heavy baryons outside the calibration set. By diagonalizing the sum of the chromoelectric and chromomagnetic mass matrices, we further obtain the ground-state spectra of $cc\bar c\bar c$, $bb\bar b\bar b$, and $bc\bar b\bar c$. The relative $S$-wave rearrangement-decay weights obtained from color--spin recoupling can be used to identify favored decay channels under the stated spatial-overlap approximation.

Abstract: In classically scale-invariant extensions of the Standard Model the electroweak scale is generated radiatively, and the absence of tree-level mass terms keeps the symmetric phase metastable down to very low temperatures. We study the resulting phase transition in a model with a real singlet and several additional scalars coupled to the Higgs doublet and to the singlet, in which electroweak symmetry is broken along a Gildener-Weinberg flat direction and a light dilaton appears in the spectrum. Computing the bounce along the two-dimensional tunnelling path and following the false-vacuum fraction through percolation and completion, we find that the transition proceeds in a single, strongly supercooled step and completes above the QCD scale over a region of parameter space consistent with Higgs measurements and perturbativity. Despite the strong supercooling the transition is fast. The bubble walls reach their terminal velocity early, and almost all of the released vacuum energy goes into bulk motion of the plasma, which thermalises and reheats the universe close to the scale set by the vacuum energy. The resulting entropy injection dilutes pre-existing relics by up to several orders of magnitude. It raises the baryon asymmetry that must be present before the transition, relaxes the unitarity bound on the mass of thermal dark matter, and suppresses earlier gravitational-wave backgrounds. If the acoustic description of gravitational-wave production extends to this regime, the signal peaks in the millihertz band, follows an approximate scaling relation between peak amplitude and frequency set by the vacuum energy, and would be observed by LISA throughout the viable region. The results depend on the multiplicity of the new scalars and their Higgs portal coupling at leading order through a single combination, and require the singlet scale to lie at or above the TeV scale.

Abstract: We investigate neutron star (NS) properties in symmetric teleparallel $f(Q)$ gravity while consistently accounting for uncertainties in the dense matter equation of state (EOS). The resulting posterior distributions simultaneously constrain the dense matter and gravity sectors and quantify their correlations within a common framework. The modified gravity parameters exhibit only weak direct correlations with most stellar observables, while a pronounced degeneracy remains within the gravity sector. Our results show that accounting for EOS uncertainties is essential for quantifying the interplay between dense matter physics and modified gravity and provide a systematic framework for confronting symmetric teleparallel gravity with nuclear and multimessenger NS observations.

Abstract: We formulate a local reconstruction of Lorentzian geometry from directed null arrival times and spacetime volume, assuming a supplied clock foliation and spatial coordinate gauge. For time-dependent ADM metrics we derive the exact future null indicatrix, its algebraic inverse, and a minimal finite-direction sampling scheme: $d(d+3)/2$ one-way arrival measurements suffice in $d$ spatial dimensions, are necessary for stable local recovery within the specified scalar-measurement class, and admit an exact semialgebraic consistency test with certified robustness and rejection margins under bounded measurement error. Abstract: We present cosmological constraints on the frozen model, in which dark matter and dark energy exchange energy only at the level of the perturbations. With shear included, $\Sigma=0.01\pm0.08$ and $S_8=0.808\pm0.025$, while $\ln B=-1.3$ favors $\Lambda$CDM. $H_0$ and the age of the Universe agree with $\Lambda$CDM for every data combination, and the SH0ES discrepancy remains at $4.4\sigma$. TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞图像中光线轨迹的几何结构，证明在Kerr和Plebanski-Demianski（PD）时空下，恒定最近接近半径的等值线构成圆锥曲线弧；利用PD场方程所导出的系数恒等式与主零方向对齐性质，在正质量、正能量区域可仅凭径向势能斜率的局域判据选取物理弧段；该结论不适用于穿越r=0区域或Ovcharenko-Podolsky可分离扩展情形；所得圆锥曲线族可导出包含临界曲线的单个多项式方程，从而将黑洞图像转化为与辐射模型无关的径向光子传播几何映射。

Abstract: Each point in a black hole image corresponds to a light ray, whose minimum radial coordinate when traced backward from the observer measures how deeply it probes the spacetime. We show that contours of constant closest-approach radius form arcs of conic sections in standard Bardeen image coordinates for Kerr, and in natural local image coordinates for finite-radius observers in the broader Plebanski-Demianski (PD) family of Einstein-Maxwell solutions. At each radius, the condition of vanishing radial velocity defines a complete conic; the physical contour consists of those points whose rays reach that radius before encountering an outer turning point. For positive Killing energy and positive mass, we prove that throughout connected positive-radius regions with positive radial metric function and allowed motion at both endpoints, a local test of the radial potential's slope is sufficient to select the physical arcs. This simplification follows from a coefficient identity imposed by the PD field equations and alignment of the principal null directions. We give explicit counterexamples in a domain crossing r=0 and in the separable Ovcharenko-Podolsky extension, where an outer turning point invalidates the local test. The conic family also yields a single polynomial equation containing the critical curve in Kerr and PD. These results turn the returning image into a geometric map of radial photon propagation, independent of the emission model. (This abstract has been abridged to meet the arXiv character limit.)

</details>

Abstract: The adiabatic approximation, in which eccentric binaries evolve under orbit-averaged fluxes, is widely used in gravitational-wave and astrophysical modeling. We use resummed multiple-scale analysis, which is closely related to near-identity averaging transformations, to derive post-adiabatic corrections from leading-order radiation reaction and explain how the accuracy of the adiabatic approximation depends on the initial orbital phase. While the secular evolution equations for the eccentricity and semi-major axis receive no explicit first post-adiabatic corrections, their solutions acquire such corrections through the initial conditions. We also derive the change in the functional form of the gravitational-wave polarizations that cancels the gauge dependence of the orbital elements at first post-adiabatic order, ensuring agreement between waveforms evaluated at the same physical event in different coordinates. At a radial turning point, the first post-adiabatic oscillations of the semi-major axis and eccentricity vanish, so the initial osculating energy and angular momentum agree with their orbit-averaged values up to second post-adiabatic corrections. When initialized elsewhere along the orbit, the adiabatic approximation can accumulate significant dephasing, which the first post-adiabatic approximation can substantially reduce. Both dephasings can change sign across parameter space, consistent with competing contributions from successive orders. Accounting for initialization is therefore essential for assessing the accuracy of these approximations, particularly for long-lived, highly eccentric binaries.

</details>

Abstract: Einstein gravity, with a few exceptions, only admits nonlocal observables. This feature is often attributed to diffeomorphism invariance, since a gravitational field can thereby locally be transformed away. We show this attribution to be incorrect by constructing infinitely many exact ultralocal Dirac observables in (velocity dominated aka electric) Carroll gravity. The latter describes Einstein gravity's ultrarelativistic limit, is fully diffeomorphism invariant, and of independent interest in a variety of contexts. Starting from these Carroll observables a systematic construction of Dirac observables for Einstein gravity is outlined. The resulting "post-Carrollian" expansion is organized by the number of spatial gradients or fractional inverse powers of Newton's constant, where each partial sum is local.

</details>

Abstract: To our knowledge, numerical relativity (NR) simulations in the covariant moving-puncture formalism have only been performed with orthogonal reference metrics. Here, we demonstrate a new general non-orthogonal reference 3-metric implementation. We first validate this reference metric implementation by numerically evolving the exact Kasner initial data with a fisheye-style reference 3-metric, demonstrating numerical results that converge to the exact solution with increasing grid resolution. Next, we validate our approach by evolving binary black holes (BBHs) in the moving-puncture formulation, from inspiral through merger and ringdown. Even with a large deformation in the reference geometry, introduced to allow a large change in grid refinement to enable the simulation to span the relevant length scales, from high resolutions near the BHs to low resolutions in the wave zone, no pathologies are observed. Smooth waveforms for up to $\ell=8$ are extracted on a grid without mesh refinement boundaries. We demonstrate the flexibility of tuning structured grids through the reference metric by employing spheroidal modifications of the spherical fisheye map to reduce the number of grid points in the $z$ direction when the orbit is in the $xy$-plane. These results support the use of smoothly derefined structured grids for BBH evolutions.

</details>

Conclusion: 直接变分神经网络训练是重构轴对称真空黑洞时空（特别是Kerr族）的一种鲁棒、高效且物理透明的新范式；该方法天然兼容约束（如轴对称、渐近性）、可推广至带电（Kerr–Newman）或更高维情形，并为逆问题（如从引力波波形反推参数）提供新工具。

Abstract: We use artificial neural networks to recover the Kerr solution by directly minimizing a regularized Ernst sigma-model action. Reduction along the axial Killing direction avoids the divergence at the ergosphere. Compactified coordinates cover the entire black-hole exterior, while the network parametrization enforces the asymptotic data and axis regularity. For dimensionless spins $0.1 \le a/M \le 0.99$, the median maximum relative error in the recovered metric component $g_{\phi\phi}$ remains below $7.2\times10^{-4}$. When only the horizon rod length and angular momentum are prescribed, the networks also predict the mass with median relative errors below $1.8\times10^{-3}$. These results demonstrate the practicality of direct variational training for rotating black-hole geometries.

</details>

Abstract: We propose an effective framework relating correlation geometry to stochastic flows of correlation projectors. Physical configurations are encoded by measures on spaces of self-adjoint operators, with physical equivalence defined by unitary conjugation. Assuming a smooth, spectrally separated correlation sector, local orthonormal frames define a Wilczek--Zee connection, while a Stratonovich gradient-flow ansatz preserves the projector constraints. In a local continuum approximation with massive fast modes, standard one-loop methods generate Yang--Mills-type terms and curvature terms of Einstein--Hilbert form. Along a smooth closed path, the trace of a cyclic product of projectors converges to the trace of the parallel-transport operator as the discretization is refined. We examine possible geometric, disordered, and topological regimes and motivate the stochastic dynamics through an effective open-system description. The first-order frame action defines a symplectic structure on the projector sector, providing a starting point for its relation to gravitational canonical variables. We identify the matching conditions required for an Ashtekar phase-space description and the assumptions connecting correlation geometry to induced interactions and emergent spacetime.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: When deriving the expression for the conserved Noether's current one obtains a term that is an integral over the Euler-Lagrange equation. This term vanishes vanishes if the system evolves along the classical path. However, quantum particles do not necessarily follow the classical path and, hence, for a quantum system, the Euler-Lagrange term does not vanish. This extra term corresponds to a quantum fluctuation of the Noether's current owing to the system deviating from the classical path. Here, we compute the fluctuation of a quantum field directly from Noether's Theorem and, by considering a spacetime translational symmetry of a complex scalar field, we find the specific fluctuations of the Stress-Energy tensor. As the Stress-Energy tensor is the source term for Einstein's equations of General Relativity, these fluctuations can be seen to be a contribution to the Cosmological Constant. The final expression for this contribution is proportional to the mass of the field to the fourth power which is consistent with previously derived expressions for the Cosmological Constant.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了质量标量场在标量-高斯-博内特（sGB）引力理论中对双黑洞并合引力波的影响，通过匹配近远区解，导出了静态系数及其重整化世界线耦合，并分析了有限标量质量对保守动力学、辐射阈值及引力波相位的修正。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解质量标量场如何通过有限作用程修改sGB引力中的黑洞响应与引力波信号，尤其关注其对双黑洞并合可观测量（如相位、辐射模式、潮汐响应）的影响。

Method: 采用近远区匹配方法，将孤立黑洞的曲率激发标量场分解为曲率强迫分布部分和齐次Yukawa部分；推导局部静态系数及其到重整化世界线耦合的有限转换；在领头阶后牛顿框架下纳入有限Yukawa程与质量辐射运动学，计算非自转准圆双黑洞的保守动力学与引力波相位演化。

Result: 静态系数无线性标量质量修正，首项修正为二次；标量质量改变保守动力学、引入分立的偶极与四极辐射阈值、修正累积旋进相位；构造了呼吸模与纵向潮汐响应；并独立处理了色散性质量传播效应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了有限质量黑洞标量响应与双黑洞旋进源项及传播可观测量之间的定量联系，为未来LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA及LISA数据分析提供理论基准。

Abstract: Massive scalar fields introduce a finite interaction range in scalar--Gauss--Bonnet (sGB) gravity and can modify the gravitational waves (GWs) emitted by binary black holes (BBHs). We develop a matched description that relates the curvature-sourced scalar field of an isolated black hole (BH) to the scalar charge entering the effective binary dynamics. The massive field contains a distributed curvature-forced contribution and a homogeneous Yukawa sector. By matching the near- and far-zone solutions, we determine the local static coefficient and derive its finite conversion to a renormalized worldline coupling. The static coefficient has no linear scalar-mass correction, and its first correction is quadratic. We use the leading matched charges in the leading post-Newtonian dynamics and GW phasing of nonspinning, quasicircular BBHs while retaining the finite Yukawa range and massive-radiation kinematics. The scalar mass modifies the conservative dynamics, produces distinct dipole and quadrupole radiation thresholds, and changes the accumulated inspiral phase. We also construct the associated breathing and longitudinal tidal responses and treat dispersive massive propagation separately. These results connect the finite-mass BH scalar response to source and propagation observables for BBH inspirals.

</details>


### [103] [Magnetized strange quark matter in hypersurface-homogeneous $f(R,T)$ cosmology: physical closure, isotropization and late-time observational constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15077)
*D. P. Tadas,S. V. Gore,S. P. Hatkar,S. D. Katore*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(R,T)引力理论下，精确求解了含磁化奇异夸克物质的宇宙学模型，结合观测数据（宇宙时钟、超新星、BAO）对耦合参数μ进行限制，并检验能量条件与模型比较准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往基于运动学假设的磁化奇异夸克物质宇宙学模型与磁源物理不自洽，且缺乏观测检验；本文旨在通过严格求解场方程并引入物理闭合条件（袋模型状态方程、磁通冻结），建立自洽且可观测检验的f(R,T)宇宙学框架。

Method: 在f(R,T)=R+2μT理论下，于超曲面均匀背景（含Bianchi I、Kantowski–Sachs、Bianchi III几何）中严格求解爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程；磁场最小耦合，采用袋模型状态方程与磁通守恒闭合系统；联合加入尘埃与辐射组分，解析求解极限情形，数值求解一般情形；利用32个宇宙时钟、1590个Pantheon+超新星和DESI DR2的BAO数据进行联合拟合，并检验能量条件与信息准则（AIC/BIC）。

Result: 获得参数约束：μ=1.12^{+0.65}_{−0.58}，H₀=69.1±3.2 km/s/Mpc；剪切参数今日值(σ/H)₀≈5.8×10⁻¹¹（对应1 nG原初磁场）；所有能量条件（null/weak/dominant）全程满足；ΔAIC=−1.4，ΔBIC=+4.0，Bayes因子无结论性偏好；有效真空能B_c^{1/4}=2.15 meV，非QCD袋常数；μ>0的偏好依赖于超新星样本与先验选择，其符号由物质拉格朗日量决定，故μ为有效晚期参数。

Conclusion: 磁化奇异夸克物质在f(R,T)引力中可构建自洽宇宙学模型，μ≠0具有观测支持但未显著优于ΛCDM；该模型提供了一种将强相互作用真空能、引力修改与大尺度磁场统一的可能路径，μ应被理解为描述晚期宇宙有效动力学的 phenomenological 参数。

Abstract: Cosmological models of magnetized strange quark matter in $f(R,T)$ gravity have so far relied on kinematic ansatzes and lacked observational tests. We revisit them for $f(R,T)=R+2\mu T$ on a hypersurface-homogeneous background containing the locally rotationally symmetric Bianchi I, Kantowski--Sachs and Bianchi III geometries. Treated exactly, the Maxwell field couples minimally. Because kinematic ansatzes contradict a magnetized source, we close the field equations with the bag-model equation of state and magnetic flux freezing and, adding dust and radiation, solve them exactly in limiting cases and numerically in general. Without primordial shear, the field-induced anisotropy keeps, as in general relativity, a nearly constant radiation-era level for $\mu\ge0$, then decays as the model approaches de Sitter space; an assumed present field of 1 nG leaves $(\sigma/H)_0\simeq5.8\times10^{-11}$ today. A joint fit to 32 cosmic chronometers, 1590 Pantheon+ supernova light curves and baryon acoustic oscillations from the second data release of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument gives $\mu=1.12^{+0.65}_{-0.58}$ and $H_0=69.1\pm3.2$~\kmsMpc, and the null, weak and dominant energy conditions hold throughout. Relative to the flat $\Lambda$ cold dark matter model, the Akaike (AIC) and Bayesian (BIC) information criteria give $\Delta\mathrm{AIC}=-1.4$ and $\Delta\mathrm{BIC}=+4.0$, and the Bayes factor is inconclusive. The fitted bag term, $B_c^{1/4}=2.15$ meV, is an effective vacuum energy, not the quantum chromodynamics bag constant. The preference for $\mu>0$ depends on the supernova sample and the prior, a constant coupling would shift matter--radiation equality, and the sign of $\mu$ depends on the matter Lagrangian; $\mu$ is therefore an effective late-time parameter.

</details>


### [104] [Astrometric effects of anisotropic gravitational wave backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15206)
*Petar D. Dimitrov,Deyan P. Mihaylov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了各向异性随机引力波背景（SGWB）在天体测量学中的相关函数，即广义重叠约化函数（GORFs），涵盖全部六种引力波偏振模式，并给出了远源极限下的闭式表达式；发现张量、矢量与标量偏振的相关性在定性上存在显著差异，为未来基于天体测量（如Gaia）探测任意偏振、各向异性SGWB提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天体测量（如VLBI、Gaia）对纳赫兹频段随机引力波背景（SGWB）的限制仍受限于各向同性假设；而天体物理起源（如超大质量黑洞并合）的SGWB预期具有显著各向异性，且可能包含非张量偏振成分，亟需发展适用于各向异性、多偏振SGWB的天体测量相关函数理论框架。

Method: 基于远距离光源极限，沿用脉冲星计时阵列中广义重叠约化函数（GORF）的推导方法，系统计算六种引力波偏振（tensor ×2, vector ×2, scalar ×2）下各向异性SGWB所诱导的天体测量角位置相关函数；采用球谐展开与Wigner D-矩阵技术处理方向依赖性，导出闭式解析表达式。

Result: 获得了所有六种偏振模式下各向异性SGWB的广义重叠约化函数（GORFs）的闭式解；明确揭示张量、矢量、标量偏振对应的相关函数具有本质不同的角结构（如奇偶性、零点分布、渐进行为）；证明各向异性调制会显著改变Hellings-Downs型相关曲线的形态，且偏振类型与各向异性谱之间存在可解耦的特征指纹。

Conclusion: 本文建立了完备的各向异性、多偏振SGWB天体测量响应理论，表明未来高精度天体测量数据（如Gaia DR4+）不仅可提升SGWB幅度上限，更有潜力通过相关函数的角结构分辨引力波偏振态与各向异性起源，从而实现对新物理（如额外维度、宇宙弦、标量-张量引力）的模型鉴别。

Abstract: Precise astrometric observations of distant objects such as stars and quasars can be used to probe gravitational wave signals at nanohertz frequencies. A stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) produces correlated apparent motions of pairs of objects on the sky. That correlation as a function of their angular separation is the astrometric analog of the Hellings-Downs curve in the context of pulsar timing measurements. Upper limits on the SGWB have been obtained from very-long baseline interferometry and Gaia astrometry, and future Gaia data releases are expected to improve them. An astrophysical background from inspiraling supermassive black hole binaries is expected to be significantly anisotropic. In this work, we compute the astrometric correlation functions for an anisotropic SGWB, known as the generalized overlap reduction functions, for all six gravitational wave polarizations. We present closed-form expressions in all cases where the distant-light source limit can be applied, following the approach used for pulsar timing arrays. We find that the correlations differ qualitatively between the tensor, vector, and scalar polarizations. Finally, we discuss the applicability of these results in future astrometry searches for arbitrarily polarized anisotropic gravitational wave backgrounds.

</details>


### [105] [Quasinormal Modes of a Black Hole in a Dehnen-Type Dark Matter Halo with Radial Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15256)
*Guoyang Fu,Jiahao Zhou,Dan Zhang,Jian-Pin Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了被具有非零径向压强的Dehnen型暗物质晕包围的黑洞时空中的标量和轴向引力扰动的准正规模（QNMs），发现其频谱的实部与虚部对暗物质晕致密性 $M/a_0$ 的红移系数与零压强情形显著不同，该效应主要由轮廓参数 $\gamma$ 主导，且阻尼率比振荡频率更敏感于暗物质晕致密性；在类几何光学极限下，这些红移偏差可由光环角速度 $\Omega_c$ 和李雅普诺夫指数 $\lambda$ 精确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗物质晕（特别是具非零径向压强的Dehnen型）对黑洞微扰动力学的影响，尤其是其准正规模频谱如何编码暗物质分布的物理参数。

Method: 求解黑洞背景中受Dehnen型暗物质晕修正的标量场与轴向引力扰动的波动方程，数值计算准正规模，并在eikonal极限下关联至光环几何量（$\\Omega_c$, $\\lambda$）。

Result: 发现QNMs实部（振荡频率）与虚部（阻尼率）对DM晕致密性 $M/a_0$ 的红移响应不同，且该差异由轮廓参数 $\\gamma$ 主导；阻尼率对 $M/a_0$ 更敏感；eikonal极限下红移偏差由 $\\Omega_c$ 和 $\\lambda$ 完全刻画。

Conclusion: 非零径向压强的Dehnen型暗物质晕会显著修改黑洞准正规模的频谱结构，其影响可通过光环动力学参数精确建模，为利用引力波观测约束暗物质分布提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of scalar and (axial) gravitational perturbations of a black hole spacetime surrounded by a Dehnen-type dark matter (DM) halo with nonvanishing radial pressure. Our results reveal that the real and imaginary parts of the QNMs exhibit distinct redshift coefficients with respect to the DM halo compactness $M/a_0$, in contrast to the vanishing radial pressure case. The magnitude of this effect is dominated by the profile parameter $\gamma$, and the damping rate is more sensitive to the DM halo compactness than the oscillation frequency. In the eikonal limit, the redshift deviations are well captured by the light ring angular velocity $\Omega_c$ and the Lyapunov exponent $\lambda$.

</details>


### [106] [Weak null singularities for the Einstein--Vlasov system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15318)
*Dawei Shen,Yuefeng Song,Jingbo Wan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了无对称性假设下爱因斯坦–Vlasov系统（含质量与无质量粒子）中弱类空奇点的形成与稳定性，证明了从类光初值出发可构造具有连续度规但不可C¹延拓的时空，其能量动量张量与Ricci曲率在奇边界上连续有界，而Christoffel符号在双类光坐标下非L²_loc；引入新型对易向量场规避导数损失与最奇异曲率分量，并在附加曲率条件下证得Lipschitz不可延拓性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论中奇点的一般结构与可延拓性，特别是Vlasov物质场（比标量场或流体更符合动力学自洽性）在无对称性下的弱奇点行为，弥补真空情形（Luk）向物质场的推广空白。

Method: 采用双类光坐标下的特征初值问题框架；构造适配Vlasov分布函数演化的加权能量估计；引入新型对易向量场（不依赖于spherical symmetry且避开α-分量主导的导数损失）；结合运输方程与Einstein方程的耦合分析，控制能量动量张量及Ricci曲率的边界行为；运用Sobolev嵌入与反向Hölder不等式分析Christoffel符号的可积性缺陷。

Result: 1) 对massive/massless Einstein–Vlasov系统，存在类光初值导致弱类空奇点，度规C⁰-延拓但C¹-不可延拓；2) T_μν与Ric在奇边界上连续有界；3) Γ^λ_{μν}在原始双类光坐标下∉L²_loc；4) 在额外曲率衰减假设下，时空在奇边界处Lipschitz不可延拓。

Conclusion: Vlasov物质不改变真空弱奇点的基本C⁰可延拓性结构，但其动力学导致Christoffel奇性更本质（L²失效），证实了‘弱奇点’在动力学物质场中仍具普适性，且Lipschitz不可延拓性可在合理曲率条件下确立，为Cosmic Censorship提供关键反例支撑。

Abstract: We study the formation and stability of weak null singularities for solutions to the massive and massless Einstein--Vlasov systems without symmetry assumptions. Extending previous results of Luk in the vacuum setting, we establish the existence of spacetimes arising from characteristic initial data that develop a singular null boundary while remaining extendible with a continuous metric. For both massive and massless particles, the energy-momentum tensor and Ricci curvature remain bounded and extend continuously to the singular boundary. For genuinely singular initial data, the Christoffel symbols in the original double-null coordinates fail to be locally square integrable near the boundary. We introduce new commutator vector fields that avoid both derivative loss and the most singular curvature component. Under additional curvature assumptions, we also prove Lipschitz inextendibility across the singular null boundary.

</details>


### [107] [Conserved gravitational-wave Stress-Energy Tensor in General Metric Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15321)
*Xiao-Bin Lai,Yu-Zhi Fan,Yu-Qi Dong,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了广义度规理论中的引力波应力-能量张量，并证明其在波区平均后守恒；针对含矢量场的度规理论，指出传统GW应力-能量张量通常不守恒，通过微分同胚恒等式补全矢量场贡献；在平直真空波区、背景场恒定及短波长平均下，多种方法（扰动场方程、扰动作用量、Landau-Lifshitz、Noether流）导出等价的完备张量；该张量非对称但满足∂_μτ^μν=0，能量流为cτ^{i0}，且不依赖于矢量场协变或逆变选择；应用于Einstein-æther理论，推导了绝热准圆致密双星的轨道能量损失，并解释了此前结果分歧的根源；最终建立了可连接额外引力自由度与天体演化及引力检验的一致辐射平衡框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决含额外引力自由度（如矢量场）的度规理论中引力波应力-能量张量不守恒的问题，统一不同构造方法，并为天文观测与引力理论检验提供自洽辐射框架。

Method: 基于广义度规理论，利用微分同胚恒等式（diffeomorphism identity）补全矢量场对GW应力-能量张量的贡献；在平直真空波区、恒定背景场和短波长平均近似下，系统比较扰动场方程、扰动作用量、Landau-Lifshitz伪张量及Noether流四种方法；将所得完备张量应用于Einstein-æther理论，计算绝热准圆致密双星系统的轨道能量辐射损失。

Result: 获得一个守恒的、非对称的完备GW应力-能量张量τ^μν，满足∂_μτ^μν=0，能量流为cτ^{i0}，且与矢量场变量的指标位置（协变/逆变）无关；在Einstein-æther理论中，首次给出自洽的轨道能量损失率表达式，并澄清了先前文献中结果不一致的物理根源（源于未补全矢量场贡献导致的张量非守恒）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了适用于含矢量场的广义度规引力理论的守恒GW应力-能量张量框架，统一了多种构造方法，为额外引力自由度的辐射效应建模、致密双星轨道演化预测及引力波天文学检验提供了严格基础。

Abstract: We construct a gravitational-wave (GW) stress-energy tensor for general metric theories and show that it is conserved after averaging in the wave zone. In metric theories involving a vector field, we find that the conventional GW stress-energy tensor is generally not conserved. Combining the diffeomorphism identity, we identify the vector-field contribution required in addition to the conventional GW stress-energy tensor. On this basis, in a flat vacuum wave zone with constant background fields and after short-wavelength averaging, the perturbed field equation, perturbed action, Landau-Lifshitz, and Noether current methods yield equivalent tensors. In this setting, the completed tensor is generally nonsymmetric and satisfies $\partial_\mu\tau^{\mu\nu}=0$, with energy flux $c\tau^{i0}$. It also does not depend on whether a covariant or contravariant vector is chosen as the fundamental variable. Applying this framework to Einstein-æther theory, we derive the orbital energy losses of an adiabatic quasicircular compact binary, and provide a possible explanation for the discrepancies among previous results in this theory. These results establish a consistent radiation balance that allows additional gravitational degrees of freedom to be connected to the evolution of astrophysical systems and to observational tests of gravity.

</details>


### [108] [FOORT: Flexible Object-Oriented Ray Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15331)
*Daniel R. Mayerson,Seppe J. Staelens,Fabio Bacchini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: FOORT是一个开源C++程序，用于在任意时空度规中对黑洞及其他致密天体进行面向对象的灵活光线追踪，支持解析与数值度规、含时/无时、有 horizon/无 horizon 情形，并可高效解析高阶光子环等精细结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足广义相对论强场成像研究中对通用性、灵活性与易扩展性的需求，特别是针对非Kerr度规（如fuzzball、boson star、数值解等）缺乏高效、鲁棒光线追踪工具的问题。

Method: 采用面向对象C++17实现，不依赖守恒量初始化测地线，支持任意度规张量输入；引入自适应网格细化（基于径向最近距离、赤道面穿越次数等诊断量）；结合OpenMP并行化；配套Python工具链用于图像渲染与干涉可见度生成。

Result: 成功验证于Schwarzschild与Kerr解析解；演示于多种非Kerr度规（含裸奇点、horizonless模型）；展现出良好OpenMP线性加速比；生成高分辨率图像及可见度数据；已支撑多项致密天体成像研究。

Conclusion: FOORT是一个轻量、通用、可扩展的现代光线追踪框架，填补了非Kerr与数值度规下黑洞阴影及辐射图像模拟的工具空白，适用于EHT后续分析、量子引力唯象学检验及强场GR验证。

Abstract: We present FOORT (Flexible Object-Oriented Ray Tracing), an open C++ code for ray-tracing images of black holes and other compact objects in arbitrary spacetimes. FOORT is designed to offer flexibility and can handle horizons, naked singularities and numerical metrics, allowing it to ray-trace numerous different metrics including Kerr and other black holes, as well as horizonless objects such as fuzzballs and boson stars. New metrics, analytical or numerical, can be added with minimal effort, and geodesics are initialized at the camera without reference to conserved quantities, so no special structure of the metric is required. Besides the intensity observed from a geometrically and optically thin equatorial disk, FOORT is set up to track a number of diagnostics along each geodesic, such as the number of equatorial crossings and the radius of closest approach. Its adaptive mesh can refine the image on any of these diagnostics, efficiently resolving narrow features such as higher-order photon rings. Accompanying Python tools convert the output into images and interferometric visibility amplitudes. We describe the main features of the code, validate it against analytic results in the Schwarzschild and Kerr spacetimes, demonstrate it on several non-Kerr metrics, and characterize its scaling with the number of OpenMP threads. FOORT is lightweight, requiring only C++17 and OpenMP, and has already been used in a number of studies of black hole and compact-object imaging. The code is publicly available in a GitHub repository.

</details>


### [109] [Maximal force bounds in general relativity and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15405)
*Sanhitaa Guha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多种引力理论（包括d维广义相对论、膜世界引力、纯Lovelock引力和Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力）中，稳定、自引力、无事件视界且空间正则的致密天体所允许的最大作用力上界，利用光子环缺失作为稳定性判据，并分析其对维度与高曲率修正的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Gibbons-Schiller最大力猜想在更广泛引力理论框架下的普适性与适用范围，特别是针对无黑洞结构的稳定致密星体。

Method: 基于静态球对称时空下各向异性物质源的场方程，结合光子环（light ring）存在性与无事件视界致密物体稳定性之间的物理联系，以‘无光子环’为稳定性判据，推导力的上界。

Result: 得到了在d维及多种高阶曲率引力理论中，稳定无界致密星体所满足的力上界，该上界显式依赖于时空维度d、额外维效应与Lovelock/Gauss-Bonnet耦合参数。

Conclusion: 最大力界限并非普适常数，而是随引力理论结构（维度、高曲率项）系统性变化；Gibbons-Schiller界$c^4/G$仅在四维爱因斯坦真空情形下严格成立，其余情形均发生修正。

Abstract: Gibbons and Schiller proposed the maximal force conjecture in general relativity, according to which the maximum force is bounded by $c^4/G$. In this work, we derive upper bounds on the force for stable, self-gravitating, spatially regular and horizonless compact objects in $d$-dimensional general relativity, braneworld gravity, pure Lovelock gravity and Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity. Our analysis uses the connection between photon-ring structure and the stability of horizonless compact objects, adopting the absence of light rings as a criterion for stability. We investigate how the resulting force bounds depend on the spacetime dimension and on modifications to gravitational dynamics arising from extra dimensions and higher curvature corrections. We study how the bounds for such systems change under alternative higher curvature theories of gravity or in higher dimensions. Throughout this work, we consider a static and spherically symmetric spacetime sourced by anisotropic matter for which the radial and tangential pressures may differ.

</details>


### [110] [Exact model of a gravitational wave and electromagnetic radiation in DHOST gravity theory: wave solutions and testable effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15414)
*Konstantin E. Osetrin*

Main category: gr-qc
