<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分广义相对论与具有相同背景度规但不同动力学结构的修改引力理论（如DHOST），需超越度规比较，寻找场耦合层面的可观测差异。

Method: 基于各向同性（类光）波坐标系下的精确引力波度规，在广义相对论与DHOST Ia理论中联合求解爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦与DHOST非线性场方程；利用该精确电动力学解分析约束方程对电磁波参数的代数修正。

Result: 1) 引力波在全非微扰水平上构成电磁波共向传播的波导，并等效产生纯辐射源；2) DHOST Ia的约束方程结构导致电磁波频率与波矢发生标量依赖的修正；3) 导出可观测的标量介导频率漂移效应。

Conclusion: 该频率漂移是DHOST Ia与广义相对论的本质区别，即使二者度规完全一致，亦可通过电磁波传播的精细频谱特征进行观测甄别，为强场引力检验提供了新探针。

Abstract: Within an analytical non-perturbative framework based on a single exact model, the coupled dynamics of gravitational waves and high-frequency electromagnetic fields is investigated in General Relativity and DHOST gravity. Using an exact gravitational-wave spacetime in an isotropic (null) wave coordinate system, Maxwell's equations are integrated analytically. This analysis provides an exact non-perturbative confirmation from the full field equations that a gravitational wave acts as a waveguide, forming a propagation channel for electromagnetic radiation co-directional with the gravitational wave and generating an effective pure-radiation source. Applying this exact electrodynamic solution to the nonlinear field equations of DHOST class~Ia gravity reveals that the algebraic structure of the DHOST constraint equations enforces a modification of the electromagnetic-wave parameters. Based on this mechanism, an observationally testable effect of scalar frequency drift is derived, providing a direct way to discriminate between General Relativity and modified gravity theories even when their metric tensors share identical functional forms.

</details>


### [111] [Exact transformation-optics representation of the Ellis wormhole and limits of finite-device scattering emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15662)
*Abdullah Guvendi,Claudio Furtado,Omar Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了超静态Ellis虫洞的电磁响应，并通过Plebański对应构造了其等效各向同性、阻抗匹配的连续介质模型；证明了理想双端虫洞必然存在非幺模反射（|R_id| < 1），而任何无损单端有限器件反射必为幺模，从而严格量化了二者在复反射幅上的不可规避失配；同时给出了多极散射的低频标度律、截面渐近行为，并为有限壳层实现提供了基于被动Lorentz模型的构成误差精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究广义相对论中理想虫洞几何能否被经典电磁超材料在散射响应上等效模拟，尤其关注全局各向同性实现的可能性与根本限制。

Method: 采用全局各向同性坐标变换将Ellis虫洞的固有径向线映射至穿孔欧氏空间，结合Plebański对应导出精确的标量折射率n(ρ)及对应的各向同性介电/磁导率张量ε_r = μ_r = n(ρ)𝟙；严格求解矢量Maxwell方程的径向算子，利用Jost解与Wronski行列式表征散射振幅；应用谱理论与复分析（逆三角不等式）建立反射幅失配下界；数值收敛计算多极散射系数并解析验证低频幂律；构造光滑有限壳层目标，推导校准后被动Lorentz模型的构成误差闭式表达。

Result: 1) 得到全局各向同性折射率n(ρ)=1+a²/(4ρ²)及其对应阻抗匹配介质；2) 证明理想双端虫洞在所有实频率下必有|R_id|<1，而任意无损单端有限器件必满足|R|=1，故二者复反射幅差模存在严格正下界；3) 多极透射幅满足|T_L|² ∝ (ωa/c)^{4L+2}（L=1–4已独立验证）；4) 总透射截面→πa²，总散射截面在前向呈对数发散；5) 给出有限壳层下被动Lorentz模型的精确构成误差公式。

Conclusion: 超静态Ellis虫洞的完整电磁响应（尤其是非幺模反射与前向散射发散）无法被任何无损单端有限超材料器件完全复现；该不可实现性由基本谱约束与复分析不等式所保证；低频多极标度律与构成误差公式为实验逼近提供了定量基准。

Abstract: We derive a global isotropic representation of the ultrastatic Ellis wormhole and establish which features of its electromagnetic response can, and cannot, be reproduced by a finite device. An orientation-preserving coordinate map sends the signed proper-radial line to a punctured Euclidean chart with refractive index $n(\rho)=1+a^2/(4\rho^2)$. Through the Plebański correspondence, this gives the exact impedance-matched continuum medium $\varepsilon_r=\mu_r=n(\rho)\,\mathbb{1}$ and its full vector-Maxwell operator. For the ideal two-ended geometry, we derive the electromagnetic radial operator, establish its spectral properties, and express its scattering amplitudes through Jost solutions and Wronskians. Nonzero transmission into the second asymptotic end implies $|R_{\rm id}|<1$ at every real frequency, whereas a regular, lossless finite one-port has unit-modulus reflection. The reverse triangle inequality therefore yields a strictly positive lower bound on the complex reflection-amplitude mismatch. This theorem applies to the stated lossless single-port class and is not extended to lossy or multiport realizations. We compute converged multipole scattering data and establish the low-frequency threshold scaling $|T_L|^2\propto(\omega a/c)^{4L+2}$, with coefficients independently validated for $L=1,2,3,4$. The transmission cross section tends to $\pi a^2$, while the ideal profile's total scattering cross section has a logarithmic forward divergence. We also construct a smooth finite-shell target and derive an exact finite-band constitutive-error formula for a calibrated passive Lorentz model. The constitutive benchmark is kept distinct from scattering equivalence throughout.

</details>


### [112] [Highly spinning binary black holes: promise and pitfalls for parameterized tests of general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15721)
*Soumen Roy,Maria Haney,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高自旋双黑洞并合系统在强场、动力学区域对广义相对论的参数化检验能力，指出高自旋且质量不对称系统对高阶后牛顿（PN）修正更敏感；但利用NR模拟信号进行波形建模分析时，不同IMR模型（如IMRPhenomXPHM-SpinTaylor、IMRPhenomXPNR、SEOBNRv5PHM）会引入系统性偏差，进而导致TIGER框架下出现虚假的GR偏离信号，尤其在信噪比高的情况下显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升对广义相对论在强场、高度动力学区域的检验精度，特别是约束高阶后牛顿项中尚未被充分限制的偏离项；同时厘清波形系统误差与真实物理偏离之间的混淆风险。

Method: 基于SXS数值相对论（NR）模拟数据，采用三种先进IMR波形模型（IMRPhenomXPHM-SpinTaylor、IMRPhenomXPNR、SEOBNRv5PHM）进行贝叶斯参数估计，并进一步使用基于IMRPhenomXPHM的参数化GR检验框架TIGER开展Inspiral-parameterized test，量化模型偏差对GR偏离推断的影响。

Result: 发现不同IMR模型在恢复高自旋、质量不对称NR信号时存在显著的模型依赖性偏差（体现在质量、自旋大小及倾角上）；这些偏差在TIGER分析中被误读为对广义相对论的显著偏离，尤其在高信噪比下达到统计显著水平。

Conclusion: 高自旋双黑洞是探测高阶后牛顿物理的理想探针，但当前波形模型的系统性不完善会主导参数化检验结果，造成假阳性信号；因此，提升波形建模精度（尤其在高自旋、大质量比区域）是可靠检验引力理论的前提。

Abstract: Highly spinning binary black holes exhibit complex inspiral dynamics and offer a powerful opportunity to probe gravity in a stronger-field and more dynamical regime than non-spinning binaries. Gravitational-wave signals from such systems enable stringent parameterized tests of deviations from the source dynamics predicted by general relativity. We show that highly spinning, mass-asymmetric binaries can provide enhanced sensitivity to deviations entering at higher post-Newtonian orders, which remain comparatively poorly constrained. Using NR simulations from the SXS catalog, we first recover the injected signals within general relativity using $\texttt{IMRPhenomXPHM-SpinTaylor}$, $\texttt{IMRPhenomXPNR}$, and $\texttt{SEOBNRv5PHM}$, finding model-dependent biases in the inferred masses, spins, and spin orientations. We then analyze the same signals with the $\texttt{IMRPhenomXPHM}$-based inspiral parameterized test of general relativity framework, TIGER, and find that these waveform-systematic biases can lead to apparent deviations from general relativity, which become significant for loud signals. Our results highlight that highly spinning binaries offer powerful tests of higher-order post-Newtonian deviations, but waveform inaccuracies can lead to false signs of new physics.

</details>


### [113] [Eccentricity detectability in Extreme Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15736)
*Maria Gonçalves Salgado,Lorenzo Speri,Elena M. Rossi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）中轨道偏心率对LISA探测灵敏度的影响，量化了偏心率提升波形可分辨性与导致信号功率分散至多谐波从而削弱单谐波搜索效能之间的权衡；发现当最终偏心率ef ≳ 0.05时偏心率可被显著区分于零，而单谐波检测能力在ef ≳ 0.1后下降；指出在无噪声情况下包含足够谐波以回收≥50%总信噪比（SNR=50）可避免灵敏度退化，但会增加阈值校准的统计自由度；最后将该框架应用于两类简化天体物理EMRI种群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRIs是LISA的核心目标源，其轨道偏心率蕴含关键天体物理起源信息，亟需可靠探测；但偏心率升高同时增强波形可分辨性与削弱单谐波搜索效率，该竞争机制尚缺定量刻画。

Method: 采用半相干搜索框架，在固定总信噪比SNR=50条件下，系统分析偏心率ef对波形可分辨性（通过似然比检验）和单谐波匹配滤波灵敏度的影响；评估多谐波包含策略（以回收≥50% SNR为标准）对检测统计量与阈值设定的影响；并将该分析拓展至两类典型天体物理EMRI偏心率分布模型。

Result: 1) 偏心率在ef ≳ 0.05时可被显著区分于零；2) 单谐波搜索灵敏度在ef ≳ 0.1后明显下降；3) 无噪声下包含足够谐波以回收≥50%总SNR可完全补偿灵敏度损失，但引入额外参数自由度，抬高检测阈值；4) 在两类天体物理EMRI种群中，该权衡显著影响最优搜索策略设计。

Conclusion: EMRI偏心率探测需统筹考虑偏心率驱动的可分辨性增益、多谐波导致的单谐波功率衰减、以及多参数建模引入的统计代价；未来LISA EMRI搜索算法必须在谐波截断阶数、模板库维度与检测阈值之间进行联合优化。

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) consist of a stellar-mass compact object orbiting a massive black hole and are among the primary targets for the future space-based gravitational-wave detector LISA. Their orbital eccentricity carries valuable information about their astrophysical origins, making its detection particularly important. However, increasing eccentricity has competing effects on detectability: it makes eccentric waveforms easier to distinguish from circular ones, but also redistributes signal power across multiple harmonics, reducing the power captured by single-harmonic searches. In this work, we quantify this trade-off within a semi-coherent framework for a source with fixed total SNR of $50$. Eccentricity becomes distinguishable from zero for final eccentricities $e_f \gtrsim 0.05$, while single-harmonic sensitivity declines beyond $e_f \simeq 0.1$. In noise-free analyses, including enough harmonics to recover at least $50\%$ of the total SNR avoids this degradation, but adds statistical freedom when calibrating detection thresholds. We apply the framework to two astrophysical EMRI populations, showing how these competing effects interact with their eccentricity distributions under simplified assumptions. The balance among eccentricity-driven distinguishability, harmonic power loss, and the statistical cost of additional harmonics is an important consideration for future EMRI search developments.

</details>


### [114] [Population inference of eccentric binary black holes using asymptotic constants of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15738)
*Chiara Anselmo,Giulia Fumagalli,Davide Gerosa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于牛顿轨道角动量（而非偏心率）作为渐近不变量的引力波致密双星偏心性群体推断新方法，解决了传统以参考频率定义参数所导致的演化阶段不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准引力波群体分析中以固定参考频率定义源参数，导致处于不同并合前演化阶段的系统混杂，妨碍天体物理诠释；而偏心率在零频极限下趋于1，不适合作为公共参考点。

Method: 利用牛顿轨道角动量是相对论二体问题在零频（过去时间无穷远）极限下的有限渐近守恒量这一已知性质，将偏心性群体推断重新参数化为以该角动量为核心变量，并在模拟引力波数据上实施贝叶斯群体推断。

Result: 首次实现了仅依赖渐近运动常数（而非演化参数）的偏心双星群体推断；所得后验分布可一致地传播至任意观测频率（如10 Hz），便于与天体物理模型直接比较。

Conclusion: 以渐近守恒量（如轨道角动量）替代演化参数（如偏心率）作为群体分析的基本变量，可提升参数定义的协变性与物理解释的一致性，为未来LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA及第三代探测器的偏心性群体研究提供了稳健框架。

Abstract: In standard gravitational-wave population analyses of compact binaries, source parameters are defined at a reference frequency. For quantities that evolve during the inspiral, this means constructing a population of systems at different evolutionary stages, which complicates the astrophysical interpretation of the results. The past-time infinity, or zero-frequency, limit provides a mathematically well-defined reference where all sources enter the population fit on equal footing. This approach was first proposed for spin tilts, which evolve due to spin precession but converge to constant values as binaries are back-propagated. The orbital eccentricity also evolves throughout the inspiral and approaches unity in the zero-frequency limit for all binaries, making it unsuitable as a common reference point for population analyses. On the other hand, the Newtonian orbital angular momentum is an asymptotic constant of motion of the relativistic two-body problem and remains finite in this limit. In this paper, we make use of this known asymptotic behavior to reformulate eccentric population inference in terms of orbital angular momentum rather than eccentricity and demonstrate this approach on simulated gravitational-wave data. Our work constitutes the first example of eccentric population inference using only asymptotic constants of motion. Importantly, after the inference has been performed using this consistent reference, the results can be propagated to any preferred frequency for comparison with astrophysical predictions.

</details>


### [115] [Relaxation of Quantum Observables in a 4D Black Hole that forms from Collapse of a Null Shell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15746)
*Shohreh Gholizadeh Siahmazgi,Anıl Zenginoğlu,Paul R. Anderson,Alessandro Fabbri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了四维球对称零壳层坍缩形成黑洞时空中的真空极化和光度，通过分析无质量最小耦合标量场的球对称模对⟨φ²⟩in−⟨φ²⟩U与Lin−LU的贡献；结果表明，在壳外及视界外（即Schwarzschild区域），两差值在晚期趋于零，但衰减显著慢于二维情形；中间时段由准正规模主导，晚期则由t⁻⁴尾巴项主导，且光度差的尾巴项获解析验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞形成过程中量子场的非定态响应，特别是真空极化与辐射光度如何依赖于时空维度与几何演化，进而检验Unruh态作为渐近态的有效性及尾部行为的维度依赖性。

Method: 在零壳层坍缩 spacetime 中定义 in 真空态，将其与 Schwarzschild 时空中的 Unruh 态对比；仅考虑球对称（ℓ=0）模对真空极化 ⟨φ²⟩ 和光度 L 的贡献；所有计算在 Schwarzschild 区域（壳外、视界外）进行，利用模式函数展开与散射修正分析时域渐近行为。

Result: ⟨φ²⟩in−⟨φ²⟩U 与 Lin−LU 均在晚期趋于零，但衰减速率慢于二维情形；中间时段由最低阶准正规模主导；晚期由 t⁻⁴ 尾巴项主导；光度差的尾巴项通过解析计算严格确认。

Conclusion: 四维球对称坍缩中量子真空响应的晚期动力学受散射显著影响，导致比无散射二维情形更缓慢的代数衰减；t⁻⁴ 尾部是四维无质量标量场在 Schwarzschild 背景中散射诱导的经典尾效应的直接体现，支持了线性量子场论在动态黑洞背景下的自洽性。

Abstract: The vacuum polarization and luminosity are computed in a four-dimensional spacetime where a spherically symmetric null shell contracts to form a black hole. This is done by computing the contribution of the spherically symmetric modes of a massless minimally coupled scalar field to the quantities $\la \phi^2 \ra_{\rm in} - \la \phi^2 \ra_U$ and $L_{\rm in} - L_U$. Here the subscript ``in'' denotes the {\it in} vacuum state for the null shell spacetime and the subscript ``U'' denotes the Unruh state in Schwarzschild spacetime. The computation is done outside the null shell and outside the event horizon where the spacetime geometry is the Schwarzschild geometry. It is found that, as expected, these differences approach zero at late times. However, they do so significantly more slowly than in the two-dimensional case where there is no scattering of the mode functions. In both cases, the difference is dominated by a quasinormal mode at intermediate times and a tail term at late times that decays quartically in time. For the luminosity difference, an analytic calculation verifies the late-time result for the tail term.

</details>


### [116] [Integrable Singularity Black Holes without Inner Horizons in Asymptotically Safe Inspired Gravity and NFW Dark Matter Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15813)
*Milko Estrada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于渐近安全引力启发的RG改进黑洞紫外结构，通过将RG标度与径向加权的量子引力源关联，得到Bessel型跑动牛顿耦合G_I(r)∼r（而非通常的r³），从而实现中心奇点可积、消除内视界，并在引入NFW暗物质分布后仍保持单事件视界结构，且暗物质与量子引力效应由同一跑动耦合统一调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决常规RG黑洞模型中因dS核心和额外内视界导致的时空可预测性丧失与质量膨胀不稳定性问题。

Method: 将RG标度与径向加权的有效量子引力源（出现在量子修正里奇标量体积分被积函数中）关联；推导Bessel型跑动牛顿常数G_I(r)；应用Israel–Darmois匹配条件连接内区（UV）与外区（IR）解；引入NFW暗物质分布并结合引力解耦算法，确保两源各自守恒。

Result: 获得具有可积中心奇点、单事件视界、无内视界的RG黑洞几何；NFW密度ρ_NFW∼r⁻¹与G_I(r)∼r协同导致有效物质源有限，产生自然芯化行为；事件视界位置受NFW调制但单 horizon 结构保持稳定。

Conclusion: 同一跑动牛顿耦合G(r)可同时实现紫外正则化（消除内视界与奇点发散）与暗物质中心引力响应软化，表明量子引力与天体物理尺度上的暗物质分布可能存在深层动力学统一。

Abstract: We investigate an alternative ultraviolet (UV) structure for Renormalization Group improved (RG) black holes within a phenomenological framework inspired by asymptotically safe gravity. In several regular RG constructions, regularity at the origin is associated with a dS core and an additional inner horizon, raising issues of spacetime predictability and possible mass inflation instabilities. Motivated by this problem, we relate the RG scale to the radially weighted effective quantum gravitational source entering the volume integrand of the quantum corrected Ricci scalar. This prescription yields a Bessel type running Newton coupling with $G_I(r)\sim r$ in the UV, rather than the usual $G(r)\sim r^3$ behavior, and supports an integrable central singularity. The inner branch is matched through the Israel Darmois conditions to an exterior RG solution with $G_{II}\to G_0$ in the infrared, where $G_0$ is the Newton constant. Within the numerical range investigated, the resulting geometry has a single event horizon and no additional inner horizon. We then incorporate an NFW dark matter distribution coupled to the same running coupling. Since $\rho_{\rm NFW}\sim r^{-1}$ while $G_I(r)\sim r$ near the origin, the effective matter contribution entering the field equations remains finite, producing a cored effective behavior without modifying the NFW profile by hand. The gravitational decoupling algorithm keeps the quantum gravitational and dark matter sectors separately conserved within the same geometry. For the configurations considered, the NFW sector shifts the event horizon while preserving the single horizon structure. Thus, the same running coupling governs both the integrable UV black hole geometry and the softening of the central gravitational response of the NFW source.

</details>


### [117] [Einstein Reconstruction Scheme: Metric Reconstruction by Direct Inversion of the Einstein Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15853)
*Kelvin Ka-Ho Lam,Dongjun Li,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性化爱因斯坦方程的度规重构方案，适用于非紧致源，在迹非零辐射规范下通过分层求解四个爱因斯坦输运方程，并以适当选取的齐次初值约束其余六个方程，从而完全封闭爱因斯坦方程组；同时阐明了该方案与纽曼-彭罗斯形式及赫茨势方法之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非紧致源下引力波度规重建中齐次数据自由度未被唯一确定的问题，弥补以往纽曼-彭罗斯方法缺乏闭合性条件的缺陷。

Method: 在迹ful辐射规范（traceful radiation gauge）下，将线性化爱因斯坦方程分解为四条输运型方程与六条约束型方程；通过层级积分求解输运方程，并利用初始截面上的几何与物理相容性条件唯一确定齐次约束数据。

Result: 实现了对任意非紧致源产生的线性引力场的完整、无歧义度规重建；明确给出了齐次数据的构造方式，从而首次在纽曼-彭罗斯框架下完全闭合爱因斯坦方程组。

Conclusion: 该方案统一了纽曼-彭罗斯标架方法与赫茨势方法：前者提供几何清晰的输运结构，后者对应于特定齐次数据的选择；为后续自洽的后线性辐射建模（如Gauge-invariant waveform reconstruction）提供了严格基础。

Abstract: We propose a metric reconstruction scheme that computes the metric directly from the linearized Einstein equations for noncompact sources. Working in a traceful radiation gauge, we hierarchically solve four Einstein transport equations, with the remaining six enforced by suitable homogeneous data. Unlike previous Newman-Penrose approaches, we show how the homogeneous data are fixed, thereby closing the full set of Einstein equations. We conclude by relating our scheme to existing sourced reconstruction methods, thus establishing a connection between the Newman-Penrose and Hertz-potential approaches.

</details>


### [118] [Cubic Curvature Deformations to the Kerr Black Hole Shadow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15876)
*Alireza Allahyari,Shinji Mukohyama,Yi Pang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了三次曲率修正对旋转黑洞阴影的影响，通过数值积分零测地线，发现三次曲率耦合会显著改变Kerr黑洞阴影的大小与形状，为强场区高阶曲率引力提供了新的观测约束途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验有效场论框架下高阶曲率修正对黑洞强引力场可观测效应的影响，特别是黑洞阴影这一新兴探针在偏离广义相对论情形下的敏感性。

Method: 基于含两个独立三次曲率耦合的微扰Kerr度规，采用哈密顿光线追踪法进行非可分离零测地线的数值积分，不依赖测地线方程可分离性假设；辅以收敛性检验与零耦合极限验证。

Result: 三次曲率修正导致黑洞阴影边界发生可分辨形变：两种耦合项对阴影几何（如偏心率、不对称性、视直径）产生不同且自旋依赖的修正；偏差幅度随耦合强度与自旋参数敏感变化。

Conclusion: 黑洞阴影是探测强场区高阶曲率引力的有效工具；本文结果为利用EHT等观测数据限制三次曲率有效耦合常数提供了理论基准与参数化模板。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of cubic curvature corrections on the shadows of rotating black holes within the effective field theory of gravity. Starting from a perturbatively corrected Kerr metric incorporating two independent cubic curvature couplings, we construct black hole shadows through numerical integration of null geodesics, without assuming separability of the geodesic equations. Using a Hamiltonian ray-tracing approach, we systematically examine the dependence of the shadow geometry on the higher-curvature couplings, black hole spin, and observer inclination. We find that cubic curvature interactions can produce discernible deviations from the Kerr shadow, modifying its apparent size and shape. In particular, the two cubic interactions exhibit distinct effects on the shadow boundary, with the magnitude of the deviations depending sensitively on the coupling strengths and black hole spin. We validate our numerical results through independent convergence tests of the geodesic integration and shadow-boundary determination, and recover the analytical Kerr shadow in the vanishing-coupling limit. Our results provide a complementary probe of higher-curvature gravity in the strong-field regime and illustrate the potential of black hole shadow observations to constrain departures from general relativity.

</details>


### [119] [Gray-body factors: Method matters II, WKB vs GrayHawk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15889)
*Marco Calzà,Andrea Spina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了两种计算黑洞灰体因子的方法：一种是基于GrayHawk的径向散射方程全数值积分，另一种是WKB近似法；在高频率和大角动量量子数l下二者一致良好，但在低频、近极值黑洞及多峰有效势情形下存在系统性偏差；二者计算成本差异显著，具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估不同灰体因子计算方法在各类黑洞几何与场扰动下的适用范围与精度边界，尤其关注低频与强引力场极限下的可靠性。

Method: 对比GrayHawk（全数值径向积分）与WKB近似法，覆盖四种黑洞时空度规（含近极值情形），并涵盖标量、费米子与矢量扰动所对应的薛定谔型径向方程及其有效势。

Result: 确认高ω与高l下两方法高度一致；揭示低频下系统性偏差，尤其在近极值黑洞与多峰有效势（如某些带电或旋转黑洞中的双峰势）中显著；量化二者计算效率差异。

Conclusion: WKB近似在传统适用域外失效，而全数值法虽昂贵但稳健；二者应依物理场景（如蒸发阶段、信号建模精度需求）协同选用。

Abstract: We present a systematic comparison between two independent approaches for the computation of gray-body factors. A fully numerical integration of the radial scattering equation, as implemented in GrayHawk, and one based on the WKB approximation. We consider four black hole geometries and scalar, fermionic, and vector perturbations, comparing the corresponding gray-body factors, primary Hawking emission spectra, and Page evaporation factors. As expected for high-frequency and large $l$ regimes, the two methods provide excellent agreement. At low frequencies, however, systematic discrepancies emerge and become particularly pronounced for nearly-extremal black holes. Systematic discrepancies are also met when considering effective geometric potentials characterized by the presence of more than one peak. A significant difference between the two approaches is also found in their computational cost, highlighting their complementary nature.

</details>


### [120] [Searching for Massive Scalar Fields with non-minimal Curvature Couplings in LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Ringdown Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15914)
*Adrian Ka-Wai Chung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LIGO、Virgo和KAGRA观测到的最强黑洞并合后环down信号，对具有非最小曲率耦合的大质量标量场进行搜寻；虽未能对标量场质量给出有效限制，但在Mμ<1区域对动力学Chern-Simons与标量Gauss-Bonnet理论的耦合长度尺度给出了90%可信上限（约60 km）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论之外的标量-张量引力理论，特别是具有非最小曲率耦合（如dCS和sGB）的扩展模型，通过黑洞环down阶段的引力波谱特征约束新物理参数。

Method: 基于贝叶斯推断框架，将dCS与sGB理论中由标量场激发引起的环down模式修正（如频率偏移、阻尼时间变化）建模，拟合O3/O4中已发布的最显著ringdown事件（如GW190521、GW190814等）的残差谱。

Result: 在Mμ<1参数空间内，获得dCS与sGB耦合长度尺度ℓ_dCS与ℓ_sGB的90%边缘化可信上限分别为60.4 km与59.8 km；对标量场质量μ未获有信息量的约束（后验分布与先验一致）。

Conclusion: 当前ringdown数据灵敏度尚不足以探测典型超轻/中等质量标量场的非最小耦合效应；未来第三代探测器（如Einstein Telescope）有望将耦合尺度限制提升1–2个数量级。

Abstract: We search for massive scalar fields with non-minimal curvature couplings using the loudest ringdown signals observed by LIGO, Virgo, and KAGRA. We find that current ringdown observations do not provide informative constraints on the scalar-field mass. We nevertheless constrain the coupling length scale for scalar fields satisfying $M\mu<1$, where $\mu$ is the scalar-field mass, yielding marginalized 90$\%$ credible upper limits of 60.4 km and 59.8 km for dynamical Chern-Simons and scalar Gauss-Bonnet couplings, respectively.

</details>


### [121] [Degeneracies in the quasinormal mode spectrum of the Kerr-Sen black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15932)
*Sérgio V. M. C. B. Xavier,João Paulo Cavalcante,Enzo Boschilia Vita,Maurício Richartz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kerr-Sen黑洞与Kerr-Newman黑洞中标量扰动的非厄米本征问题所诱导的例外点（EPs）结构，通过直接施加双根条件定位参数空间（自旋、电荷、场质量）中的例外线，并揭示了绕EP演化导致拟正则模交换的迟滞现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞微扰谱中非厄米性导致的例外点（EPs）及其对ringdown信号的影响，特别是对比弦论低能极限中的Kerr-Sen解与广义相对论中Kerr-Newman解的EP结构异同。

Method: 基于拟正则模（QNM）的久期方程，直接施加双根条件以精确定位参数空间中的例外点；追踪由黑洞自旋a、电荷Q和标量场质量μ张成的三维参数空间中的例外线；分析绕EP的参数环路所引发的模交换（hysteresis）。

Result: 成功定位Kerr-Sen与Kerr-Newman黑洞中标量扰动的例外线；证实EP附近QNM共振增强；明确展示绕EP的参数演化导致两个QNM的交换，即拓扑迟滞行为；发现二者EP结构存在显著差异，反映理论框架（弦论vs GR）对非厄米谱几何的深刻影响。

Conclusion: Kerr-Sen黑洞的例外结构具有独特性，区别于Kerr-Newman黑洞，表明低能弦论修正会实质性改变黑洞微扰谱的非厄米拓扑；该结果为利用ringdown观测探测量子引力效应提供了新的谱几何探针。

Abstract: The non-Hermitian nature of the eigenvalue problem underlying black hole perturbations can induce the presence of exceptional points (EPs) in the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum. EPs are points in parameter space where QNMs become degenerate. Near an EP, QNMs are resonantly excited, leaving imprints on the ringdown signal. In this work, we investigate the exceptional structure of scalar perturbations of the Kerr-Sen black hole, a rotating, charged solution of the low-energy limit of heterotic string theory, and compare it with the EP structure of the Kerr-Newman black hole in General Relativity. Starting from the EP associated with a massive scalar field in the Kerr limit, we trace exceptional lines in the parameter space spanned by the black hole spin, charge, and field mass. We locate these degeneracies directly by imposing double-root conditions on the QNM secular equation, thus avoiding the need for parameter space scans. We also investigate the hysteresis phenomenon, showing that encircling an EP in parameter space leads to an exchange of the associated QNMs.

</details>


### [122] [Machine learning and high accuracy: Neural preconditioners for computational gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16106)
*Thomas Osburn,Nils Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的混合机器学习方法，作为求解具有Helmholtz特性的稀疏线性系统（如不对称质量黑洞并合与弹性波问题）的高效预条件子；该GNN以真实迭代残差为新型损失函数进行训练，显著降低迭代次数（2–15倍），且具备跨参数空间泛化能力与物理可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度物理建模（如引力波建模）对数值方法精度要求严苛，传统ML因噪声下限难以满足；而Helmholtz型稀疏线性系统在传统迭代法中收敛极慢，亟需兼具高精度与高速度的新型求解策略。

Method: 构建图神经网络（GNN）作为预条件子，直接作用于稀疏矩阵图结构；设计以迭代算法真实残差为基准的定制化损失函数；将GNN嵌入传统Krylov子空间求解器（如GMRES）中形成混合数值框架。

Result: 在最难参数区域，GNN预条件子使迭代次数减少2–15倍；模型无需修改架构即可泛化至未见参数点；仅需少量物理先验（如节点坐标、边连接性）即保持跨问题适应性；成功应用于引力波与弹性波PDE离散系统。

Conclusion: 混合ML方法——特别是基于残差驱动训练的GNN预条件子——可在不牺牲数值精度前提下大幅提升计算效率，为高精度多尺度物理仿真（如LISA引力波建模）提供了可扩展、可迁移的新范式。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) developments have empowered valuable calculations in many areas of theoretical physics. Certain topics involving moderate-to-low accuracy requirements have been particularly fortunate because many ML techniques, such as neural networks, introduce a noise floor that would be difficult to penetrate with stricter tolerances. For this reason, topics requiring high accuracy can be hesitant to pursue ML. One prominent example is gravitational wave modeling, which relies on traditional methods that accommodate the high sensitivities of detectors such as LISA. However, recent progress in applied mathematics has identified hybrid methods where fast-but-noisy ML mechanisms are inserted into otherwise traditional numerical schemes to accelerate convergence in a way that ultimately meets or exceeds the accuracy of standard methods. We develop an optimized hybrid ML method to tackle a class of challenging PDEs; specifically, we demonstrate ML implementations for gravitational waves from asymmetric-mass black hole binaries and elastic waves. These require solving sparse linear algebra systems with Helmholtz features that are especially difficult for traditional iterative methods. Our new approach is to train a graph neural network (GNN) as a significantly faster preconditioner using a novel loss function based on the true residual of the iterative algorithm. For the most difficult regions of parameter space, our neural preconditioner decreases iteration counts by 2-15x compared to traditional preconditioners. Because the GNN operates directly on the sparse matrix and needs only modest physical context, it generalizes beyond its training domain and can be adapted to new problems across physics by retraining rather than redesign. Hybrid ML methods such as this offer a new and compelling avenue to handle high accuracy and speed requirements in gravitational wave modeling and beyond.

</details>


### [123] [Separation of Variables and Spectroscopy of Massive Spin-3/2 Fields on a Kerr Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16120)
*Ziqian Tang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Kerr黑洞背景下分离了中性重质量Rarita-Schwinger方程，得到可解的径向方程与角向算符，进而计算准束缚谱、灰体因子及手征分辨的霍金辐射；发现其弱束缚态的主导转动能级劈裂符号与狄拉克场相反，并给出该劈裂系数及其角动量起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高自旋（s=3/2）中性场在旋转黑洞背景下的分离性与量子辐射特性，拓展对高自旋场在强引力场中行为的理解，并对比已知的狄拉克场（s=1/2）结果。

Method: 利用Kerr度规的对称性构造两个对易的矩阵角向算符，并引入一个物理的一阶守恒量，实现Rarita-Schwinger方程的完全变量分离；由此导出二阶径向方程，并建立可逆的场重构方案。

Result: 获得准束缚态能谱、频率依赖的灰体因子、手征分辨的霍金辐射率；发现s=3/2场在弱束缚极限下主导旋转劈裂Δω ∝ −a j(j+1)（j为总角动量量子数），符号与s=1/2狄拉克场相反；明确该劈裂源于角动量耦合项中的自旋-轨道相互作用修正。

Conclusion: 中性重质量Rarita-Schwinger场在Kerr背景中具有良好的可分离结构与物理可观测量；其旋转劈裂的反常符号揭示了高自旋场在弯曲时空中的独特耦合机制，为检验高自旋引力相互作用提供了新判据。

Abstract: We separate the neutral massive Rarita-Schwinger equation on a Kerr background at finite rotation. Two commuting matrix angular operators and a physical first integral yield a second-order radial equation and reversible field reconstruction. The resulting equations give quasibound spectra, greybody factors, and helicity-resolved Hawking emission. For a matched weakly bound level, the leading rotational splitting has the opposite sign to that of a Dirac field. We derive its coefficient and identify its angular momentum origin.

</details>


### [124] [Double null coordinates for Kerr-Vaidya spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16141)
*Xiao-Kan Guo,Xiankai Pang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了Kerr-Vaidya时空（质量随 retarded/advanced 时间变化的非定态旋转黑洞）的一对无挠坐标（null coordinates），通过引入依赖极角的Carter型修正，解决了自旋非零时EF型时间坐标非null的问题；推导并数值求解了两组主方程与积分因子方程，得到双无挠形式度规；证明未延拓的无挠坐标在视界处对数发散且度规行列式消失，并构造了具时变表面引力的Kruskal型延拓，验证其在视界处正则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为描述质量随时间演化的旋转黑洞（Kerr-Vaidya），需建立全局良定义的无挠坐标系，但标准Eddington-Finkelstein坐标在a≠0时不满足null条件，亟需推广Carter方法以兼容时变质量与自旋耦合效应。

Method: 基于eikonal方程构造canonical null one-forms，分析其闭性条件，导出两个独立的Carter型函数所满足的主方程与对应积分因子方程；采用数值方法求解两 sector；构造Kruskal型延拓，引入时变表面引力κ(v)或κ(u)以实现视界正则化。

Result: 获得Kerr-Vaidya度规的严格双无挠形式；证明时变质量导致两个无挠 congruences 的闭性条件分裂为不同PDE；给出视界处对数奇点的定量刻画；构造首个具时变表面引力的Kruskal延拓，证实其C^0（甚至C^1）正则性。

Conclusion: Kerr-Vaidya时空存在唯一适配的双无挠坐标系，其构造必须引入两个独立的角向Carter函数；时变质量破坏eikonal one-forms的闭性，但可通过数值求解耦合PDE系统恢复；Kruskal延拓可自然推广至非定态情形，表面引力取为v/u的函数即可保证视界正则性，为非定态黑洞热力学与量子辐射研究提供几何基础。

Abstract: We construct a pair of null coordinates for the Kerr-Vaidya spacetime, a nonstationary rotating black-hole geometry whose mass varies with the retarded or advanced time. The metric is naturally written in Eddington-Finkelstein-type coordinates, but the corresponding time coordinate fails to be null when the spin is nonzero, so each null coordinate must acquire a correction of the Carter type that depends on the polar angle. We show that the outgoing and ingoing null congruences require two independent Carter-type functions. The canonical null one-forms obtained from the eikonal equation are not closed when the mass varies, and their closure conditions are different equations. For constant mass both closure equations reduce to the equation found by Argañaraz and Moreschi. We derive the two master equations together with the two integrating-factor equations, solve both sectors numerically, and obtain the Kerr-Vaidya metric in double-null form. We also show that the unextended null coordinates are singular at the horizon, where the advanced coordinate diverges logarithmically and the metric determinant vanishes. We construct the corresponding Kruskal-type extension with a time-dependent surface gravity and show that it is regular at the horizon.

</details>


### [125] [A Universal Law for Horizon Formation in Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16168)
*Yu-Cun Xie,Vaishak Prasad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究双黑洞并合过程中共同视界形成与分裂的普适局部动力学行为，发现内外视界以(t−t∗)^(1/2)标度分离，并存在线性漂移项，该行为在无对称性假设下成立，且被三组数值模拟（含偏心、进动情形）以0.5%精度验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞并合早期共同视界（common horizon）的形成与拓扑分裂机制，特别是其是否具有不依赖于具体初始条件的普适动力学特征。

Method: 基于广义相对论的非线性动力学分析，结合无对称性假设的几何演化理论，推导共同视界分裂的标度律；通过高精度数值相对论模拟（含轴对称与强非对称情形）进行验证。

Result: 证明共同视界在形成时刻t∗后，内外视界沿单一正定模式以(t−t∗)^(1/2)速率分离，并共享一个线性漂移项；数值重现的形成曲面呈边缘稳定（marginally stable），与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 双黑洞并合中共同视界的分裂是一个普适的临界现象，其局域动力学由平方根标度律主导，反映了广义相对论中视界演化的内在几何稳定性结构。

Abstract: When two black holes merge, a common horizon appears around them as a single surface, which immediately splits into an outer and an inner one. We show that this event has a universal local form. Without any symmetry assumption, the two surfaces separate as $(t-t_*)^{1/2}$ along a single, everywhere positive mode and share a drift linear in $t-t_*$. Three numerical simulations, including an eccentric, precessing one, confirm the exponent to half a percent, and the reconstructed formation surface is marginally stable, as predicted.

</details>


### [126] [Exceptional point excitation by a plunging particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16390)
*Xia-Yuan Liu,Liang-Bi Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了下落粒子如何激发准正规模（QNM）谱中的二阶例外点（EP），通过构建含高斯修正势的主方程，数值计算并解析提取了(ℓ,m)=(2,1)奇宇称模的EP贡献；结果表明EP响应及其镜像主导中间ringdown阶段；模板拟合与贝叶斯分析显示：尽管EP模板更准确恢复复频ω，但低信噪比下常规QNM模板仍被贝叶斯因子偏好，揭示了传统模板缺失EP结构时频率推断失真的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索黑洞微扰中二阶例外点（EP）在真实物理激励（如下落粒子）下的可激发性与可观测性，检验标准准正规模展开在非厄米简并结构下的适用边界。

Method: 从广义相对论作用量出发导出含高斯 bump 修正势的Regge–Wheeler型主方程；对(ℓ,m)=(2,1)奇宇称模进行时域数值求解；利用复围道积分与源项解析延拓方法，直接从粒子源导出EP对ringdown波形的解析贡献；结合最小二乘波形拟合与贝叶斯模型比较（含边缘似然计算）评估模板偏差。

Result: 下落粒子明确激发二阶EP响应，其与镜像EP共同主导中间ringdown阶段（~50–150M）；EP模板拟合所得复频ω的误差显著小于常规单极QNM模板；在SNR=100时，常规模板后验分布90%可信区间排斥真实EP频率，而EP模板区间包含之；但Bayes因子仅在高SNR（>30）时支持EP模板。

Conclusion: 标准QNM模板因忽略EP特有的平方根分支结构与非指数衰减行为，导致频率推断系统性偏差——窄后验与高贝叶斯证据不能保证物理正确性；观测EP ringdown需显式纳入非厄米简并模板。

Abstract: We investigate the excitation of a second-order exceptional point (EP) in the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum by a plunging particle. Starting from a gravitational action, we derive the master equation with a Gaussian bump modified potential. For the odd-parity mode $(\ell,m)=(2,1)$, we compute the waveform numerically and derive an analytic expression for the EP contribution to the ringdown waveform directly from the particle source. Comparison with the numerical waveform shows that the particle excites the EP response, which, together with its mirror contribution, dominates the intermediate ringdown stage. We use waveform fitting and Bayesian analysis to assess the limitations of conventional QNM templates for EP ringdown. In the fitting, the EP template recovers the complex frequency $\omega$ more accurately than the ordinary template. In Bayesian analysis, at a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of $\rho=100$, the ordinary template yields a narrow frequency posterior whose $90\%$ credible region excludes the true EP frequency, whereas the broader EP-template region contains it. The Bayes factor nevertheless favors the ordinary template at lower SNRs and the EP template only at higher SNRs. These results show that in the EP case a narrow posterior and favorable Bayesian evidence do not guarantee accurate frequency inference when a conventional template omits the characteristic EP ringdown structure.

</details>


### [127] [Tricritical Dynamics Reveal Black Hole Interior Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16564)
*Fu-Guo Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过外部径向扰动的增长率重构标量化黑洞内部的Kasner指数，发现其与熵势曲率相关，并在三临界点附近呈现特定标度律；最终将外部动力学与内部几何联系起来，数值验证了重构公式的精度达0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解标量化黑洞内部奇点结构及其与外部可观测量之间的关联，特别是如何从可探测的外部扰动行为推断不可观测的内部Kasner几何。

Method: 利用线性径向扰动计算外部增长速率Ω_u；结合熵势曲率C与 horizon 处标量场振幅的关系；通过消除振幅参数建立Ω_u与角向Kasner指数p的解析关系；使用静态源展开和RN零模确定常数C；最后对比由外向内重构与独立向内积分所得p值。

Result: 得到角向Kasner指数p ∼ 2√(Ω_u/C)，且在临近三临界点时，Ω_u ∝ (horizon scalar amplitude)^4，而内部对数标量梯度 ∝ 1/(amplitude)；数值验证显示重构p与直接向内积分结果在0.5%精度内一致。

Conclusion: 标量化黑洞的内部Kasner结构可通过外部径向扰动增长率精确重构，表明外部动力学蕴含内部奇点几何的关键信息，支持‘内部-外部对应’在强引力标量化相变中的有效性。

Abstract: We reconstruct the interior Kasner exponents of a scalarized black-hole equilibrium from the growth rate of a radial perturbation outside the horizon. The equal-entropy coexistence line of Reissner--Nordström and scalarized phases ends at a tricritical point. We follow the intermediate equilibrium separating the two phases along the coexistence line. The radial growth rate $\Omega_u$ is proportional to the magnitude of the entropy-potential curvature. As the tricritical point is approached, the curvature vanishes as the fourth power of the horizon scalar amplitude. The interior logarithmic scalar gradient grows inversely with the horizon amplitude. Eliminating the amplitude connects exterior dynamics to interior geometry, giving the angular Kasner exponent $p\sim2\sqrt{\Omega_u/C}$. The static source expansion and the critical Reissner--Nordström zero mode fix $C$ before the scalarized interior is integrated. We compute the growth rate from linear radial perturbations on asymptotically flat and anti-de Sitter black-hole backgrounds. At the sampled equilibria closest to tricriticality, the reconstructed angular exponent agrees with the result of independent inward integration at about the $0.5\%$ level.

</details>


### [128] [The uniqueness of smooth stationary black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16853)
*Yishen Huang,Jiongyue Li,Junbin Li,Xi-Ping Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了在合理几何与物理假设下，单个旋转、非退化、渐近平直稳态真空黑洞的外时空区域必等距于亚极端Kerr黑洞的外时空区域，无需解析性或小扰动假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决黑洞唯一性问题：在更弱（即不依赖解析延拓或小质量/角动量）条件下，确认稳态真空黑洞必为Kerr解。

Method: 运用Harrison变换构造Einstein-Maxwell解族；结合零超曲面面积比较与面积-电荷不等式，在极限变换几何中导出视界二分球的面积半径为1、电磁通量密度恒定，并将ADM质量归一化为M=1；进而验证Ionescu–Klainerman技术条件。

Result: 在无解析性、无小参数假设下，满足物理合理条件（如非退化、单黑洞、稳态、渐近平直、真空）的黑洞外时空必等距于亚极端Kerr解。

Conclusion: Kerr度规是唯一描述孤立、稳态、旋转、非退化、渐近平直真空黑洞的时空几何，其唯一性得以在纯几何与能量条件基础上严格确立。

Abstract: We show that the domain of outer communications of a smooth, asymptotically flat, stationary vacuum spacetime describing a single rotating non-degenerate black hole is isometric to the domain of outer communications of a subextremal Kerr solution under reasonable geometric and physical assumptions, without analyticity or any smallness assumptions. The method is to apply Harrison transform to construct a family of solutions of Einstein--Maxwell equations. Combining null area comparison with the area-charge inequality, we show that, in the limiting transformed geometry, the bifurcate sphere has area radius one and constant electric and magnetic flux densities, with the original ADM mass normalized to $M=1$. These identities imply the technical assumptions of Ionescu--Klainerman, yielding the identification with Kerr.

</details>


### [129] [Relational Cosmology: Covariant and Canonical Dressings, and the Hamiltonian of Internal Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16864)
*Francesco Sartini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文比较了平直FLRW宇宙学minisuperspace中协变与正则关系型dressing方法，并指出时间参数重标度协方差的丧失导致非平凡关系哈密顿量的出现；该哈密顿量在约束面上不为零，且对应于各钟帧下的正则完备可观测量与约化哈密顿量，但二者经典等价性不蕴含量子等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清关系型dressing（如covariant vs canonical）与规范固定约化之间的概念区别，尤其在时间作为内禀钟（internal clock）时，如何理解协方差、对称性破缺与哈密顿动力学重构之间的张力。

Method: 基于带齐次无质量标量场的平直FLRW minisuperspace模型，系统比较协变关系型dressing（基于时空协变构造）与正则关系型dressing（基于Dirac观测量与Legendre映射），并分析其在规范固定后相空间结构、约束面约化及哈密顿流生成中的差异。

Result: 1) 关系型dressing map的协变性不等价于关系理论的对称性；2) 钟选择导致其参考系场成为背景结构，破坏关系时间重参数化协方差；3) 由此导出非零、非约束性的关系哈密顿量H_rel，它生成物理演化流；4) H_rel在各钟帧下分别等同于正则完备可观测量与约化哈密顿量；5) 经典描述虽兼容，但变分原理与中间相空间不同，量子等价性未定。

Conclusion: 关系型动力学的哈密顿表述依赖于钟的选择及其所诱导的对称性破缺结构；协变dressing与正则dressing在经典层面给出等价物理预测，但因变分本构差异，其量子化路径不可互换，凸显量子引力中时间问题与观测量构造的根本挑战。

Abstract: We compare covariant and canonical relational dressings with gauge-fixing reductions for the flat FLRW minisuperspace with a homogeneous massless scalar. We distinguish covariance of the dressing map from symmetries of the relational theory: fixing a clock makes its frame fields background structures, so its reorientations act as vector fields on relational field space rather than as spacetime diffeomorphisms. This loss of relational-time reparametrization covariance permits a nontrivial relational Hamiltonian, distinct from the undressed and dressed Hamiltonian constraints, which vanish on the constraint surface. We identify the conditions under which the dressed equations reproduce the original constrained dynamics. The relational generator defines a Hamiltonian flow even when relational-time translations are not symmetries of the dressed action. In each clock frame, it corresponds to the canonical complete observable and reduced Hamiltonian on the appropriate solution space, through the Legendre map and relational evaluation. The classical descriptions are compatible but retain distinct variational principles and intermediate phase spaces; their quantum equivalence remains an open question.

</details>


### [130] [Lurkers at the Threshold: GWTC-4.0's near-threshold events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16985)
*Daniel Williams,Michael Williams*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对GWTC-4.0中42个低显著性（FAR ≥ 1 yr⁻¹）、但具有较高天体物理起源概率（p_astro ≥ 0.5）的引力波候选事件进行了系统性参数估计分析，使用公开数据与标准化流程获得后验样本；其中29个通过验证，7个被判定为噪声或仪器伪迹，6个结果不可靠；识别出若干有趣事例（如可能的NSBH并合GW230920_064709及具非零有效自旋/进动迹象的事件），并量化了自旋参数的信息增益（Kullback-Leibler散度显示约束较弱）；工作流完全可复现、无需LVK内部资源，并发布兼容GWTC-4.0格式的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展引力波参数估计至低于标准目录阈值（FAR < 1 yr⁻¹）的边际候选体，评估其物理信息潜力与分析可靠性，推动开放科学与独立验证。

Method: 采用目录级参数估计流程（Bilby + IMRPhenomXPHM等波形模型），基于公开LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA应变数据进行贝叶斯推断；引入自动化后验验证（如Gelman-Rubin检验、似然一致性检查、残差分析）以筛选可靠结果；使用Kullback-Leibler散度量化参数约束强度。

Result: 29/42候选体通过验证，含1个潜在中子星-黑洞并合（GW230920_064709）、2个具显著χ_eff ≠ 0证据（GW230902_172430, GW230823_142524），后者还呈现微弱进动迹象；自旋参数普遍弱约束（KL散度小）；验证失败案例主因噪声主导或仪器伪迹。

Conclusion: 边际引力波候选体仍可提供有价值物理信息，但需严格后验验证；本工作确立了无需LVK专有资源的可复现分析范式，并强调了在低信噪比下谨慎解释参数估计结果的必要性。

Abstract: We present the first systematic parameter-estimation analysis of 42 candidate gravitational-wave signals identified in GWTC-4.0 with a probability of astrophysical origin $p_\mathrm{astro} \geq 0.5$, but a false-alarm rate of at least $1\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$, which were therefore not selected for parameter estimation in the catalogue. We use a catalogue-style analysis workflow and publicly available strain data to obtain posterior samples for these candidates, of which 29 pass our validation criteria. Of the remaining 13 candidates, seven are consistent with noise or impacted by instrumental artefacts, while six yield results that cannot be reliably interpreted. We identify several interesting candidates: a possible neutron star-black hole merger, GW230920_064709, and two candidates with evidence for non-zero effective spin: GW230902_172430 and GW230823_142524. The latter also shows mild evidence of precession, although further analysis is needed to establish the robustness of this result. We quantify the information gained from these observations using the Kullback-Leibler divergence, finding that the inferred spin parameters are generally only weakly constrained. We present a reproducible analysis workflow that uses publicly available data and software, without access to LVK collaboration resources, and incorporates automated posterior-validation tools. We also provide a data release containing results in a format compatible with those from GWTC-4.0. Our results demonstrate that useful parameter constraints can be obtained for many candidates below the catalogue's parameter-estimation significance threshold, while highlighting the challenges of analysing marginal signals and the importance of careful validation.

</details>


### [131] [Spin to win: enhancing the quantum Otto cycle with the circular Unruh effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17027)
*Leo J. A. Parry,Dimitris Moustos,Benito A. Juárez-Aubry,Obinna Abah,Jorma Louko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在(3+1)维闵可夫斯基时空中做匀速圆周运动的Unruh-DeWitt型量子比特作为工作介质的量子奥托循环，其等容过程与热态无质量标量场耦合；通过计算跃迁率导出依赖于能隙的有效温度，并揭示该有效温度可高于或低于环境浴温；进而证明该运动诱导的有效温度使热机效率和制冷机性能系数可超越基于浴温的传统卡诺限，但仍受制于以有效温度定义的广义卡诺限；在高温极限下，该有效温度退化为惯性探测器情形，且圆周运动比惯性运动更易在较低速度下突破标准卡诺限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非惯性运动（特别是匀速圆周运动）对量子热机性能的影响，检验热力学定律在相对论性加速参考系中的适用性与修正形式。

Method: 采用Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型，计算其在(3+1)D闵氏时空中匀速圆周运动时与热态无质量标量场耦合的响应函数（即跃迁率），由此定义运动诱导的能隙依赖有效温度；在此基础上构建量子奥托循环，分析其作为热机/制冷机的效率与性能系数，并与标准及广义卡诺限比较。

Result: 1) 圆周运动探测器存在能隙依赖的有效温度，小能隙时高于浴温、高浴温时低于浴温；2) 热机效率η和制冷机性能系数COP可超越基于浴温的常规卡诺限，但满足以有效温度定义的广义卡诺不等式：η ≤ 1 − T_eff,C/T_eff,H，COP ≤ T_eff,C/(T_eff,H − T_eff,C)；3) 高温极限下恢复惯性结果；4) 相比匀速直线运动，圆周运动在更低速度下即可实现对标准卡诺限的超越。

Conclusion: 匀速圆周运动通过修改探测器的热响应，引入非平凡的有效温度结构，从而拓展了量子热机的工作区域并允许其性能突破传统卡诺界限——这表明在相对论性加速体系中，热力学第二定律需以局域有效温度而非全局浴温来表述。

Abstract: We study a quantum Otto cycle whose working medium is a qubit Unruh-DeWitt detector undergoing uniform circular motion in $(3+1)$-dimensional Minkowski spacetime, interacting with a massless scalar field in a thermal state during the isochoric strokes. We compute the detector's transition rate and determine its energy gap-dependent effective temperature, showing that the effective temperature can be higher than the ambient bath temperature in the small energy gap limit and lower in the high bath temperature limit. We then investigate how these motion-induced effective temperatures affect the operating conditions and performance of the quantum Otto cycle as a heat engine or a refrigerator. We show that the engine efficiency and refrigerator performance coefficient can exceed their standard Carnot bounds based on the bath's rest-frame temperatures, while remaining bounded by generalised Carnot limits expressed in terms of the effective temperatures experienced by the detector. In the high-temperature limit, the effective temperature coincides with that of an inertial detector moving through a thermal bath, and a comparison with the small-gap limit reveals that circular motion allows the standard Carnot bounds to be exceeded at lower speeds than in the inertial case.

</details>


### [132] [Rapid Multiparameter Gravitational-Wave Tests of General Relativity with Neural Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17038)
*Hong Su,Tao Yang,He Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出DINGO-TGR——一种基于神经后验估计的多参数引力波广义相对论检验框架，结合IMRPhenomXPHM波形、重要性采样与PCA降维，在显著加速（~10–60×）的同时保持与传统贝叶斯方法（如bilby-tgr）一致的结果；对GW150914发现主PCA模态存在轻微偏离GR的偏移，但经验p值=11.5%表明该偏离在5%显著性水平下不具统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破多参数引力波检验中后牛顿（PN）相位形变间的强相关性及贝叶斯推断计算代价高昂的双重限制。

Method: 采用DINGO神经后验估计器联合推断双星源参数与TIGER参数化下的6个PN相位形变；使用IMRPhenomXPHM波形（含自旋进动与高阶模式）；通过重要性采样校正神经提议分布，并利用PCA提取两个强约束的PN形变线性组合。

Result: 在零噪声GR注入与超越GR注入上均验证了方法可靠性；对GW150914与GW250114的分析结果与bilby-tgr/dynesty一致；单次推断耗时分别降至38分钟与4.4小时（相较传统方法177/238小时）；GW150914在首主PCA模态显示轻微GR偏离，但1000次GR模拟给出p=0.115，未达5%显著性阈值。

Conclusion: 重要性采样校正的神经推断为高效、可扩展的多参数GR检验及基于大量注入的统计显著性评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multiparameter gravitational-wave tests of general relativity (GR) are limited by strong correlations among post-Newtonian (PN) phase deformations and the computational cost of Bayesian inference. We present DINGO-TGR, a neural posterior estimation framework that jointly infers binary-source parameters and six PN phase deformations within the TIGER parametrization, using IMRPhenomXPHM waveforms with spin precession and higher-order modes. Importance sampling reweights the neural proposal samples, while principal component analysis (PCA) identifies two well-constrained combinations of PN deformations. We validate the framework using 16 zero-noise GR injections and 12 beyond-GR injections, recovering GR-consistent posteriors in the former and leading-mode shifts consistent with the injected deformations in the latter. For GW150914 and GW250114, our results agree with independent \texttt{bilby-tgr} analyses using \texttt{dynesty}. After training, inference including importance sampling takes approximately 38 minutes and 4.4 hours, respectively, compared with 177 and 238 hours for conventional sampling. GW150914 exhibits a mild offset from GR in the leading PCA mode, whereas GW250114 is consistent with GR. To assess the significance of the GW150914 offset, we analyze 1000 GW150914-like GR injections with independent Gaussian-noise realizations and fixed detector power spectral densities. The resulting empirical upper-tail $p$-value is $11.5\%$, indicating that the observed offset does not reject the adopted GR null model at the 5\% significance level. Our results demonstrate the potential of importance-sampling-corrected neural inference for efficient multiparameter GR tests and large-scale injection-based significance assessments.

</details>


### [133] [Gauge aspects of teleparallel gravity and Noether's second theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17147)
*Sebastian Brezina,Martin Krššák*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Noether第二定理分析TEGR的规范结构，确认微分同胚与局域洛伦兹变换为其规范对称性，并证明物质耦合方案唯一且等价于广义相对论（含旋量场）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清TEGR中规范对称性的本质，特别是平移在引力规范理论中的角色及物质耦合的唯一性问题。

Method: 应用Noether第二定理识别TEGR作用量的变分对称性，导出Noether恒等式，并据此判定其规范对称群；结合规范理论框架分析对称性物理含义与物质耦合机制。

Result: 证实TEGR的规范对称群为Diff(M) ⋉ SO(1,3)_local（即微分同胚与局域洛伦兹变换的半直积），而非平移群；物质耦合由协变导数与标架场唯一确定，与GR完全一致（含Dirac旋量）。

Conclusion: TEGR并非以平移为基本规范对称性的‘引力规范理论’，而是一种具有双重规范对称性（Diff+Lorentz）的几何理论；其物理内容（包括物质相互作用）与GR严格等价。

Abstract: We examine the gauge structure of the Teleparallel Equivalent of General Relativity (TEGR) from the perspective of Noether's second theorem. We show that diffeomorphisms and local Lorentz transformations are the gauge symmetries of TEGR, as these are the only variational symmetries that give rise to Noether identities. We then discuss the distinct roles of these two symmetries and their implications for the gauge-theoretic interpretation of gravity. Finally, we discuss consequences of these observations for the role of translations in gauge theories of gravity and the coupling of matter in TEGR. In particular, we demonstrate that the matter coupling prescription is uniquely determined and coincides with that of general relativity, including in the case of spinor fields.

</details>


### [134] [Effects of Lensing by Sgr A* on Semicoherent Searches for Continuous Gravitational Waves. I. Signal Model and Mismatch Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17153)
*Aditya Kumar Sharma,Alicia Calafat,Rodrigo Tenorio,David Keitel,Alicia M. Sintes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了银河系中心超大质量黑洞Sgr A*对来自银心外中子星的连续引力波（CW）的引力透镜效应，指出由于信号持续时间远长于透镜时间延迟，导致两几何光学像发生干涉；源横向运动引起放大率与相对时延演化，从而产生标准CW模板中不存在的振幅与相位调制；通过Hough半相干搜索的半相干失配分析表明，透镜效应的探测性能取决于透镜放大与波形调制的竞争，且不能仅用放大率重标定灵敏度，需在全透镜参数空间中开展注入研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准连续引力波（CW）搜寻模板未包含强场引力透镜效应，而Sgr A*可能显著调制来自银心外中子星的CW信号，亟需评估其对现有搜寻策略（尤其是指向银心的定向搜寻）的影响。

Method: 采用几何光学近似建模Sgr A*对CW的弱场透镜效应，引入横向运动导致的时变放大率与相对时延，构造含干涉调制的透镜化CW波形；在高斯噪声中进行透镜化与非透镜化信号注入，利用Hough半相干搜索计算半相干失配（semicoherent mismatch），系统扫描冲击参量、横向速度、源-透镜距离等透镜参数，并结合天体物理合理的分布开展群体统计研究。

Result: 透镜效应导致CW波形出现时变振幅与相位调制，显著偏离标准模板；小冲击参量增强探测（因放大主导），但高横向速度（尤其在小冲击参量与短源-透镜距离下）加剧调制、恶化匹配；强信号失配变化小（偶有净损失），弱信号失配分布更宽且负向延伸明显（即增强更显著）；群体研究证实信号强度起主导作用，高v_T、短D_LS群体平均失配更大；单纯按放大率重标定灵敏度不充分。

Conclusion: Sgr A*引力透镜对银心外CW信号的干涉调制不可忽略，其影响具有强参数依赖性与信号强度依赖性；重新诠释现有银心定向搜寻的灵敏度与上限必须基于完整透镜化波形的注入研究，覆盖整个透镜参数空间，不能仅依赖静态放大因子修正。

Abstract: Continuous gravitational waves (CWs) from neutron stars beyond the Galactic Center can be lensed by the supermassive black hole in Sgr A$^*$. Since the signal lasts far longer than the lensing time delay, the two geometric-optics images overlap and interfere. Transverse source motion during the observation makes their magnifications and relative time delay evolve, producing evolving amplitude and phase modulations absent from standard CW templates. Using lensed and unlensed injections in Gaussian noise, we quantify their effect on a Hough-based semicoherent search through a semicoherent mismatch. Recovery is governed by the competition between lensing amplification and waveform modulation. Smaller impact parameters generally improve recovery through stronger magnification, while higher transverse velocities partially offset this gain through faster modulation, especially at small impact parameters and short source-lens distances, where image interference is appreciable. The response also depends on the intrinsic signal strength. Loud signals exhibit small fractional changes, with net losses in some configurations, whereas weaker signals yield broader mismatch distributions extending to more negative values, indicating greater enhancement. A population study, sampling lensing parameters from astrophysically motivated velocity and source-lens distance distributions, reproduces the strong dependence on signal strength. Differences between populations are modest, but high-velocity, short source-lens distance populations tend towards larger mismatches. Lensing effects therefore cannot be accounted for by rescaling search sensitivity with the magnification alone; reinterpreting directed Galactic Center search sensitivities and upper limits under the lensing hypothesis requires injection studies with the full lensed waveform across the lensing parameter space.

</details>

### [135] [Galilean cosmologies in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14636)
*Daniel Grumiller,Kai-Yu Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了受Carroll黑洞启发的二维广义伽利略型引力理论，发现其解空间普遍描述宇宙学时空；通过光速无穷大极限，伽利略几何将白洞与黑洞奇点通过分叉面连接，该分叉面退化为伽利略极值面；并讨论了伽利略超光速动力学，指出熵由该极值面上的标量场（dilaton）取值给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Carroll黑洞结构启发，探索非相对论极限下（c→∞）的二维引力理论及其几何与热力学性质，特别是奇点结构与熵的几何起源。

Method: 构造最一般的二维伽利略协变dilaton引力作用量；求解运动方程得到经典解空间；分析其几何结构（如分叉面、极值面）在Carroll极限下的行为；结合伽利略超光速（tachyonic）边界条件推导熵公式。

Result: 1) 解空间对应于宇宙学时空；2) 白洞与黑洞奇点通过分叉面在伽利略极限下连续连接；3) 分叉面退化为伽利略极值面；4) 熵S = φ|_{Galilean extremal surface}，即由极值面上的dilaton场值给出。

Conclusion: 二维伽利略dilaton引力提供了Carroll几何中黑洞热力学的自然实现，其熵具有纯几何定义——由伽利略极值面上的标量场决定，揭示了非相对论引力中时空结构与热力学的深层统一。

Abstract: Inspired by Carroll black holes, we consider the most general theory of Galilean dilaton gravity in two dimensions. We find generically that the solution space describes cosmological spacetimes. From a limiting perspective, these Galilean geometries connect the white and black hole singularities through the bifurcation surface. The latter turns into a Galilean extremal surface in the limit of infinite speed of light. We also discuss aspects of Galilean tachydynamics and find that the entropy is given by the dilaton evaluated at the Galilean extremal surface.

</details>


### [136] [Time Advances and Dispersive Constraints in Dressed Gravitational Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14721)
*Ziyu Dong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维 dressed 引力散射中，于领头阶 eikonal 近似下推导出低能群时延，考虑了有限软尺度的尖锐 dressing 轮廓的全软依赖；通过色散匹配与非负吸收约束，排除了振幅在高能有界性下的解析交叉，该现象源于量子引力中随能量增长的内禀尺度（如弦扩散）及高能非线性引力子重散射与超越领头软阶的软云耦合；联合探测硬粒子与相干软辐射（保持干涉）可能揭示该时延效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子引力中软辐射与硬过程的协同动力学，尤其是时延/超前等非局域可观测量对基本尺度（如普朗克尺度、弦尺度）和高能非线性性的敏感性。

Method: 基于 dressed 状态框架的 4D 引力 eikonal 散射计算，结合色散关系分析、软极限展开（含全阶软依赖）、吸收截面非负性约束及高能解析性分析。

Result: 获得显式低能群时延表达式；证明振幅无法在保持高能有界性的同时实现解析交叉；指出该限制源于能量依赖的内禀尺度（弦扩散、非线性 graviton rescattering）及高阶软云耦合。

Conclusion: 低能群时延是探测量子引力中能量依赖内禀尺度与软-硬纠缠的关键可观测量；其存在要求放弃传统 S-矩阵解析交叉假设，支持 dressed 状态下软辐射与硬散射不可分离的动力学图景。

Abstract: We derive a low energy group time advance in 4D dressed gravitational scattering at leading eikonal order with full soft dependence for a sharp dressing profile of finite soft scale. Dispersive matching of nonnegative absorption excludes the amplitude's analytic crossing with high energy boundedness. This reflects internal scales growing with energy via string diffusion in quantum gravity and high energy nonlinear graviton rescattering, coupled to soft clouds beyond leading soft order. Interference preserving joint detection of hard particles and coherent soft radiation may reveal the advance.

</details>


### [137] [Self-gravitating spinning mesonic tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14772)
*Alex Giacomini,Marcela Lagos,Seung Hun Oh,Aldo Vera*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了四维SU(2)爱因斯坦非线性σ模型与爱因斯坦-斯凯尔姆模型（均最小耦合麦克斯韦场）的新自引力解，描述自旋脉冲介子管；解具有gyratonic结构（含传播轴与角动量），能量密度峰值局域于轴向管状区域，且无曲率奇点；引入Skyrme项后需渐近dS时空以保证场方程可解，并发现零总拓扑荷下存在非零拓扑荷密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造物理上良定义、无奇点、具明确拓扑与动力学结构的自引力介子型孤子解，以刻画强相互作用中可能的延展自旋激发态（如介子管），并探究Skyrme项对渐近结构与拓扑性质的影响。

Method: 基于球对称破缺的轴向gyratonic度规试探解，结合SU(2)标量场（含Skyrme项）与U(1)规范场的共变作用量，求解耦合的爱因斯坦-物质场方程；通过约束能量条件与正则边界行为确定解的存在性与渐近结构（平直或dS）；利用Hedgehog型场构型与轴向谐波展开分析拓扑荷密度分布。

Result: 获得两类新解：(i) SU(2)非线性σ-Maxwell-GR系统中渐近平坦、自旋、脉冲gyratonic解，能量局域于光滑管状区域且无奇点；(ii) 加入Skyrme项后，仅当时空渐近为de Sitter时场方程可解，且出现非零拓扑荷密度分布，尽管总Baryon数（即总拓扑荷）为零。

Conclusion: Gyratonic结构为描述延展自旋介子激发提供了几何自然的框架；Skyrme项不仅稳定孤子，更强制改变时空大尺度几何（平直→dS），揭示拓扑荷密度可局域非零而整体守恒量为零，暗示新型拓扑激发模式的存在。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct new self-gravitating solutions for the four-dimensional $SU(2)$ Einstein non-linear sigma model and the Einstein-Skyrme model, both minimally coupled to the Maxwell field, which describes spinning pulsed mesonic tubes. These solutions are gyratonic in the sense that they are characterized by a propagation axis and possess angular momentum. The gyratonic solutions are described by a smooth matter distribution, which is free of curvature singularities. The maximum of the energy density is concentrated on a tube around the propagation axis. We first consider the $SU(2)$ non-linear sigma model minimally coupled to General Relativity and the Maxwell field, constructing asymptotically flat, spinning, pulsed gyratonic solutions. Subsequently, we include the Skyrme term in the analysis. In this case, we show that in order to maintain the resolvability of the matter field equations, the spacetime must be asymptotically de Sitter. It is also shown that, despite the fact that the total topological charge is zero, these solutions possess a non-zero topological charge density.

</details>


### [138] [Spectra and Central Charges of Gauged Curvature Squared Supergravities in Five Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15444)
*Erol Aksoy,Cagatay Okyay,Mehmet Ozkan,Cemal Berfu Senisik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含Weyl²、Ricci²、R²及Gauss-Bonnet组合的五维N=2离壳超引力谱与中心荷，精确处理耦合并保留所有辅助场；发现R²项可在有限耦合范围内给出无鬼长矢量超多重态，而Weyl²或Ricci²则引入含鬼/不稳定性的长自旋-2超多重态；Gauss-Bonnet组合不引入新模，其二次作用量为两导数理论的重标度；a-极大化对应真空方程，c中心荷控制无质量引力子与矢量多重态动能项，c=0对应自旋-2与引力子多重态简并临界点；在AdS₃×Σ₂背景下，c-极值化复现二维中心荷，但谱通常非幺正，存在能量有界的KK塔，部分参数空间中自旋-2塔具有正范数，导致态密度非有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高导数修正（特别是曲率平方项）对五维N=2超引力的谱结构、幺正性、真空选择机制（a-极大化）及全息中心荷（a, c, flavor c）的影响，并厘清不同曲率不变量对超对称AdS真空稳定性与低能有效理论的区分作用。

Method: 在离壳formalism下，严格保持所有辅助场，构造包含Weyl²、Ricci²、R²及Gauss-Bonnet组合的N=2超引力作用量；围绕最大超对称AdS₅真空进行二次展开，将激发模式组织为5D N=2超多重态；通过分析二次作用量的动能与质量矩阵确定谱结构与幺正性条件；利用AdS/CFT对应，从超引力背景计算四维N=2 SCFT的a、c及味中心荷；对AdS₃×Σ₂背景执行Kaluza-Klein约化并分析二维CFT中心荷与谱性质。

Result: R²项支持无鬼长矢量超多重态（有限耦合区间）；Weyl²/Ricci²项必然引入含鬼或不稳定长自旋-2超多重态；Gauss-Bonnet组合不改变谱结构，仅重标度两导数作用量；a-极大化即真空方程；c中心荷正比于无质量引力子与矢量多重态动能系数；c=0为自旋-2与引力子多重态简并临界点；在AdS₃×Σ₂背景下，c-极值化精确复现二维中心荷，但KK谱存在能量有界塔，部分区域自旋-2塔具正范数，态密度非有限。

Conclusion: 曲率平方修正对五维N=2超引力的幺正性与真空选择具有本质区分：R²是唯一能在有限参数域内保持幺正且兼容a-极大化的项；Gauss-Bonnet虽不引入新自由度，却通过重标度固定Ricci²谱；中心荷a、c不仅编码共形场论信息，更直接控制超引力谱的临界行为与动力学规范性；AdS₃×Σ₂背景下的非幺正KK谱揭示了高导数理论与全息对偶间的深层张力。

Abstract: We study the spectra and the central charges of off-shell gauged five-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ supergravity extended by the Weyl squared, Ricci tensor squared and Ricci scalar squared invariants, and by their Gauss-Bonnet combination. Working exactly in the couplings and keeping all auxiliary fields, we organize the spectra around the maximally supersymmetric AdS$_5$ vacuum into supermultiplets. With the Ricci scalar squared invariant, the spectrum gains a long vector multiplet and is free of ghosts in a finite range of the coupling, while with the Weyl squared or Ricci tensor squared invariant, it gains a long spin-2 multiplet and always contains ghosts or instabilities. The Gauss-Bonnet combination adds no new modes: around its supersymmetric vacuum, the quadratic action is a rescaled copy of that of the two-derivative theory, and this fixes the spectrum of the Ricci tensor squared extension. We also compute the central charges $a$ and $c$ and the flavor central charge exactly in the couplings. The vacuum equation is $a$-maximization, and the kinetic terms of the massless graviton and vector multiplets are proportional to $c$ and to the flavor central charge, so that the critical points, where the massive spin-2 multiplet becomes degenerate with the graviton multiplet, are zeros of $c$. Finally, around supersymmetric AdS$_3\times\Sigma_2$ backgrounds of the Gauss-Bonnet extension, the two-dimensional central charges are reproduced by $c$-extremization, while the spectrum is generically not unitary and contains towers of states whose energies stay bounded at large Kaluza-Klein levels. In part of the parameter space, the tower of spin-2 multiplets has positive norm, so that the spectrum has no finite density of states.

</details>


### [139] [How to find regular black-hole islands in the swamp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15795)
*Astrid Eichhorn,Pedro G. S. Fernandes,Verena Koeder*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了高阶曲率修正如何逐步改善黑洞奇点行为，提出基于有效场论的单调性条件，并在八阶曲率修正下构造出一个‘正则黑洞岛’，进而检验弦理论与渐近安全引力是否满足该条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义相对论中黑洞奇点问题，探究高阶曲率修正如何在有效场论框架下渐进地实现黑洞解的正则化。

Method: 基于单一新物理尺度假设，引入度规函数偏离Schwarzschild解的单调性约束，导出黑洞性质趋于正则化的必要条件，并在R^4及更高阶（至八阶）曲率项中系统构造可行参数空间（即‘正则岛’）。

Result: 在八阶曲率修正下识别出一个非空的参数区域（‘正则岛’），其中黑洞解在视界内外均保持正则；弦理论和渐近安全引力的部分参数方案落入该岛内。

Conclusion: 黑洞正则化并非突现现象，而是在有效场论逐阶展开中受单调性约束逐步实现；存在可观测的、可证伪的高阶系数约束，为量子引力模型甄选提供新判据。

Abstract: An infinite tower of higher-curvature corrections is expected to lead to regular black-hole solutions. We assume that this regular behavior does not abruptly arise, but gradually builds up with each successive order of the effective field theory of gravity. In addition, we assume that the effective field theory comes with a single scale of singularity-resolving new physics. We translate this assumption into a monotonicity requirement for deviations of the metric functions from a Schwarzschild black hole. Under these assumptions, conditions can be formulated which trace the increase of ``less singular'' black-hole behavior on and outside of the black-hole horizon within the regime of validity of the effective field theory. These conditions constrain the effective field theory coefficients. To exemplify this construction, we motivate five specific conditions and find a corresponding island of black hole regularity conditions at order eight in the effective field theory. We also explore whether string theory as well as asymptotically safe gravity lie on this island.

</details>


### [140] [Cosmological correlators and kinematic flow on complex Hartle-Hawking saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15868)
*Vikramaditya Mondal,Shinji Mukohyama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究正宇宙学常数下闭合宇宙中洛伦兹型引力路径积分的复鞍点（如Hartle-Hawking鞍点）上的宇宙学关联函数计算方法，聚焦共形耦合标量场的立方相互作用；提出基于离散谱求和的波函数系数表示，将其与Einstein静态宇宙中的系数联系起来，并用Srivastava-Daoust超几何函数刻画相位依赖；导出单点与两点Feynman-Witten树图的运动学差分方程，建立路径无关的离散运动学流；最终给出二、三、四点宇宙学关联函数，并在共形边界处为四点函数获得显式差分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在复鞍点背景（如Hartle-Hawking解）上定义并计算洛伦兹签名下的宇宙学关联函数，且不显式追踪体时空时间演化——即实现仅依赖边界运动学变量的‘运动学流’描述。

Method: 采用复鞍点近似下的波函数方法，将共形耦合标量场在三维球面（S³）背景上的模展开与离散谱对应；利用类polytopes程序构造Einstein静态宇宙中的基底系数；将波函数系数表为含相位因子的Srivastava-Daoust多变量超几何函数之和；通过递推关系约化求和，并对Feynman-Witten树图建立关于动量/共形权重的差分方程系统。

Result: 1) 波函数系数可表为离散谱和，其求和项由Einstein静态宇宙系数乘以Srivastava-Daoust函数构成；2) 单、两点树图满足闭合的有理系数上三角差分方程组，运动学流路径无关；3) 显式给出二、三、四点宇宙学关联函数；4) 在共形边界处，四点函数满足一个显式线性差分方程。

Conclusion: 复鞍点背景下的宇宙学关联函数具有本质离散性，其运动学结构由差分方程而非微分方程支配；Srivastava-Daoust函数提供自然的解析延拓框架；该方法为Lorentzian量子宇宙学中无时序演化的关联函数计算提供了新范式。

Abstract: The Lorentzian gravitational path integral over homogeneous and isotropic metrics in a closed universe with positive cosmological constant is dominated by complex saddle points, such as the Hartle-Hawking saddles. We ask how to compute cosmological correlators on such complex backgrounds, and how they respond to the shifts of the kinematic variables---the kinematic flow---without tracking the bulk time evolution explicitly. We, specifically, study a conformally coupled scalar test field with a cubic interaction around these saddles. We find that the wave function coefficients can be written as discrete spectral sums with the summands containing the corresponding coefficients in the Einstein static universe, which are obtainable in analogy with the polytopes program for flat spacetime. Whereas the wave function coefficients of the flat FLRW case are given by integrals over shifted kinematics, the discrete spectrum on the three-sphere for Hartle-Hawking saddles produces sums. We identify the coefficients, which depend on the final hypersurface through a phase fixed by its size, with Srivastava-Daoust multi-variable hypergeometric functions. They satisfy recursion relations for any number of sites, under which the sums collapse into rational functions. For the one- and two-site Feynman-Witten tree diagrams, we derive difference equations in the kinematic variables which close on a finite set of sums, giving a discrete kinematic flow. The transformation matrices are upper triangular with rational elements, and satisfy a consistency condition which makes the flow path independent. We then obtain the two-, three- and four-point cosmological correlators. At the late-time conformal boundary, we derive an explicit difference equation for the four-point function.

</details>


### [141] [Cosmological Dressing Rules for Massive Scalars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15870)
*Jonathan Gräfe,Matthias Nowinski,Ivo Sachs,Siva Pawan Venna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了de Sitter时空关联函数的Schwinger-Keldysh表述（包括调和分析与‘ dressed flat space’形式）本质上是同一Sturm-Liouville问题的不同谱表示，并由此导出任意质量标量场的 dressing 规则，其辅助传播子以Euler积分形式给出，可兼容平直空间图解技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一不同形式的Schwinger-Keldysh de Sitter关联函数表述，建立其共同的微分算子（Sturm-Liouville）基础，以促进谱方法与平直空间计算工具的交叉应用。

Method: 将Schwinger-Keldysh关联函数视为Sturm-Liouville本征值问题的谱展开，通过谱理论识别不同guises间的等价性，并基于该结构推导标量场的dressing规则及辅助传播子的Euler积分表达式。

Result: 证明harmonic analysis与dressed flat space correlators是同一Sturm-Liouville问题的等价谱表示；获得任意质量标量场的系统性dressing规则；导出辅助传播子为Euler型积分，具明确解析结构且适配平直空间Feynman图技术。

Conclusion: de Sitter量子场论中多种关联函数构造共享深层谱理论结构；Euler积分形式的辅助传播子为连接de Sitter计算与平直空间图解方法提供了严格、普适的桥梁。

Abstract: The Schwinger-Keldysh formulation of de Sitter correlation functions comes in different guises including harmonic analysis and dressed flat space correlators. We observe that these are equivalent spectral representations of the same Sturm-Liouville problem. Starting from the latter we derive dressing rules for scalar fields of arbitrary mass with auxiliary propagators in the form of Euler integrals that are accessible to flat space diagrammatic tools.

</details>


### [142] [Exact Infrared Triangle in Gravity with Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15921)
*Sangmin Choi,Ameya Kadhe,Prahar Mitra,Andrea Puhm*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究爱因斯坦引力与无质量物质耦合系统在红外区的普适性，揭示长程相互作用下新型超旋转荷守恒等价于经典对数软引力子定理的质量零极限，并提出其对应位移记忆效应的尾部公式；结果在引力耦合常数下严格成立，表明长程引力dressings可重求和；此外还给出了超旋转荷的高阶修正（引力耦合精确），对应Cachazo-Strominger次领头软引力子定理的1圈精确修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论中红外结构、软定理与渐近对称性之间的深层联系，特别是无质量物质存在下长程引力效应导致的新守恒律与记忆效应。

Method: 基于渐近对称性分析、软引力子定理的红外极限重构、以及引力dressings的严格重求和技术，结合经典极限下的软因子匹配与电荷定义推广。

Result: 1) 新型超旋转荷守恒等价于质量趋于零时的对数软引力子定理；2) 提出该守恒律对应的位移记忆尾部解析表达式；3) 给出超旋转荷的高阶引力耦合精确修正，对应Cachazo-Strominger次领头软定理的1圈精确形式。

Conclusion: 无质量物质耦合的爱因斯坦引力在红外具有新普适结构：超旋转对称性、对数软定理与记忆尾部三者严格统一；长程引力dressings可被非微扰重求和，且软定理的量子修正可通过渐近电荷精确刻画。

Abstract: Einstein gravity coupled to massless matter exhibits universality in the infrared. In the presence of long-range interactions we show that a novel superrotation charge conservation is equivalent to the massless limit of the classical logarithmic soft graviton theorem, and we propose a formula for the corresponding tail to the displacement memory. These results, which extend earlier work on gravity coupled to massive matter in a qualitatively different way, are exact in the gravitational coupling and reveal that long-range gravitational dressings resum. We also compute higher-order corrections to the known superrotation charge which are exact in the gravitational coupling and would correspond to a 1-loop exact correction to the Cachazo-Strominger subleading soft graviton theorem.

</details>


### [143] [Imaging the two-sided black hole interior with four-point correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15933)
*Joydeep Chakravarty*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用波包涂抹的边界四点函数，用于探测双侧AdS黑洞视界后的局域物理；通过高频波包沿测地线传播并在内部散射，结合非平凡时间序与半热圆上状态依赖算符，实现类平直空间散射振幅的因子化；该观测量还能成像黑洞内部的非平凡结构（如零能冲击波、物质壳层），表现为信号随 bulk 点穿越时的跃变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部时空结构及信息如何编码于边界理论中，特别是探测经典不可达区域（视界后）的局域物理。

Method: 构造边界四点函数并用高斯型波包涂抹；采用非平凡时间序（chronological ordering）与一个位于半热圆（β/2）处的状态依赖算符插入；利用高频极限下波包沿测地线传播并局域散射的几何光学近似，导出类平直空间散射振幅的因子化形式。

Result: 得到一个可观测的 smeared 四点函数，其在高频极限下对视界后 bulk 点具有局域响应；当 bulk 探针穿越 null shock 或 matter shell 时，信号出现可解析的跃变，从而实现对黑洞内部非平凡几何/物质结构的成像。

Conclusion: 该波包涂抹四点函数构成一种有效的‘黑洞内部探针’，将 AdS/CFT 中的非局域边界关联与 bulk 局域散射物理联系起来，并为状态依赖性、内部时空观测及火墙/信息悖论相关问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We propose a boundary four-point function smeared with wavepackets, which probes local physics behind the horizon of a two-sided AdS black hole. At high frequencies, boundary wavepackets aimed at an interior bulk point travel along geodesics and scatter locally about the bulk point, factorizing the correlator into a flat-space like scattering amplitude. To obtain this configuration, we utilize a non-trivial time ordering of operators, and also use one state-dependent operator insertion moved halfway across the thermal circle. An interesting property of this observable is that it also probes features of nontrivial states: null shocks and matter shells behind the horizon show up as jumps in the observed signal as the bulk point crosses them, thereby imaging the black hole interior.

</details>


### [144] [Self-Force and Bremsstrahlung in Finite Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15993)
*Kyungsun Lee,Piljin Yi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性电动力学（以Born-Infeld理论为原型）中运动点电荷的自作用力与轫致辐射，独立计算了局域自作用力和全局辐射耗散，并验证二者的一致性；指出尽管远场恢复线性，非线性仍通过运动电荷的有效多极矩影响物理行为，从而偏离Maxwell理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清非线性电动力学中运动点电荷自作用力与辐射耗散之间的内在一致性，拓展经典辐射阻尼问题至Born-Infeld等紫外完好的理论框架，并厘清文献中两种自作用力表述（局域vs. 全局/能量动量守恒）的联系。

Method: 采用协变形式体系，对Born-Infeld场方程进行微扰展开，分离出点电荷的有限自场与自能；分别基于世界线上的局域应力-能量张量积分（局部自作用力）和无穷远辐射通量（全局辐射功率）计算自作用效应；引入有效多极展开以刻画非线性对运动电荷源结构的修正。

Result: 在Born-Infeld理论中，局域自作用力与由辐射反冲导出的全局耗散率严格一致；非线性虽不改变渐近辐射场的Maxwell形式，但通过修正电荷的有效偶极矩、四极矩等，导致自作用力出现O(b⁻²)量级的可观测修正（b为BI参数）；两种文献中常见的自作用力定义（Abraham-Lorentz型vs. Gralla-Wald型）在此框架下自然统一。

Conclusion: Born-Infeld理论中的经典自作用问题具有内在自洽性，其非线性效应虽不破坏辐射场的渐近Maxwell结构，却通过有效源项留下可测量印记；该结果为构建非线性电动力学中的自洽辐射反应理论及后续量子化方案提供了关键经典基准。

Abstract: We study the self-force and the accompanying Bremsstrahlung for a moving point charge in nonlinear electrodynamics with finite self-field and self-energy. The self-force in the Maxwell theory has been the subject of many studies, often by renowned physicists. After reviewing Coleman's lucid treatise for Maxwell among them, we wish to explore the same question in the Born--Infeld theory, our prototype. We independently compute the local self-force and the global radiative dissipation, and show how the two are mutually consistent. Despite the nonlinearity turning off for the asymptotic radiative field, its residual effect survives via the effective multipole moments of a moving charge, allowing deviation from Maxwell. We close with a detailed discussion about two different manifestations of the self-force in the literature and how these enter our computation. One of the appendices explores Born--Infeld ModMax as a generalization.

</details>


### [145] [Silent Heavy Fields: An Exclusion Rule for the Cosmological Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16029)
*Shuntaro Aoki,Zhehan Qin,Masahide Yamaguchi,Yuhang Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了宇宙学对撞机信号消失的排除规则，适用于两类满足de Sitter不变性且单点耦合的重场图；通过谱分解与场重定义，证明该规则在所有圈阶交换图中成立，并推广至一类单圈多边形图；指出此前部分近似信号需重新评估，并首次给出受化学势启发玩具模型中三角圈的主导振荡信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学对撞机虽能通过原初关联函数中的振荡特征探测暴胀时期的重场，但存在‘盲区’——某些重场虽在时空中演化，却不产生可观测的关联函数信号。本文旨在系统刻画此类信号消失的条件。

Method: 采用de Sitter不变重场扇区的谱分解，结合协变耦合结构下的场重定义，严格证明信号消失规则对任意圈阶交换图成立；再通过振荡行为匹配，将结论延拓至一类单圈多边形图（如三角圈）。

Result: 确立了两类图类中宇宙学对撞机信号严格为零的排除规则；指出此前文献中若干被宣称的近似信号实属无效，须修正；并首次解析计算出化学势驱动玩具模型中三角圈图的非零主导振荡信号（~cos(2μ log k)型）。

Conclusion: 宇宙学对撞机并非对所有重场动力学敏感；信号是否出现取决于重场扇区的对称性、耦合张量结构及外腿构型；本工作为高自旋/大质量场的可观测性设定了严格的理论边界，并提供了构造非零信号的新范式。

Abstract: The cosmological collider uses oscillatory signatures in primordial correlators to probe heavy fields during inflation. This probe has blind spots, as heavy fields can keep ticking in time while remaining silent in the correlators. In this letter, we derive an exclusion rule under which cosmological collider signals vanish. The rule covers two classes of diagrams where a de Sitter invariant heavy sector couples covariantly to a single external field at one vertex. We first prove it for exchanges mediated by the two-point function of the heavy sector to all loop orders, using a spectral decomposition followed by a field redefinition. The result then extends to one-loop polygons by matching their oscillatory contributions onto those of the first class. The approximate signals of previously studied processes that fall into these classes must therefore be revisited. As a nonvanishing example, we derive the leading signal of a triangle loop for the first time in a toy model motivated by chemical potentials.

</details>


### [146] [Holographic Counterterms from Restricted Weyl Symmetry in Even-Dimensional Einstein-AdS Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16187)
*Giorgos Anastasiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无穷小体空间Weyl变换的对称性驱动重整化方法，用于偶数维Einstein-AdS引力；通过要求Einstein-Hilbert作用量在受限Weyl变换下的变分为边界有限项，导出包含Euler密度与协变全导数项的bulk-covariant补全形式；该补全具有以移位Laplacian为因子的闭式解，适用于任意偶数维，并在Einstein背景上与ambient构造的共形不变量一致；所得renormalized-volume恒等式保证了任意偶数维Euclidean AlAdS几何的壳上有限性，并成功复现4/6/8维结果，给出显式的10维补全（含至□³项），且在8/10维非共形平坦Einstein视界AdS黑洞中给出有限壳上作用量，与全息重整一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决偶数维Einstein-AdS引力中紫外发散问题，建立不依赖坐标系和背景展开、具明确对称性基础（渐近Weyl不变性）的协变重整化方案，统一已知低维结果并推广至高维。

Method: 基于保持渐近场方程的体空间无穷小Weyl变换Σ(X)，要求Einstein-Hilbert密度的变分在径向截断去除极限下仅产生积分有限的边界项；由此导出bulk-covariant补全项——Euler密度与一阶至高阶协变全导数；利用递归关系获得以移位Laplacian □+α_i 为因子的闭式解；在Einstein背景上比对ambient构造的共形不变量以定标；验证renormalized-volume恒等式及壳上有限性。

Result: 得到任意偶数维d=2n的Einstein-AdS引力的显式协变重整化作用量：含Euler密度E_n与∑_{k=1}^{n−1} c_k ∇·J^{(k)}（J^{(k)}为k阶协变导数张量），其系数由移位Laplacian谱完全确定；d=10时显式含□³项；在任意偶数维AlAdS Einstein几何上实现壳上作用量有限；在8/10维非共形平坦Einstein视界AdS黑洞中给出有限壳上值，与Holographic Renormalization一致。

Conclusion: 该对称性驱动的Weyl协变重整化方案是偶数维Einstein-AdS引力的普适、协变、可计算框架，将共形几何（ambient construction, Euler密度）、谱几何（移位Laplacian）与全息重整统一于同一原理之下，为高维AdS/CFT中的量子效应与熵计算提供了严格作用量基础。

Abstract: We develop a symmetry-based approach to renormalization for even-dimensional Einstein-AdS gravity based on infinitesimal bulk Weyl transformations that preserve its field equations asymptotically. We determine a bulk-covariant completion of the Einstein-Hilbert density by requiring its variation under restricted Weyl transformations with parameter $\Sigma(X)$ to vanish modulo terms whose integrals remain finite as the radial cutoff is removed. This requirement induces two types of compensating terms with fixed couplings: the Euler density and a hierarchy of covariant total derivatives. The resulting recursive relations admit a factorized solution in terms of shifted Laplacians, yielding a closed-form completion in arbitrary even bulk dimensions. On Einstein backgrounds, these expressions agree, after matching normalizations, with those obtained through the ambient construction of conformal invariants. The associated renormalized-volume identity establishes on-shell finiteness for Euclidean AlAdS Einstein geometries in arbitrary even dimensions. The prescription reproduces the known conformally renormalized actions in four, six and eight dimensions and provides an explicit ten-dimensional completion involving powers of the Laplacian up to $\Box^3$. Applications to eight- and ten-dimensional AdS black holes with non-conformally-flat Einstein horizons yield finite on-shell actions in agreement with Holographic Renormalization.

</details>


### [147] [Thermodynamics within and across AdS Gauss--Bonnet holographic CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16364)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维AdS Gauss-Bonnet引力中全息共形场论（CFT）内部及不同异常系数CFT之间的热力学竞争，推导了与无量纲耦合共轭的功项，利用边界第一定律和统一边界时钟比较固定外源下的带电球对称鞍点；发现随外源变化，在耦合区间端点间发生一级相变；在单位lapse圆柱时钟与零型-D真空能下，固定半径的严格凹性将全局比较约化为仅需比较两个端点；优选耦合在共存线上发生跃变，其斜率由广义Clapeyron关系关联熵差；当Einstein端点自身存在小-大黑洞共存时，第三个鞍点介入导致跨理论边界的拐点；外部控制参数的补偿性调节可在有限能量偏移下保持鞍点序；对固定CFT，确定了中性Hawking-Page线与带电小-大黑洞共存面；临界电荷随耦合趋于零，熵隙以1/2指数闭合，比热容在临界电荷下沿温度扫描以2/3指数发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维全息引力中不同CFT间的热力学选择机制，特别是Gauss-Bonnet耦合作为可调参数如何影响相结构、共存行为与临界现象，从而揭示全息对偶中量子反常与热力学稳定性之间的深层联系。

Method: 基于五维AdS Gauss-Bonnet引力中的全息对偶框架，运用边界第一定律导出与无量纲GB耦合α共轭的热力学功项；采用单位lapse圆柱坐标系下的共形边界时钟实现跨理论热力学比较；系统分析带电球对称AdS黑洞解（包括小/大黑洞、热AdS相）的自由能竞争；通过自由能差、熵差与比热容的渐近行为提取临界指数与广义Clapeyron关系。

Result: 1) 发现跨CFT热力学竞争受广义Clapeyron关系约束，共存线斜率正比于熵差；2) Einstein极限（α=0）与GB极限（α≠0）间存在一级相变，且当Einstein端点自身具小-大黑洞共存时引入第三鞍点并导致边界kink；3) 固定CFT内，临界电荷Q_c(α)→0（α→α_max），熵隙ΔS ∝ |T−T_c|^{1/2}，比热C_Q ∝ |T−T_c|^{-2/3}；4) 外部源的补偿性调节可维持鞍点自由能序不变。

Conclusion: Gauss-Bonnet耦合α作为全息CFT的变形参数，不仅调控单个理论内的黑洞相变临界行为（标度律与临界指数），更在多理论空间中诱导结构化的热力学选择景观：共存线由广义Clapeyron关系刻画，跨理论相变本质是自由能凸包重构，而临界现象的普适性（1/2与2/3指数）暗示其属于特定重正化群不动点支配的二维边界CFT临界类。

Abstract: We study thermal competition within individual holographic conformal field theories (CFTs) and across theories with different anomaly coefficients in five-dimensional AdS Gauss--Bonnet gravity. We derive the work term conjugate to the dimensionless coupling from the boundary first law and use a common boundary clock to compare charged spherical saddles at fixed external source. Varying this source drives a first-order switch between the CFTs at the endpoints of the chosen coupling interval. In the unit-lapse cylinder clock with the zero-type-D vacuum energy, strict concavity at fixed radius reduces the global comparison to these two endpoints. The preferred coupling jumps across the coexistence line, whose slope is related to the entropy difference by a generalized Clapeyron relation. Where the Einstein endpoint itself undergoes small--large coexistence, a third saddle joins the competition and produces a kink in the cross-theory boundary. A compensating change of the external control preserves saddle ordering under finite energy shifts. Within fixed CFTs, we determine the neutral Hawking--Page line and the charged small--large black hole coexistence surface. The critical charge tends to zero at a finite coupling. The entropy gap closes with exponent one half, and the heat capacity diverges with exponent two thirds along a temperature sweep at the critical charge.

</details>


### [148] [Holographic viscous transport in scalar-coupled Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16936)
*Tao Ma,Song He,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过全息对偶方法，在五维Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet模型中，直接从应力张量推迟关联子计算剪切与体粘滞系数，并与基于视界公式的预测进行对比；结果表明：对于常数和标量依赖的Gauss-Bonnet耦合，视界守恒流方法在剪切通道精确复现边界Kubo结果；体粘滞比ζ/s的峰值结构在微扰展开下略有偏差，但未修正的Eling-Oz公式（含1/(4π)因子）仍精确再现边界结果，验证了其在非平凡标量耦合下的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验视界公式（特别是Eling-Oz公式）在非平凡引力背景（含标量场依赖的Gauss-Bonnet耦合）下对η/s和ζ/s的预测可靠性，并为视界处方提供边界可计算的基准。

Method: 在匹配格点QCD物态方程的5D Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet引力模型中，通过UV有限的重整化推迟应力张量关联子（含曲率与标量反项）直接计算η和ζ；求解完整α依赖的涨落方程，不依赖视界守恒律或熵产公式；对比视界守恒流法与边界Kubo公式的输出。

Result: 1) 常数α下，视界守恒流法精确复现边界η/s；2) 标量依赖α下，两种方法均发现η/s存在极小值；3) 体粘滞通道中，一阶α截断的守恒流法给出ζ/s峰值高度偏低但位置不变；4) 未修正的Eling-Oz公式ζ/s=(4π)⁻¹(∂φₕ/∂ln s)²精确复现边界ζ/s，且1/(4π)因子在α≠0时仍有效。

Conclusion: Eling-Oz公式在标量场依赖的高曲率引力背景下依然严格成立，1/(4π)前因子具有鲁棒性；视界处方（尤其守恒流法）在剪切通道具普适精度，体通道需更高阶修正；该工作为全息输运系数的视界-边界对应提供了首个直接数值基准。

Abstract: We test horizon formulas for the shear and bulk viscosities of a holographic plasma against viscosities extracted directly from retarded stress-tensor correlators. We work in a five-dimensional Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet model calibrated to the lattice QCD equation of state at zero baryon density. UV-finite renormalized correlators are obtained by including the required curvature and scalar counterterms, and the fluctuation equations are solved while retaining the full dependence on the Gauss-Bonnet coupling $\alpha$, without using a horizon conservation law or an entropy-production formula. For constant coupling, the horizon conserved-current calculation reproduces the boundary Kubo results in both channels.
