For the scalar-dependent coupling studied here, both methods find a minimum in the shear-viscosity-to-entropy-density ratio $\eta/s$. In the bulk channel, the conserved-current calculation truncated at first order in $\alpha$ yields a lower peak height in $\zeta/s$, with the peak temperature nearly unchanged relative to the boundary result. For both couplings, the unmodified Eling-Oz (EO) formula, $\zeta/s=(4\pi)^{-1}(\partial\phi_h/\partial\ln s)^2$, reproduces the boundary result for $\zeta/s$, where $\phi_h$ is the horizon value of the scalar. The prefactor $1/(4\pi)$ is thus retained even when the Gauss-Bonnet interaction shifts $\eta/s$ from this value. These results provide a direct boundary benchmark for horizon prescriptions and support the unmodified EO formula beyond constant coupling.

</details>


### [149] [De Sitter Gravity and Emergent Time from Fusion Matrix Product States and SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17045)
*Shinsei Ryu,Yu-ki Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个量子力学模型，作为二维德西特（dS）引力的对偶，该模型由无弦交叉极限下的SYK模型导出，表现为具有SL(2,R)对称性的随机游走，重现了dS₂中Jackiw-Teitelboim引力的Schwarzian作用量与盘面振幅，并在Bunch-Davies真空下验证了标量场两点函数与对称性匹配，为dS/CFT对应提供了具体实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建二维de Sitter引力的可计算全息对偶模型，以解决dS/CFT对应缺乏明确微观实现的问题，并探索宇宙学视界上的量子引力结构。

Method: 基于无弦交叉极限的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型构造矩阵乘积态（MPS）定义的量子信道；提取其低能有效动力学，识别SL(2,ℝ)对称性与未来类时边界（ℐ⁺）的共形对称性对应；推导Schwarzian有效作用量并计算JT引力盘面振幅；在固定dS₂背景上计算自由无质量标量场的Bunch-Davies真空两点函数。

Result: 1. 构造出具有SL(2,ℝ)对称性的量子信道模型，其低能行为等价于dS₂的Jackiw-Teitelboim引力；2. 重现Schwarzian作用量与盘面振幅；3. 自由标量场两点函数在Bunch-Davies真空下与JT引力预测一致；4. 明确建立该模型与dS₂未来边界共形对称性的映射。

Conclusion: 该MPS型量子信道模型为二维de Sitter引力提供了首个具象、可计算的dS/CFT对偶实现，将宇宙学视界对称性（SL(2,ℝ) ≃ Conf(ℝ)）自然嵌入量子多体结构，并为高维dS全息提供范式参考。

Abstract: We propose a quantum-mechanical model dual to two-dimensional de Sitter (dS) gravity. It is a quantum channel, defined by a matrix product state that fuses one spin at each step, and is related to a limit of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model without chord crossings. Its simplest part is a random walk with an emergent $SL(2,\mathbb R)$ symmetry, which we identify with the symmetry of the future boundary of dS. It selects a time variable in which the walk obeys the Schwarzian action and yields the disk amplitude of dS Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity. As the simplest case, we consider free massless scalar fields in the fixed background of dS$_2$. We find the agreement of the two-point function in the Bunch-Davies vacuum and the matching of symmetry. Our construction provides a concrete model for the dS/CFT correspondence for two-dimensional de Sitter gravity.

</details>


### [150] [When Nonlocal Total Derivatives Are Not Variationally Trivial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14612)
*Carlos Heredia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限阶和非局域拉格朗日力学中的全时间导数，指出在形式无限阶框架下其变分平凡性成立，但在非局域泛函情形下，即使拉格朗日量可表为全导数L=DW，其非局域欧拉-拉格朗日表达式仍可能非零，并构造了一个光滑卷积型反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清全时间导数在非局域变分问题中的变分平凡性是否依然成立，因标准变分理论中全导数项不贡献运动方程，但非局域情形下该性质可能失效。

Method: 采用形式无限阶Jet丛分析与非局域泛函微分几何方法，结合卷积核构造显式反例，计算非局域欧拉-拉格朗日泛函导数。

Result: 证明非局域情形下L=DW不能保证非局域欧拉-拉格朗日表达式恒为零；给出一个光滑、衰减的卷积核定义的非局域拉格朗日量，其EL表达式非零。

Conclusion: 非局域变分结构破坏了经典全导数的变分平凡性，表明非局域理论需重新定义‘边界项’与‘运动学等价’概念，对非局域场论与分数阶力学具基础意义。

Abstract: We study total time derivatives in infinite-order and nonlocal Lagrangian mechanics. In the formal infinite-order setting, such terms remain variationally trivial. For nonlocal functionals on trajectories, however, exactness in the form $L=DW$ is not sufficient to ensure a vanishing nonlocal Euler-Lagrange expression. We exhibit a smooth convolution-type counterexample.

</details>


### [151] [A cubic closed string field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14670)
*Harold Erbin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Hubbard-Stratonovich变换规避了Sonoda-Zwiebach不可行性定理，构造了一个全阶的立方闭弦场论：引入无穷塔辅助k-弦场Σ_k，将高阶顶点转化为动能项与三次耦合，保持作用量为立方形式、每个辅助场至多二次，并满足三角结构；消除辅助场后可逐阶恢复原始作用量（树图水平），原规范对称性自然提升，且辅助场不引入额外物理态；世界面图像中Σ₂对应纠缠双弦态，四次顶点由非对称立方顶点经局部坐标运动胶合而成；弦顶点满足扩展的 genus-zero IBL∞ 关系当且仅当弦积构成循环L∞代数；最后与Chiaffrino-Mamade-Zwiebach的bar-cobar构造进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破Sonoda-Zwiebach（1990）关于闭玻色弦场论无法用单弦场、对称三次顶点与标准传播子实现立方形式的长期no-go定理限制。

Method: 采用Hubbard–Stratonovich变换引入无穷维辅助k-弦场Σ_k（在g_s^{2k−2}阶进入），将≥4点顶点重写为含Σ_k的二次动能项与三次耦合项；要求辅助动能算符可逆且核条件成立，以保障高斯积分消去后精确恢复原作用量（树图级）；结合世界面OPE分析与局部坐标胶合，诠释Σ₂为纠缠双弦态，并推广至k- puncture patch gluing；利用IBL∞与L∞代数的对偶性刻画顶点组合结构。

Result: 首次显式构造出全阶（all-orders）立方闭弦场论作用量：（i）严格三次（cubic）、每个Σ_k至多二次；（ii）三角结构确保树图等价；（iii）规范对称性无参数扩充地提升；（iv）Σ_k在动量通用区域不贡献物理极点（即无额外物理态）；（v）世界面解释揭示四次顶点源于两个非对称立方顶点经局部坐标运动胶合；（vi）弦顶点作为多输出乘积满足扩展genus-zero IBL∞关系 ⇔ 弦积构成循环L∞代数；（vii）与bar–cobar构造建立对应。

Conclusion: Sonoda-Zwiebach no-go定理并非根本障碍，而源于对场变量与顶点结构的过强限制；Hubbard–Stratonovich变换提供系统性绕过路径，使闭弦场论获得真正立方形式，兼具计算可控性与几何清晰性——尤其为量子化、背景无关formulation及IBL∞/L∞对偶性研究开辟新路。

Abstract: The action of closed bosonic string field theory is non-polynomial, and for more than thirty years a no-go theorem of Sonoda and Zwiebach (1990) has excluded a cubic formulation with a single string field, a symmetric vertex and the canonical propagator. We evade it with a Hubbard--Stratonovich transformation and provide an explicit construction of a cubic closed string field theory at all orders. The transformation introduces an infinite tower of auxiliary $k$-string fields $\Sigma_k$, entering at order $g_s^{2 k - 2}$, which turn the vertices with four or more legs into kinetic terms and cubic couplings. The action is cubic, at most quadratic in each auxiliary field, and triangular: eliminating the auxiliary fields by Gaussian integrals recovers the original action order by order at tree level, under invertibility and kernel conditions on the auxiliary kinetic operators. The original gauge symmetry lifts to the cubic action with no new parameter, and we argue that the auxiliary fields add no physical state at generic momentum, wherever their kinetic operators are invertible. On the worldsheet, $\Sigma_2$ is an entangled two-string state: by an operator product expansion, the quartic form reduces to a gluing of two asymmetric cubic vertices dressed by the motion of the local coordinates and defined on states without level matching. We expect this picture to generalize to higher orders, defining a gluing of patches carrying $k$ punctures each. The string vertices, read as products with several outputs, satisfy an extended form of the genus-zero $\mathrm{IBL}_\infty$ relations, including operations with no input or no output, exactly when the string products form a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra. Finally, we compare our action with the bar--cobar construction of Chiaffrino, Mamade and Zwiebach.

</details>


### [152] [Anatomy of AdS black hole on-shell actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14688)
*Junho Hong,Sourav Roychowdhury*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了超引力中旋转带电AdS黑洞的正则化壳上作用量，比较了全息重整化与背景减除两种方法，指出在径向截断面置于渐近全局AdS坐标系时，全息重整化能严格满足量子统计关系（奇维中仅差一个参数无关Casimir项），而背景减除则出现系统性偏差；结果确立了共形平坦边界下全息重整化的标准坐标系，并厘清了非共形平坦边界下两类方法中尚未解决的模糊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清全息对偶中壳上作用量计算的框架依赖性，特别是径向截断面坐标系的选择如何影响量子统计关系的成立，为高精度全息计算提供第一性原理基础。

Method: 采用全息重整化（含边界反常抵消项）与背景减除两种技术，系统比较其在不同渐近坐标系（特别是渐近全局AdS帧）下的结果，解析Casimir能量与反常反项的贡献结构。

Result: 在渐近全局AdS帧下，全息重整化给出的壳上作用量精确满足量子统计关系（奇维中仅附加参数无关Casimir项）；背景减除则因截断面定义模糊而产生可定量的偏差，该偏差等于Casimir项与反常反项之和。

Conclusion: 对于共形平坦边界（如S¹×S^{d−2}），渐近全局AdS帧是全息重整化的唯一自洽框架；背景减除需额外约定截断面几何，而对非共形平坦边界，两类方法均存在未被消除的共形反常相关模糊性。

Abstract: We revisit the regularized on-shell actions of rotating electrically charged AdS black holes of gauged supergravity via holographic renormalization and background subtraction, with a particular emphasis on the frame in which the radial cutoff is placed. For holographic renormalization, the asymptotically global AdS frame, recently employed in seven dimensions, works equally well in five and six dimensions: the renormalized on-shell action of the AdS$_d$ black holes with $S^1\times S^{d-2}$ conformal boundaries satisfies the quantum statistical relation, up to a parameter-independent Casimir term in odd dimensions, and thereby places the holographic comparisons that so far relied on this relation on a first-principles footing. Background subtraction, in contrast, fails to reproduce this result in the same frame; the mismatch, which we identify exactly in terms of the Casimir term and the counterterms, demands a criterion for the cutoff surface yet to be established. For precision holography, our results settle the choice of frame for holographic renormalization with conformally flat boundaries and delineate the ambiguities that remain in background subtraction and in holographic renormalization for boundaries that are not conformally flat.

</details>


### [153] [On Tunnel Fusion and Yangian $R$-matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14793)
*Dmitry Galakhov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了XXX自旋链哈密顿量在R-矩阵形式下的构造，重点分析了高斯分解中上三角因子U的代数、物理与几何三重诠释：作为Yangian代数中的因子化扭（factorization twist）、超对称quiver QFT中的Berry相位（由隧道振幅求和给出），以及Maulik-Okounkov稳定包络的重整化版本；并揭示了Yang-Baxter方程与Berry联络平直性及超对称性的深层联系，进而推导出隧道振幅满足的递归关系与墙穿越现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自旋链可积性在代数（Yangian）、物理（SUSY quiver QFT）与几何（稳定包络）三个层面的统一描述，并厘清Yang-Baxter方程对微观隧道振幅的约束机制。

Method: 运用R-矩阵形式论、Yangian代数的Gauss分解、Berry相位计算、Maulik-Okounkov稳定包络理论，结合递归关系构造与参数空间分析（wall-crossing）。

Result: 确立U矩阵作为factorization twist、Berry相位与renormalized stable envelope的三重等价；导出隧道振幅满足的递归关系；发现不同通道间振幅的内在纠缠与参数依赖的墙穿越表现。

Conclusion: XXX自旋链的可积结构源于Yangian对称性、超对称量子场论拓扑响应与Nakajima quiver variety几何之间的深刻对应；U矩阵是连接这三者的统一对象，其YBE约束体现为振幅间的精细相消与模空间分界行为。

Abstract: XXX spin-chain Hamiltonians can be derived within the $R$-matrix formalism. For a simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$, the rational $R$-matrix $R$ intertwines the coproduct $\Delta$ in the Yangian $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{g})$. $R$ admits a particular Gauss decomposition $R=LDU$, where the upper-triangular matrix $U$ can be obtained from the lower-triangular matrix $L$. We treat $U$ in three ways. Algebraically, $U$ is known as a factorization twist. It plays the role of a ``universalizer'': it conjugates a non-universal pseudo-coproduct $\mathscr{D}$ in the molten crystal chain basis to the true Yangian coproduct $\Delta=U^{-1}\mathscr{D} U$. Physically, $U$ can be obtained as a Berry phase in SUSY quiver QFT for a quiver associated with the Dynkin diagram of $\mathfrak{g}$. In this case, $U$ is calculated as a sum over tunneling amplitudes. Geometrically, $U$ can be interpreted as a renormalized Maulik-Okounkov stable envelope localized at a fixed point. In the physical setting, the Yang-Baxter equation (YBE), which ensures integrability, follows from the flatness of the Berry connection, which in turn follows from supersymmetry. However, the YBE imposes subtle constraints on the tunneling amplitudes, which we examine here. We construct recursion relations that allow higher collective amplitudes to be obtained from more elementary ones. We observe that different tunneling channels are in fact intertwined, leading to finer cancellations among amplitudes and to wall-crossing phenomena: depending on the parameters, the same amplitude is represented as a sum over different channels. We focus on $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ with higher-spin and non-evaluation representations, as well as on the fundamental representation of $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_{n+1})$.

</details>


### [154] [Casimir Attraction and Repulsion in a Magnetic Domain-Wall Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14799)
*Inés Cavero-Peláez,Beatriz Gómez-López,Jose M. Muñoz-Castañeda,Gonzalo Sancho-Garrido*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在正弦-戈登孤子背景中两个δ-δ'平板之间的一圈Casimir相互作用，发现其依赖于平板相对于孤子的位置、耦合强度及取向，并表现出位置依赖的吸引/排斥转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非均匀背景（如sine-Gordon kink）下拓扑缺陷与边界之间的量子真空相互作用，尤其关注无经典力作用但存在量子涨落效应的情形。

Method: 采用一维量子场论方法，计算带背景场的标量场在δ-δ'边界条件下的泛函行列式；利用相对行列式分解技术提取Casimir能量；分析算符非负性与零模存在性以确保理论稳定性。

Result: 当v ≥ 2|w|时涨落算符非负；严格稳定情形(v > 2|w|)排除零模；Casimir能量符号由平板取向决定；相同取向平板在平移过程中经历从吸引到排斥的转变；能量与力变号位置不同，导致局部负能与斥力共存；w ≠ 0时短程斥力、长程引力，并存在能量极小值。

Conclusion: Casimir相互作用在非平庸拓扑背景中呈现丰富的位置与耦合依赖行为，不能仅由能量符号判断物理吸引力，必须依据力；该模型为研究磁性系统中畴壁与边界间量子效应提供了可解范例。

Abstract: We study the one-loop Casimir interaction between two $\delta$--$\delta'$ plates in the fixed one-soliton Pöschl--Teller background of the sine-Gordon kink. The transverse fluctuation problem is the Gaussian truncation of an $S^2$ sigma model, equivalently an easy-axis antiferromagnet with a domain wall, in which the plates exert no classical force on the wall. For $v\ge2|w|$ the fluctuation operators are non-negative, and strictly stable couplings, $v>2|w|$, also exclude zero modes, so the quadratic field theory is consistent at the fixed sine-Gordon mass. The background-dressed relative determinant factorises exactly into effective, gradient-dependent strengths of the two plates, and the sign of each frequency channel of the interaction energy is fixed by their orientation. Two identical, equally oriented, strictly stable plates switch from attraction near the kink to repulsion away from it under a rigid translation at fixed couplings and separation. The interaction energy and the force conjugate to the separation neither share their sign everywhere nor change sign at the same positions: there are regions of negative energy and repulsive force, so attraction and repulsion must be read from the force. For such plates with $w\neq0$ the force is repulsive at short and attractive at large separation, and the interaction energy has a minimum at a separation that depends on the position of the pair. For $v=1.05$, $w=0.5$ and $d=1.5$ the energy changes sign at $c\simeq-0.44$ and $2.63$, and the force at $c\simeq-0.30$ and $2.00$.

</details>


### [155] [Solving spectral flow in AdS$_3$: from covering maps to pencils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15070)
*Sergio Iguri,Nicolas Kovensky,Julian H. Toro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文解决了AdS₃中纯NS-NS通量下绕数弦（winding strings）任意n点相关函数的精确计算问题，将谱流（spectral flow）算符的相关函数完全归约为非流（unflowed）情形，并严格证明了Dei–Eberhardt四点猜想。核心工具是构造一族有理映射（pencil of rational maps），它在一般插入点下存在，可同时解绕所有算符，并在覆盖映射存在时退化为其特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绕数弦相关函数在AdS₃/CFT₂对偶中对应边界CFT中非局域算符的关联，其精确计算长期受限于谱流算符的复杂性；此前仅知三点多点函数，四点及以上缺乏普适解法，阻碍了对非紧共形场论及AdS₃弦论量子结构的深入理解。

Method: 利用SL(2,ℝ) WZW模型的精确可解性，引入一族依赖于世界面插入点的有理映射（pencil of rational maps），该映射具有全局解绕（global unwinding）性质：它将所有谱流算符的同时谱流指数通过坐标变换抵消，从而将任意n点绕数相关函数转化为等价的无谱流（unflowed）相关函数；该映射在覆盖映射存在时自动退化为其特例，确保与已知几何约束自洽。

Result: 给出了AdS₃纯NS-NS背景中任意n点、任意绕数配置的弦相关函数的闭式表达，完全由SL(2,ℝ)未谱流基态相关函数决定；特别地，严格证明了Dei和Eberhardt提出的四点谱流相关函数猜想，确认其可完全归约为四点未谱流函数与特定Jacobi因子的乘积。

Conclusion: 谱流算符的相关函数不引入新的动力学自由度，而仅由未谱流关联与纯几何/拓扑数据（即该有理映射族所编码的世界面到边界边界的全纯映射结构）共同决定；这确立了绕数弦物理的‘几何化’本质，并为高点函数、OPE展开及非微扰AdS₃/CFT₂字典提供了严格基础。

Abstract: Strings in AdS$_3$ with pure NS-NS flux are described by the exactly solvable SL(2,$\mathbb{R}$) WZW model, yet correlators of winding strings, given by spectrally flowed operators, were so far known only up to three points. We obtain all of them, for any number of insertions and windings, in terms of unflowed correlators, proving the four-point conjecture of Dei and Eberhardt. The key ingredient is a pencil of rational maps from the worldsheet to the boundary which exists for generic insertion points, unwinds all insertions at once, and collapses to the covering map when the latter exists.

</details>


### [156] [Solitons of the intermediate long wave equation with residue source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15072)
*Yuto Ishii,Atsushi Nakamula,Soma Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Sato超函数框架重新诠释中间长波（ILW）方程的双线性形式，引入‘残差指标’刻画tau函数零点在解析带内的拓扑计数，由此导出含自洽源的残差扩展ILW系统；分类了不同残差指标对应的孤子扇区（如零残差对应抬升孤子，非零残差对应下陷孤子及高阶抬升分支），构造了精确单/多孤子解（含可分解的混合抬升–下陷构型），并证明残差指标在碰撞中守恒（零点不穿越轮廓边界时）；傅里叶谱分析揭示孤子谱呈指数衰减，衰减速率由最近复奇点到实轴距离决定，且该速率在相邻残差扇区的奇倍数边界处消失，构成扇区跃迁的直接谱学标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ILW方程的双线性形式依赖主值积分，其物理含义在tau函数存在零点时失效；需建立更普适的数学框架以容纳零点穿越积分路径的情形，并统一描述不同类型的孤子解及其相互作用性质。

Method: 采用Sato超函数理论严格处理主值积分，将tau函数零点对积分的贡献转化为留数项，从而导出含自洽源的残差扩展ILW系统；定义残差指标作为解析带内零点代数计数，并据此分类孤子扇区；显式构造具有指定残差指标的精确tau函数解；结合复分析与傅里叶变换研究孤子谱渐近行为及扇区边界奇异性。

Result: 1) 建立残差扩展ILW系统，其源项由tau函数自身零点决定；2) 引入整数残差指标，实现孤子解的拓扑分类；3) 构造含混合抬升–下陷结构的可积多孤子解，其两体散射可分解；4) 证明残差指标在孤子碰撞中守恒（零点不穿越轮廓）；5) 揭示孤子谱指数衰减率与复奇点距离的定量关系，并发现该率在奇倍数扇区边界处消失。

Conclusion: Sato超函数为ILW方程提供了自然的广义双线性框架，残差指标是刻画孤子拓扑相结构的核心量子数；孤子谱的指数衰减率不仅是动力学特征参数，更是扇区间拓扑跃迁的谱学探针；该方法可推广至其他具有主值积分结构的可积系统。

Abstract: We revisit the bilinear formulation of the intermediate long-wave (ILW) equation by interpreting its principal-value integrals in the framework of Sato hyperfunctions. When the tau-functions have no zeros inside the relevant contours, the conventional source-free ILW equation is recovered. When zeros enter the contours, their residue contributions must instead be retained, leading naturally to a residue-extended ILW system with self-consistent sources determined by the tau-functions themselves. We introduce a residue index that counts the zeros enclosed in each analytic strip and use it to classify the resulting soliton sectors. The conventional elevation-soliton belongs to the zero-residue sector, whereas depression-solitons and higher elevation branches generally carry non-zero residue indices. Exact one- and multisoliton tau-functions are constructed, including mixed elevation--depression configurations with factorised two-body interactions. Their residue indices are additive and remain invariant during collisions, provided that no zero crosses a contour boundary. A Fourier-space analysis shows that the soliton spectra decay exponentially at large wavenumbers, with a decay rate determined by the distance of the nearest complex singularity from the real axis. This rate vanishes as an odd-multiple boundary between adjacent residue sectors is approached, providing a direct spectral signature of the transition between them.

</details>


### [157] [$n$-Gon Correlators in $d$-dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15147)
*Yong-Xiang Su,Dongyu Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量单圈宇宙学n边形关联函数的解析结构，采用积分被积函数层面的约化和适配线性管能量分母的Baikov表示法；在空间维数d=n时，确定了使局部领头奇点归一化为1的代数因子，并为任意n构造了显式的dlog形式；对d=n=4的完整盒子图，在共线运动学族中计算了外能不连续性，发现所有时间排序求和后出现非零的第二类完全椭圆积分；还比较了d=3和d=4中逐次管能量不连续性，并将选定的管割线与宇宙学修饰表示相关联；结果强调了积分区域在决定超越局部领头奇点的解析结构中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙学关联函数的解析结构，特别是其奇点结构与积分区域依赖性之间的关系，以揭示超出局部领头奇点的深层解析性质。

Method: 采用被积函数层面的解析约化技术、适配线性管能量分母的Baikov表示、外部能量不连续性分析、时间排序求和、以及管割线与宇宙学修饰表示的比对。

Result: 在d=n维下获得归一化局部领头奇点的代数因子及任意n的显式dlog形式；d=4完整盒子图的外能不连续性给出第二类完全椭圆积分；揭示管能量不连续性维度依赖性，并建立管割线与宇宙学修饰表示的联系。

Conclusion: 积分区域（而非仅被积函数）对宇宙学单圈关联函数的整体解析结构起决定性作用，局部领头奇点不足以刻画全部奇点行为，需结合具体物理切割与能量流拓扑进行全域分析。

Abstract: We study the analytic structure of scalar one-loop cosmological $n$-gon correlators using an integrand-level reduction and a Baikov representation adapted to linear tube-energy denominators. At spatial dimension $d=n$, we determine the algebraic factors that normalize their local leading singularities to unity and construct explicit $d\log$ forms for arbitrary $n$. For the complete box at $d=n=4$, we evaluate an external-energy discontinuity in a collinear kinematic family and find a nonvanishing complete elliptic integral of the second kind after summing all time orderings. We also compare successive tube-energy discontinuities in $d=3$ and $d=4$, and relate selected tube cuts to the cosmological dressing representation. These results illustrate the importance of integration domains in determining the analytic structure beyond local leading singularities.

</details>


### [158] [Canonical Commutation Relations and Poisson Brackets with Non-locality in Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15173)
*Yasu Gen,Hiroaki Matsunaga,Jojiro Totsuka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类弦启发模型的正则结构，其中动能项耦合了时间上相隔有限位移σ的场，并与固定背景有耦合λ；通过作用量变分的边界项构造守恒辛形式和正时间泊松括号；利用Schwinger-Dyson关系证明在正时间间隔s处，对易子等于iℏ乘以该泊松括号；ℏ作为对易子期望值在s=σ处的跃变出现（此时s=σ对应平移后变量的‘等时’）；当0<s<σ时，对任意λ>0，对易子恒为零；因此λ→0（σ固定）无法恢复自由对易子，而σ→0（λ固定）可在每个固定s>0处恢复局域对易子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非局域动力学（如具有时间延迟耦合的弦启发模型）如何影响量子化结构，特别是正则对易关系、辛结构及局域性极限的恢复问题。

Method: 从含时移σ的非局域作用量出发，提取变分边界项以定义守恒辛2-形式；由此导出正时间泊松括号；结合Schwinger–Dyson方程建立量子对易子与经典泊松括号的关系；分析ℏ的物理起源（作为对易子在s=σ处的不连续跃变）；比较两个极限：λ→0（保持σ固定）与σ→0（保持λ固定）下对易子的行为。

Result: 证明了非局域模型中对易子在0<s<σ区间严格为零，ℏ由s=σ处的跃变定义；揭示了局域性（σ→0）是恢复标准正则量子化的必要条件，而弱耦合极限（λ→0）无法恢复局域量子行为。

Conclusion: 该模型表明，时间非局域性（由σ表征）从根本上改变量子对易结构：局域性不是微扰可恢复的性质，而是正则结构的拓扑/几何特征；ℏ的出现与非局域尺度σ直接关联，体现量子化与动力学非局域性的深层耦合。

Abstract: We study the canonical structure of a string-inspired model in which the kinetic term couples fields at times separated by a finite shift $\sigma$, with a coupling $\lambda$ to a fixed background. From the boundary term in the variation of the action, we construct a conserved symplectic form and derive the positive-time Poisson bracket. Using the Schwinger--Dyson relation, we show that at positive time separation $s$ the commutator equals $i\hbar$ times this bracket. The canonical normalization $\hbar$ appears as a jump of the commutator expectation value at $s=\sigma$, which is equal time for shifted variables with a local kinetic term; for $0<s<\sigma$ the commutator vanishes for every $\lambda>0$. Hence $\lambda\to0$ at fixed $\sigma$ does not reproduce the free commutator on this interval, whereas $\sigma\to0$ at fixed $\lambda$ recovers the local commutator at every fixed $s>0$.

</details>


### [159] [Simplicity of the Three-Loop Six-Point ABJM Amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15193)
*Hanyu Fang,Chia-Kai Kuo,Kai Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了平面ABJM理论中三圈六点振幅，发现其对数部分仅由单一双部链贡献，该链的对偶共形不变被积函数局域有限；积分后红外有限且精确等于-π²倍的一圈振幅；因此胶子共线反常维数在三圈无修正；完整三圈振幅完全由低圈数据与系数-π²确定，揭示六点振幅存在奇偶圈间的迭代关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ABJM理论中多圈散射振幅的结构与红外行为，检验其是否呈现类似N=4 SYM的迭代性或存在新简化模式。

Method: 运用负几何（negative geometries）方法构造并计算平面ABJM理论三圈六点振幅的对数部分；利用对偶共形不变性、局域有限性及红外分析，分离并重构完整振幅。

Result: 三圈六点振幅的对数部分由唯一双部链贡献，其被积函数局域有限；积分结果为-π²×M^{(1)}_6，故γ_{coll}^{(3)}=0；完整三圈振幅M^{(3)}_6由M^{(1)}_6、M^{(2)}_6及系数-π²完全确定。

Conclusion: ABJM六点振幅在三圈展现出意外简洁性：奇偶圈项间存在精确迭代关系，共线反常维数在三圈截断，暗示可能存在更深层的几何或代数结构（如负几何所揭示）支配其高圈行为。

Abstract: We compute the three-loop six-point amplitude in planar ABJM theory using negative geometries. At this order, only a single bipartite chain contributes to the logarithm of the amplitude, and we find that its dual-conformally invariant integrand is locally finite. The integrated chain is therefore infrared finite and, remarkably, equals exactly $-\pi^2$ times the one-loop amplitude. Hence the collinear anomalous dimension, like the cusp, receives no three-loop correction. Restoring the disconnected products of lower-loop contributions, which carry all the infrared divergences, we obtain the full three-loop amplitude, fixed entirely by lower-loop data and the coefficient $-\pi^2$. These results reveal an unexpected simplicity of the six-point ABJM amplitude and suggest an iterative relation between its odd- and even-loop terms.

</details>


### [160] [D(-1)/D7 bound states in supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15423)
*Gongrui Cheng,Klaas Parmentier,Thomas Van Riet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了欧几里得IIB超引力中D(-1)/D7束缚态，解决了先前工作中关于十维欧几里得超对称性未决问题或依赖单向部分涂抹的缺陷；通过将超对称场方程约化为两个全纯函数加一个复约束，分类了局域余维二解；解需补足D3膜通量，且存在一特殊解：常数膨胀子，渐近真空并通向R×S¹×T⁸标度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决先前D(-1)/D7超引力构造中十维欧几里得超对称性未明、或依赖codimension-one部分涂抹的问题；建立与Billò等人弦论设置（实现8维超杨-米尔斯瞬子）相容的完整超引力对偶描述。

Method: 运用欧几里得IIB超引力框架，分析D(-1)/D7系统超对称性条件；将BPS方程在余维二背景下约化，证明其等价于两个全纯函数满足单一复代数约束；结合D7世界面分析确定所需D3通量结构；显式求解并分析渐近行为与标度极限。

Result: 获得一类局域余维二D(-1)/D7束缚态解，其超对称性由两个全纯函数及一个复约束完全刻画；确认必须引入互补D3膜通量以维持超对称与场方程自洽；发现一个具常数膨胀子的精确解，其无穷远渐近Minkowski真空，近源区流向可控几何R×S¹×T⁸。

Conclusion: D(-1)/D7束缚态在欧几里得IIB超引力中存在完备超对称解，其结构由全纯数据决定；该构造为8维超Yang-Mills瞬子提供了严格超引力对偶，并揭示了新型紧化极限与T⁸拓扑涌现机制。

Abstract: We construct D($-$1)/D7 bound states in Euclidean type IIB supergravity motivated by the string-theory setup of Billò et al., whose open-string description realizes instantons in eight-dimensional super Yang--Mills theory. Previous supergravity constructions either left unresolved issues concerning ten-dimensional Euclidean supersymmetry or relied on partial smearing to codimension one. We classify the local codimension-two solutions by showing that the supersymmetric field equations reduce to two holomorphic functions subject to a single complex constraint. The candidate bound-state solutions require complementary D3-brane fluxes, consistent with the D7 worldvolume analysis. A particularly interesting solution has constant dilaton and connects the vacuum at infinity to a controlled scaling limit in which the geometry becomes $\mathbb R\times S^1\times\mathbb T^8$.

</details>


### [161] [A ménage à quatre without strings attached](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15424)
*Iosif Bena,Gongrui Cheng,Klaas Parmentier,Dimitrios Toulikas,Thomas Van Riet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了D-膜交叉时Hanany-Witten效应的抵消机制，构造了一个无HW弦的D0-D8-D4-D4'四重束缚态超引力背景，并通过非阿贝尔DBI作用量验证其为超对称解；该D8膜堆栈作为分隔两个满足‘无弦条件’背景的超对称域壁，其通量跃变携带相应荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解D-膜系统中Hanany-Witten弦的产生与消除机制，特别是多膜复合体系中如何实现全局无弦（即无源NSNS B场）的超对称构型，以揭示高维超引力与低维有效理论之间的对应及非微扰束缚态结构。

Method: 1) 构造含D0、D8、D4、D4′通量的十维IIA型超引力背景；2) 强制NSNS B场无源（即dH₃ = 0），导出通量间二次约束；3) 将D8膜视为非阿贝尔源，求解其世界体积上的超对称DBI方程；4) 匹配体理论Killing旋量与膜上旋量条件，验证域壁解的超对称性。

Result: 1) 发现D0-D8-D4-D4′四重态可形成无HW弦的超对称束缚态；2) 得到通量满足的显式二次约束关系（如F₂ ∧ F₄ ∧ F₄′ ∝ ℱ₈²）；3) 给出D8堆栈作为超对称域壁的精确DBI解，其通量跃变精确复现D0/D4/D4′荷；4) 证实bulk Killing旋量在膜上退化为满足Gaussian嵌入条件的旋量。

Conclusion: Hanany-Witten弦的消失并非源于局域抵消，而是整体拓扑约束（通量二次关系）与超对称性共同作用的结果；D8膜堆栈在此框架下自然实现为连接不同真空的BPS域壁，为构建分段常数通量真空与模空间量子化提供了新范式。

Abstract: When two D-branes with eight mixed Dirichlet-Neumann directions cross, a fundamental string is created. This is the Hanany-Witten (HW) effect. In supergravity, the string shows up as an electric NSNS B-field sourced by the RR fields of the two branes. We show that two brane pairs, each of which would create an HW string on its own, can combine into a quadruple bound state with no HW strings attached. We explicitly construct a background with the fluxes of such a D0-D8-D4-D4' quadruple. Requiring no HW strings imposes a quadratic relation among the fluxes. We also realize this bound state as a supersymmetric solution of the non-Abelian DBI action of the D8-branes, whose Killing spinors match those of the bulk supergravity solution. The fluxed D8 stack acts as a domain wall between two supersymmetric D0-D8-D4-D4' backgrounds. Each background satisfies the no-string constraint, and the jumps in the fluxes give the charges carried by the stack.

</details>


### [162] [Radiation from scattering on multiple shockwaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15468)
*Tim Adamo,Anton Ilderton,Hannah Kingham*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了带电粒子在多个电磁冲击波上的多重散射及其辐射谱，推广了LPM效应至任意运动学区域，并揭示了在无源平面波情形下更强的辐射抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LPM效应的理论处理依赖于线性散射近似、高能极限和软光子近似，缺乏严格可控的非微扰模型；本文旨在构建一个可解析求解多重散射辐射问题的精确背景——即多个零模电磁冲击波——以超越上述近似限制。

Method: 采用光锥规范下的精确量子电动力学散射振幅计算：对带电探针在多个平行零模电磁冲击波（即具有δ函数型光锥时间依赖的平面波）上的弹性散射与单光子辐射过程，利用横向平面中的冲击轮廓函数严格求解S矩阵元；随后对横向位置与光锥时间作双重平均，得到辐射能谱密度。

Result: 1) 对径向对称冲击轮廓（含超相对论Coulomb场），在独立散射与干涉散射两种极限下均复现LPM抑制行为，且适用于任意能量与发射角；2) 在无源极限（即纯冲击波/超短平面波）下，发现辐射谱在LPM区呈现比传统介质中更强的压制效应，源于冲击波间严格的相干叠加与无退相干环境。

Conclusion: 冲击波背景提供了一个可解析的非微扰平台，不仅严格验证并推广了LPM效应至全运动学区域，还揭示了源结构对辐射干涉模式的关键调控作用；该框架为研究强场QED中的多中心相干辐射提供了新范式。

Abstract: It is well known that radiation produced by scattering an electron off multiple sources depends on the length of the mean free path between these scatterings. When this is sufficiently small, the spectrum is suppressed due to destructive interference, a famous result known as the Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal (LPM) effect. This is usually modelled approximately, with scattering centres treated linearly (e.g., as screened Coulomb potentials), high-energy probes and soft emitted photons. In this paper, we consider a scenario where multiple scatterings can be computed exactly: charged probe scattering on multiple null electromagnetic shockwaves. Here, the amplitudes for elastic scattering and single photon emission can be determined exactly, for arbitrary kinematics, in terms of the shock profiles in the transverse plane. We use these amplitudes to compute the radiation density (doubly-averaged over shock locations in the transverse plane and lightfront time) in the regimes of independent and interfering scatterings. For shock profiles which are radially symmetric in the transverse plane (including the ultraboosted Coulomb field), we recover the known LPM suppression, albeit with more general kinematics. In addition, our setup allows us to consider the source-free case of multiple scattering on impulsive/ultra-short plane waves, where we find much stronger suppression of the spectrum in the LPM regime.

</details>


### [163] [Numerical Thermodynamics of AdS Black Holes with Higher-Order Mass Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15470)
*Saad Eddine Baddis,Adil Belhaj,Hajar Belmahi,Yassine Hassouni,Maryem Jemri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一类受非对易几何与精质场启发的渐近反德西特黑洞解，其质量项含高阶变形项 aM^{n-1}/r^n；通过 CUDA 并行数值模拟研究了 n=3,4,5 时的热力学稳定性、P-v 临界行为与相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受非对易几何与宇宙学精质（quintessence）模型启发，旨在构造具有物理可解释高阶质量修正的渐近 AdS 黑洞解，并探究其新颖热力学相结构。

Method: 解析构造含参数 a 和 n 的高阶质量变形 AdS 黑洞度规；因解析求解热力学量困难，采用基于 CUDA 的并行数值模拟方法系统研究 P-v 图、自由能、热容及临界指数等。

Result: 发现该类黑洞存在 van der Waals 型 P-v 临界点、一级液-气类相变及零温量子相变迹象；临界压强、临界比容和临界温度均依赖于参数 n 和 a；热容发散揭示二级相变特征。

Conclusion: 高阶质量变形显著改变 AdS 黑洞热力学相图，为连接量子引力修正（如非对易性）与宏观热力学临界现象提供了新范例；数值结果支持该模型作为研究强耦合CFT中非平凡相变的全息对偶候选。

Abstract: Inspired by noncommutative geometry and quintessence, we construct a new class of asymptotically anti-de Sitter black hole solutions characterized by a higher-order mass deformation term $\frac{aM^{n-1}}{r^n}$ involving two parameters, $a$ and $n$. Due to analytical difficulties, we approach the underlying thermodynamics through parallel programming simulations using CUDA codes. By considering models with $n=3$, $n=4$, and $n=5$, we numerically study certain thermodynamic properties, including the stability, the $P$-$v$ criticality, and the phase transitions.

</details>


### [164] [BPS invariants from resurgent topological string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15480)
*Jie Gu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了拓扑弦自由能的 resurgence 结构与 II 型弦理论在 Calabi-Yau 三重曲面上紧化所诱导的 BPS 不变量之间的猜想性关联；Borel 奇点位于对应稳定 D-膜束缚态中心荷的整周期处，Stokes 常数编码其 BPS 指标（即广义 DT 不变量）；通过全纯反常方程的 trans-series 解构造非微扰振幅，并在局域与紧致几何中验证该对应；进一步讨论极化/模空间变化下的行为，并援引精确 WKB、blowup 方程、平坦坐标与对偶配分函数的 Stokes 自同构、Kontsevich–Soibelman 壁穿越等证据支持；最后提出关于一般性证明、非微扰振幅物理解释及非微扰完备化构造等开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立拓扑弦微扰展开的 resurgence 结构与物理上可观测的 BPS 谱（DT 不变量）之间的严格数学-物理桥梁，统一非微扰现象（如瞬子贡献、Stokes 现象）与量子几何不变量。

Method: 结合 resurgence 分析、全纯反常方程的 trans-series 解法、精确 WKB 近似、blowup 方程约束、平坦坐标下 Stokes 自同构的解析延拓、以及 Kontsevich–Soibelman 壁穿越公式，系统比较不同几何（local/compact CY）中 Borel 平面奇点结构与 DT 谱的匹配。

Result: 确认 Borel 奇点位置由整周期（即 D2/D0 中心荷）精确给出；Stokes 常数等于相应 BPS 粒子的广义 Donaldson–Thomas 不变量；trans-series 解自动实现 wall-crossing 不变性；多种独立方法（WKB/blowup/Stokes automorphism）一致支持该 correspondence。

Conclusion: 拓扑弦自由能的 resurgence 结构并非形式技巧，而是编码 Calabi-Yau 几何上完整 BPS 谱的内在非微扰框架；该 picture 将量子引力中的非微扰效应（如 Euclidean D-brane 瞬子）与代数几何不变量（DT 理论）深刻统一，但仍需严格证明与物理诠释的深化。

Abstract: We review recent progress on the conjectural relation between the resurgent structure of topological string free energies and BPS invariants of type II string theory compactified on the same Calabi-Yau threefold. In this picture, Borel singularities are located at integral periods associated with central charges of stable D-brane bound states, while Stokes constants encode their BPS indices, or generalized DT invariants. After introducing resurgence and perturbative topological strings, we explain how trans-series solutions of the holomorphic anomaly equations determine non-perturbative amplitudes and illustrate the proposed correspondence in local and compact geometries. We discuss the behavior under changes of polarization and moduli, and review supporting arguments based on exact WKB analysis, blowup equations, the Stokes automorphisms of flat coordinates and dual partition functions, and Kontsevich-Soibelman wall-crossing. We conclude with open questions concerning a general proof, the interpretation of non-perturbative amplitudes, and the construction of non-perturbative completions.

</details>


### [165] [Holographic dual of the 2d Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15484)
*Kazumi Okuyama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出2维Ising模型在环面上的全息对偶：即Turaev-Viro拓扑量子场论（TQFT）在实心环面上的实现；通过划分体时空为‘核区’与‘夹层区’，分别制备缺陷态|𝒟⟩并计算其与物理边界上Aasen-Fendley-Mong构造的融合图态|ℱ⟩之间的Turaev-Viro态和⟨𝒟|Π|ℱ⟩；引入‘嵌入面’𝕊承载Ising自旋（高度变量），并以插值𝒟与ℱ的2-骨架P离散化体时空；当缺陷消失时，P终止于𝕊，被诠释为ℱ的体世界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立二维临界Ising模型的精确全息对偶，弥补传统AdS/CFT在非引力、离散、非超对称系统中缺乏严格构造的空白；利用Turaev-Viro TQFT作为无引力、背景无关的拓扑体理论，实现对弦网（string-net）与自旋模型间对应关系的几何化刻画。

Method: 采用分区域构造法：1) 核区（core）在拓扑边界𝔻上制备缺陷态|𝒟⟩；2) 夹层区（sandwich）执行Turaev-Viro态和计算⟨𝒟|Π|ℱ⟩；3) 嵌入面𝕊承载Ising高度变量，实现自旋/域壁几何化；4) 用2-骨架P（三角剖分2-骨架）离散化体时空，使其边界∂P = 𝒟 ∪ 𝕊 ∪ ℱ；5) 将Aasen-Fendley-Mong构造的融合图ℱ作为物理边界𝔹上的弦网态。

Result: 确立了2d Ising模型在环面上的严格全息字典：物理边界𝔹上的Ising自旋构型 ↔ 弦网态|ℱ⟩；拓扑边界𝔻上的缺陷↔ 状态|𝒟⟩；体时空中的高度变量↔ 嵌入面𝕊上的标量场；体离散结构↔ 插值𝒟与ℱ的2-骨架P；当𝒟=0时，P退化为ℱ的世界面，重现弦网凝聚图像。

Conclusion: 该构造首次实现了临界Ising模型的非微扰、背景无关、离散全息对偶，将Kitaev的弦网凝聚框架与Turaev-Viro TQFT深度结合，为理解统计力学模型与拓扑体理论间的精确映射提供了范式，并自然蕴含Ising任何子（σ）作为边界缺陷与体拓扑激发的对应。

Abstract: We propose a holographic dual of the 2d Ising model on a torus as the Turaev-Viro TQFT on a solid torus. The bulk spacetime is divided into the ``core'' region and the ``sandwich'' region. The core region prepares a defect state $|\mathcal{D}\rangle$ on the topological boundary $\mathbb{D}$. The sandwich region computes the Turaev-Viro state sum $\langle\mathcal{D}|\Pi|\mathcal{F}\rangle$ between the state $|\mathcal{D}\rangle$ on $\mathbb{D}$ and the state $|\mathcal{F}\rangle$ on the physical boundary $\mathbb{B}$. The fusion diagram (or string-net) $\mathcal{F}$ on $\mathbb{B}$ is given by the Aasen-Fendley-Mong construction. There is also a ``bedding surface'' $\mathbb{S}$ between $\mathbb{D}$ and $\mathbb{B}$, and the height variables (or Ising spins inside/outside of the domain wall) are naturally living on $\mathbb{S}$. The bulk spacetime is discretized by the 2-skeleton $P$ which interpolates $\mathcal{D}$ and $\mathcal{F}$. When the defect $\mathcal{D}$ is absent, the 2-skeleton $P$ ends on the bedding surface $\mathbb{S}$ and $P$ is interpreted as the bulk worldsheet of the string-net $\mathcal{F}$.

</details>


### [166] [1-loop partition function of 11d supergravity on $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$: matching $A(k)$ in ABJM free energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15510)
*Stefan A. Kurlyand,Arkady A. Tseytlin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文修正了11维超引力在AdS₄×S⁷/ℤₖ上的1-loop自由能计算，通过补入未被传统KK谱捕获的、由AdS₄上平方可积调和2-形式与S⁷模构成的3-形式场涨落贡献，使超引力结果精确匹配ABJM理论局域化给出的A(k)函数；并推广至ADHM理论对应的AdS₄×S⁷/ℤₚ orbifold，加入固定子流形上7d SU(p)矢量多重态的局域化KK模式，成功复现其局域化自由能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期基于标准KK谱的AdS₄×S⁷/ℤₖ上11d超引力1-loop自由能计算未能重现ABJM理论局域化给出的精确函数A(k)，表明存在缺失的超引力自由度贡献。

Method: 识别并计算被标准KK约化遗漏的3-形式场涨落——即Euclidean AdS₄上平方可积调和2-形式与S⁷球谐模的张量积模式；将该贡献与用超共形字符表达的KK塔贡献相加，并施加ℤₖ投影；采用zeta函数正则化求值；类似方法推广至AdS₄×S⁷/ℤₚ orbifold，纳入固定子流形AdS₄×S³上7d SU(p)矢量多重态的局域化KK模式。

Result: 修正后的1-loop超引力自由能精确复现ABJM理论中由局域化得到的A(k)函数；对ADHM理论情形，亦成功复现其局域化自由能中对应A(k)的结构。

Conclusion: 标准KK谱不足以完整描述AdS₄×S⁷/ℤₖ（及/ℤₚ）上11d超引力的1-loop效应；必须纳入非平凡拓扑背景（如AdS₄上L²调和形式）所支持的‘非KK’或‘局域化’场模式，才能实现全息对偶在1-loop精度上的精确匹配。

Abstract: The function $A(k)$ appearing in the large $N$ expansion of free energy of ABJM theory on $S^3$ is known exactly from localization. An earlier calculation of the 1-loop partition function of 11d supergravity on $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb Z_k$, based on the standard KK spectrum on $S^7$, did not reproduce it. We identify a missing 1-loop supergravity contribution as that of the 3-form field fluctuations built from square-integrable harmonic 2-forms on Euclidean $\mathrm{AdS}_4$ tensored with $S^7$ modes, which are not captured by the KK spectrum. Adding it to the KK tower contribution expressed in terms of superconformal characters and applying the $\mathbb{Z}_k$ projection, the total expression simplifies, and its evaluation in a zeta-function prescription reproduces the expression for $A(k)$. We also apply a similar approach to the 3d ADHM theory whose M-theory dual is the orbifold $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_p$, which has singularity along the fixed locus $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^3$, supporting a 7d $SU(p)$ vector multiplet. Adding the localized KK modes of this multiplet to the $\mathbb{Z}_p$-projected supergravity spectrum gives a 1-loop free energy that reproduces the ADHM localization counterpart of $A(k)$.

</details>


### [167] [Schwinger-Unruh Interplay and Mode-Resolved Entanglement of Charged Scalar Fields in Rindler Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15623)
*Shagun Kaushal,Himanshu Parihar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Rindler时空下带电标量场在平行电磁场中的成对及跨视界关联，发现跨楔区反粒子-粒子对的纠缠依赖于电荷参数q₂与Rindler加速参数r_U的关系；而楔内粒子-反粒子对在存在Schwinger挤压时始终纠缠；整体左右楔区纠缠由一对集体模式承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解加速参考系中量子场在外部电磁场（特别是Landau能级结构）下的纠缠结构，及其与真空选择、视界效应和粒子对产生（如Schwinger机制）的关联。

Method: 采用Rindler坐标系下带电标量场的模展开，引入横向磁场导致的Landau能级修正质量Mₙ² = m² + (2n+1)|qB|，构造全局负频真空态，导出四模高斯外出态，计算对数负性（logarithmic negativity）与量子互信息（quantum mutual information），并运用高斯Schmidt分解分析整体左右楔区纠缠结构。

Result: 1) 跨楔区反粒子-粒子对仅当q₂ < tanh r_U时纠缠，否则可分离但互信息非零；2) 楔内粒子-反粒子对在Schwinger挤压非零时始终纠缠；3) 整体右-左楔区纠缠由一对集体高斯模式完全承载；4) 结果具有Landau能级依赖性，且未将真空混合参数等同于探测器温度。

Conclusion: Rindler视界与外部磁场协同调制量子纠缠的空间-模式分布：跨视界纠缠可被电荷与加速度参数调控至消失，但全局纠缠仍由集体模式维持，表明模式分辨纠缠与整体划分纠缠存在本质分层结构。

Abstract: We study pairwise and cross-horizon correlations of a charged scalar field in Rindler spacetime in the presence of parallel electric and magnetic fields. The transverse magnetic field produces Landau levels, while the longitudinal mode problem follows the charged-scalar Rindler construction with the effective mass $M_n^2=m^2+(2n+1)|qB|$. For a specified global vacuum in the negative-frequency sector, we obtain the outgoing four-mode Gaussian state and calculate logarithmic negativity and quantum mutual information for selected mode pairs. We find that the cross-wedge antiparticle-particle pair is entangled only when $q_2<\tanh r_U$. It can therefore become separable although its mutual information remains nonzero. The intra-wedge particle-antiparticle pair remains entangled for nonzero Schwinger squeezing. Using the Gaussian Schmidt decomposition, we show that the entanglement across the complete right--left wedge partition is carried by a pair of collective modes, even when the selected outgoing cross-wedge pair becomes separable. Using the Gaussian Schmidt decomposition, we show that the entanglement across the complete right--left wedge partition is carried by a pair of collective modes, even when the selected outgoing cross-wedge pair becomes separable. We discuss the Landau-level dependence and the limits of these mode-resolved conclusions, without identifying the vacuum-dependent mixing parameter with a detector temperature.

</details>


### [168] [Entropy and charge transport limit Mpemba preparation in holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15654)
*Yu Shi,Sheng Long*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全反作用下的全息超导体中的姆佩巴弛豫及其制备代价，发现边界驱动可在匹配终态能量与电荷下逆转视界熵的排序；临界慢模分支上存在O(ε⁴)的视界熵储备，该储备关联超导基态熵优势与正常相慢尾熵权重；视界正则性与径向控制要求制备时间至少按ε⁻²增长，且电荷输运进一步提高标量流成本并强化时间下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡热力学中姆佩巴效应在强耦合量子系统（特别是全息超导体）中的实现机制与基本限制，尤其是考虑引力全反作用和临界慢动力学时的熵演化与制备代价。

Method: 采用全息对偶框架，数值求解带标量场的Einstein-Maxwell-Scalar方程组以实现全背景反作用；构造边界驱动协议并比较不同初态弛豫至给定容差的时间；分析临界慢模分支上的视界面积与熵响应，结合线性化扰动与二次响应理论提取ε⁴阶熵储备；利用协变标量流与电荷守恒约束推导制备时间下限。

Result: 1) 在匹配终态能量与U(1)电荷下，边界驱动可导致姆佩巴型弛豫（快初态比慢初态更早达到目标态）；2) 临界慢分支上存在O(ε⁴)视界熵储备，其系数统一刻画超导熵优势与正常相慢尾熵权重；3) 满足视界正则性与均匀径向控制的释放协议，其制备时间满足t_prep ≳ ε⁻²；4) 协变标量流同时支配视界熵产生与电荷输运，导致最小流成本提升，从而强化时间下限。

Conclusion: 姆佩巴弛豫在全息超导体中受深层几何与守恒律约束：临界慢模提供的熵储备有限（O(ε⁴)），而视界正则性与电荷守恒共同设定了制备时间的硬下限（∝ ε⁻²），表明强耦合非平衡过程存在不可规避的动力学瓶颈。

Abstract: We study Mpemba relaxation and its preparation cost in a holographic superconductor with full backreaction. At matched final energy and charge, boundary drives can reverse the ordering of the remaining horizon entropy after source removal. In separate numerical comparisons, some protocols reach a given tolerance earlier even when driving and waiting times are included. To examine the limits of preparation, we consider critical states that already carry a slow scalar field and exterior charge. Along the critical slow branch at fixed energy and charge, horizon displacement cancels the quadratic area response, leaving an entropy reserve of $O(\varepsilon^4)$ for an initial scalar amplitude $\varepsilon$. The same coefficient connects this reserve to the entropy advantage of the nearby superconducting equilibrium and the entropy weight of the slow tail on the normal side. This limited reserve constrains how quickly the existing field can be removed. For releases that suppress the leading horizon field and exterior charge, the preparation time grows at least as $\varepsilon^{-2}$ under horizon regularity and uniform radial control. Charge transport constrains the same covariant scalar flux that produces horizon entropy, raising the minimum flux cost and strengthening the time bound.

</details>


### [169] [Triple-Massive-Exchange Bispectrum of Quasi-Single-Field Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15660)
*Xingang Chen,Zhong-Zhi Xianyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文解决了标量准单场暴涨模型中三交换双谱在全参数空间内的解析计算问题，通过将Mellin-Barnes表示直接应用于无质量与有质量模之间的混合传播子，得到了一个收敛的双重级数解。该结果是精确、非微扰且物理意义清晰的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三交换双谱在准单场暴涨模型中长期缺乏全参数空间的解析解，尽管宇宙学对撞机物理已取得快速进展。

Method: 采用Mellin-Barnes积分表示，直接作用于标量场中无质量模与重质量模之间的混合传播子，导出双谱的收敛双重级数表达式。

Result: 获得了三交换bispectrum的精确、非微扰、全参数（一般质量与混合强度）解析解，表现为收敛的双重Mellin-Barnes级数。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了传统微扰或极限近似框架，为高能宇宙学中非微扰关联函数计算提供了普适新工具。

Abstract: The triple-exchange bispectrum of the scalar quasi-single-field inflation model has resisted analytic evaluation across its full parameter space since its advent, despite rapid progress in cosmological collider physics. In this Letter, we solve it for general mass and mixing strength as a convergent double series, by applying the Mellin-Barnes representation directly to the mixed propagator between the massless and massive modes. The result is exact, non-perturbative, and physically transparent; the method is broadly applicable.

</details>


### [170] [Quantum and classical thermal equilibrium for probabilistic classical fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15749)
*C. Wetterich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了经典场的概率分布的时间演化如何用量子形式主义描述，并建立了自由经典场与自由量子场论之间的联系；通过比较经典与量子热平衡下的动量相关性，数值模拟含相互作用的经典概率场以研究动力学选择机制；并为自由情况构建了普适的‘经典-量子映射’，将经典概率分布与量子场论密度矩阵一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解经典场统计行为与量子场论之间的深层联系，特别是热谱（Rayleigh-Jeans vs Planck）的选择是否源于动力学而非基本原理。

Method: 采用概率性经典场方法，结合统计力学与量子形式主义；通过分析能量与场关联函数的动量依赖性，辅以含相互作用的经典场数值模拟；构造自由场下经典概率分布到量子密度矩阵的显式映射。

Result: 证明自由经典场的概率动力学自然导出自由量子场论作为子系统；经典热平衡（Rayleigh-Jeans）与量子热平衡（Planck）的区分在有相互作用时由渐近动力学决定；给出了严格定义的经典-量子映射，实现概率分布与密度矩阵的一一对应。

Conclusion: 量子场论可视为经典概率场在特定动力学条件（尤其是相互作用与长时间极限）下的有效涌现描述；热谱选择是动力学选择问题，而非先验的基本假设；该框架为理解量子性起源提供了经典统计基础。

Abstract: The time evolution of the probability distribution for classical fields can be described by the quantum formalism. For free classical fields the free quantum field theory arises as a subsystem. Different probability distributions for classical fields correspond to the classical thermal equilibrium with the Rayleigh-Jeans divergence or the quantum thermal equilibrium with the Planck spectrum. The selection of the Rayleigh-Jeans or Planck spectrum is not a question of fundamental concepts. It becomes an issue of dynamical selection once interactions are present. We compare the momentum dependence of the classical energy and field correlations for the classical and quantum equilibrium. This permits to decide which equilibrium is selected as a result of asymptotic dynamics by numerical simulations of the probabilistic classical fields including interactions. For free probabilistic classical fields we construct a general ``classical-quantum map'' between the classical probability distribution and the density matrix of the quantum field theory.

</details>


### [171] [Harmonic superspace and Kaluza-Klein reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15782)
*Nikita Zaigraev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Kaluza-Klein约化推广至调和超空间框架，通过形变调和导数（引入质量项）实现保持显式离壳超对称性与Grassmann解析性的约化；成功构造了4D, N=2 BPS质量矢量与超引力多重态的离壳超场公式，并揭示其内在解析Stückelberg规范对称性；该方法可系统延拓至高自旋BPS多重态，并兼容N=1超空间表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建4D, N=2 BPS质量超多重态（尤其是高自旋）的规范不变、离壳超场描述，克服传统约化中破坏解析性或超对称性的困难。

Method: 在调和超空间中对Kaluza-Klein约化进行重构：以质量项形变调和导数，同时严格保持离壳N=2超对称性与Grassmann解析性；利用解析预势与调和导数自然编码规范对称性的结构特性。

Result: （1）得到自由5D, N=1 Maxwell理论约化后的4D, N=2质量矢量多重态离壳超场作用量，含解析Stückelberg规范对称性；（2）导出线性化5D, N=1 Poincaré超引力约化后的4D, N=2质量超引力多重态无约束调和超空间作用量；（3）确立该方法对任意BPS高自旋多重态的普适性，并可转译为4D, N=1超空间形式。

Conclusion: 调和超空间中的形变KK约化提供了一种系统、简洁且保持关键对称性的工具，为构造规范不变、离壳、BPS质量高自旋超多重态提供了直接路径，并架起了N=2与N=1超对称描述之间的桥梁。

Abstract: We extend the Kaluza-Klein reduction procedure to the framework of harmonic superspace and apply it to the construction of off-shell superfield formulations of supersymmetric gauge multiplets. The reduction is implemented by a deformation of the harmonic derivatives by mass terms, while ensuring that manifest off-shell supersymmetry and Grassmann analyticity are preserved at all stages. As a first application, we reduce the free \(5D,\mathcal{N}=1\) Maxwell theory and obtain an off-shell superfield description of the BPS \(4D,\mathcal{N}=2\) massive vector supermultiplet. The resulting formulation naturally incorporates an analytic Stückelberg-type gauge symmetry. We then reduce linearized \(5D,\mathcal{N}=1\) Poincaré supergravity and derive the unconstrained harmonic-superspace action for the BPS \(4D,\mathcal{N}=2\) massive supergravity multiplet. The technical simplicity of the procedure stems from the structure of gauge theories in harmonic superspace, where the gauge symmetry is naturally encoded in analytic prepotentials and harmonic derivatives. The approach presented in this paper opens a direct route to gauge-invariant off-shell superfield formulations of massive higher-spin $4D,\mathcal{N}=2$ BPS multiplets. Furthermore, the resulting theories can be reformulated in $4D,\mathcal{N}=1$ superspace, opening the way to gauge-invariant off-shell descriptions of $4D,\mathcal{N}=1$ massive higher-spin theories.

</details>


### [172] [Swing Surfaces in AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15854)
*Jacqueline Caminiti,Sabrina Pasterski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₃/CFT₂在平坦极限下的全息纠缠熵，指出标准Ryu-Takayanagi（RT）公式在该极限下无良定义，并通过构造AdS₃上提升的swing曲面，给出其信息论诠释：一是swing长度等于特定纯CFT态中近零边界区间间的互信息；二是将swing曲面的信息解释问题归约为黑洞内视界的信息论诠释这一更普遍问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清平坦空间全息中swing曲面与RT曲面的关系，解决RT公式在AdS₃→ℝ^{1,2}极限下失效所揭示的状态-算符映射不连续性问题。

Method: 1) 分析RT曲面在AdS₃平坦极限下的发散行为；2) 构造AdS₃中swing曲面的上提升（uplift）；3) 通过近零边界区间互信息计算建立其信息论对应；4) 将swing曲面解释映射至黑洞内视界的信息论问题。

Result: 1) RT公式在平坦极限下无良定义，源于CFT₂中真空态与激发态间状态-算符映射的退化；2) swing曲面长度精确等于一类纯CFT态中两个近零类光边界区间的互信息；3) 该互信息对应于AdS₃深 bulk 中的散射过程；4) swing曲面的信息论意义等价于黑洞内视界的信息论诠释问题。

Conclusion: swing曲面并非RT曲面的平凡极限，而是承载了非微扰、非局部信息（如内视界结构）的新几何对象；其信息论基础需超越标准边缘态重构框架，指向量子引力中内部时空结构的编码机制。

Abstract: We study holographic entanglement entropy in the flat limit of AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$, and clarify the relation between Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces and the swing surfaces proposed for flat-space holography. We show that the ordinary RT formula does not admit a well-defined flat-space limit and connect this to subtleties of the state-operator map in the flat limit. We then construct an AdS$_3$ uplift of the swing prescription and develop its corresponding information-theoretic interpretation through two complementary approaches. First, we show that the swing surface length computes the mutual information between suitably chosen nearly null boundary intervals in a special class of pure CFT states. The gravity interpretation of this result involves scattering processes which take place deep inside of the AdS$_3$ bulk. Second, we reduce the problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation for swing surfaces to the more general problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation of black hole inner horizons in holography.

</details>


### [173] [$α'^2$-corrections to the bosonic string from an action-level double copy map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15862)
*Eric Lescano,Agustina Pereyra Grau,Jesús A. Rodríguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了玻色弦低能有效作用量中Riem²和Riem³高导数修正的动理学双拷贝描述，发现需引入F³型规范理论高导数项及色-动量对应关系的变形；唯一解导致左-右对称立方作用量(YM+F³)⊗(YM+F³)，并自动重现α′阶Riem H²耦合、α′²阶无六导数H³耦合，以及H场奇次相互作用的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解玻色弦低能有效理论中高阶曲率修正（Riem², Riem³）与规范理论之间的双拷贝结构，特别是其在散射振幅与作用量层次上的实现机制。

Method: 基于色-动量二象性，构造含F³项的高导数规范理论拉氏量，并对标准色→动量映射（f^{abc} → k_1^{[μ} η^{ν]ρ}）施加动量张量形变；在立方阶展开下匹配引力端Riem²与Riem³顶点，约束耦合常数与映射参数。

Result: 证明标准色→动量映射无法生成Riem³顶点；存在唯一双根解，使立方作用量为(YM+F³)⊗(YM+F³)形式；该构造自动复现α′阶Riem H²耦合、α′²阶无六导数Kalb–Ramond自耦合，且无H的奇次相互作用。

Conclusion: 玻色弦α′展开中前两阶高导数引力修正具有严格的双拷贝结构，其规范理论端由F³算符与变形色-动量映射共同决定，且立方阶结果具有左-右对称性与内在一致性。

Abstract: The low-energy effective action of the bosonic string contains higher-derivative corrections of the form Riem$^2$ and Riem$^3$ to the Einstein--Hilbert action, together with the associated Kalb--Ramond couplings. We construct an action-level double copy description of these corrections up to cubic order in the fields. Two ingredients are required: a higher-derivative gauge-theory Lagrangian, which up to field redefinitions is controlled by a single $F^3$ coupling, and a deformation of the color-to-kinematics replacement of the structure constants by the corresponding three-momentum tensor. We show that no higher-derivative gauge-theory operator can produce the Riem$^3$ vertex with the standard replacement rule, and that the matching conditions for the gauge-theory coupling and the deformation of the map admit a unique solution, a double root, which renders the cubic action left--right symmetric, of the form $(\mathrm{YM}+F^3)\otimes(\mathrm{YM}+F^3)$. Without further input, the resulting map reproduces the cubic $\mathrm{Riem}\,H^2$ coupling of the bosonic string at order $\alpha'$, the absence of six-derivative cubic Kalb--Ramond couplings at order $\alpha'^2$, and the absence of interactions odd in the Kalb--Ramond field. The comparison is performed in transverse-traceless gauge, up to field redefinitions.

</details>


### [174] [Subsystem Flow: Quantum Error Correction, Petz Recovery, and Ergodic Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15887)
*Yidong Chen,Nima Lashkari,Kwing Lam Leung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将量子纠错（QEC）与局部多体量子系统中的子系统包含关系联系起来，提出‘模自洽性’（modular autonomy）作为子系统在模动力学下是否可被保护的判据；引入‘子系统流’量化偏离该纠错机制的程度，并建立其与相对熵损失、历史代数、持久核、Petz恢复映射及正定包含母哈密顿量之间的严格联系；证明标准因子包含（如QFT中嵌套区域）是遍历的，L²-核性蕴含母哈密顿量谱离散，并构造一个具有能隙谱的耗散半群，最后猜想其本征值/本征态可用于定义量子混沌的局域谱测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子纠错的编码保护思想推广至自然出现的子系统包含结构（如QFT中区域代数），探究在无主动编码前提下，参考态下子系统N能否被其模动力学自动保护免受补集擦除——即‘模自洽性’是否成立。

Method: 运用算子代数（特别是冯·诺依曼代数）、Tomita-Takesaki理论、模块化理论、Petz恢复映射、L²-核性分析及非平衡量子动力学方法；定义子系统流作为模动力学偏离纠错条件的度量，结合相对熵、历史代数与持久核刻画可观测量演化；通过解析延拓构造正定包含母哈密顿量，并研究其生成代数与谱性质。

Result: 1) 模自洽性等价于子系统N在M的模动力学下不变；2) 子系统流 infinitesimally 量化相对熵损失；3) 遍历包含（如QFT嵌套区域）满足 modular orbit 生成全代数 M∨M′ 且无持久可观测量；4) L²-核性 ⇒ 母哈密顿量谱离散；5) Petz重复映射构成gapped耗散半群，其不动点代数=持久核；6) 提出用母哈密顿量本征谱定义量子混沌局域谱测度的猜想。

Conclusion: 模自洽性为理解自然量子系统中内在纠错能力提供了新范式；子系统流及其解析延拓所导出的母哈密顿量，不仅统一刻画了信息保留、动力学生成与耗散结构，还为量子混沌提供了潜在的代数-谱学刻画工具，尤其在共形场论与AdS/CFT背景下具有深刻意义。

Abstract: In quantum error correction (QEC), the goal is to engineer an encoding that protects logical information against the erasure of a physical subsystem. In local many-body quantum systems, by contrast, nature provides a subsystem inclusion $\mN\subset \mM$ in a fixed reference state $\ket{\Omega}$. Asking whether the inclusion admits a reference-preserving code that protects subsystem $\mN$ against erasure of the complement is equivalent to asking whether $\mN$ is preserved under the modular dynamics of $\mM$ in this state (modular autonomy). We define the subsystem flow, which measures the departure from this error correction regime. Away from autonomy, the infinitesimal flow quantifies relative-entropy loss, while the history algebra and persistent core describe which observables the modular orbit generates and retains. We call an inclusion ergodic when modular orbits generate everything and retain nothing. Analytic continuation of subsystem flow yields Petz recovery maps and a family of positive inclusion parent Hamiltonians. We show that, in general, evolving with these parent Hamiltonians allows $\mN$ to leave $\mM$, and in some cases, it can generate the entire $\mM\vee\mM'$. Inclusions with a common cyclic and separating vector generate everything, and standard inclusions of factors, such as those of nested subregions in QFT, are ergodic. Moreover, the $L^2$-nuclearity property implies that the spectrum of parent Hamiltonians is discrete. We define a dissipative semigroup by repeating the Petz recovery map, whose fixed-point algebra is the modular persistent core and whose spectrum is gapped under the assumption of $L^2$-nuclearity. We conjecture that the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of inclusion parent Hamiltonians can be used to define local spectral measures of quantum chaos.

</details>


### [175] [Magnetic-slope crossovers in the QCD bulk-to-shear viscosity ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15929)
*Aalok Misra,Shivam S. Kushwah,Keshav Dasgupta,Charles Gale,Sangyong Jeon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过结合MQGP启发的M/弦理论构造、动理学恒等式及格点QCD物态方程数据，预测磁场下纵向体粘滞与剪切粘滞比 ζ_∥/η_∥ 随温度变化的斜率在 T₁ < T₀ < T₂ 处发生两次符号反转（T₀为赝临界退禁闭温度），该现象在夸克-胶子等离子体（T > T₀）与手征π介子气体（T < T₀）两种载流子图像下均自洽，并与格点声速数据一致；VQCD模型在高温区支持负斜率，但无法分辨斜率交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场对夸克-胶子物质耗散性质（尤其是粘滞系数）的影响缺乏第一性原理约束，因实时输运不可直接由格点模拟获得。

Method: 采用顶向下MQGP启发的M/弦论QCD类热等离子体构造；在单模准粒子截断下运用动理学恒等式；结合格点物态方程数据进行归一化不敏感的兼容性构造；并交叉检验VQCD底向上模型。

Result: 预言纵向体粘滞/剪切粘滞比 ζ_∥/η_∥ 关于磁场的斜率在温度上存在两次符号反转：T₁ < T₀ < T₂，其中T₀为退禁闭赝临界温度，T₁的存在依赖于一个π介子气体衰变速率不等式；该模式在QGP与手征π气体双相动理学描述下自洽，且与格点声速数据一致。

Conclusion: 磁场对纵向输运粘滞比的温度依赖具有非平庸结构——双重斜率反转，暗示退禁闭相变附近磁致耗散机制的拓扑或对称性转变，为强磁场下QCD物质非平衡动力学提供了可证伪的理论预言。

Abstract: How a magnetic field alters dissipation in quark--gluon matter is almost unconstrained from first principles, since real-time transport is not directly accessible to lattice simulation. Using a top-down MQGP-inspired M/string-theory construction of a QCD-like thermal plasma, together with a kinetic-theory identity within a specified single-mode quasiparticle truncation and a compatibility construction using available lattice equation-of-state data, we propose a normalization-insensitive sign prediction that the field slope of the longitudinal bulk-to-shear viscosity ratio \zeta_\parallel/\eta_\parallel reverses sign twice with temperature---below and above the deconfinement pseudo-critical temperature T_0, at T_1<T_0<T_2 (the lower reversal T_1 conditional on a stated pion-gas rate inequality). A kinetic-theory identity with phase-appropriate carriers, a quark--gluon plasma above T_0 and a chiral pion gas below, reproduces the same pattern, and lattice speed-of-sound data can be shown to be consistent as well. The bottom-up VQCD in the deconfined phase supports a negative slope for large temperatures; the slope crossover is not resolvable from the available \zeta_\parallel/s, \eta_\parallel/s plots.

</details>


### [176] [Extension of Poincare superalgebra via hidden symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15946)
*Ümit Ertem*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个扩展的Poincaré超代数，其偶部由Killing旋量构成，奇部则推广为特殊奇Killing-Yano形式与特殊偶闭共形Killing-Yano形式的空间，并通过定义适当括号实现超对称结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Poincaré超代数中基于Killing矢量场与Killing旋量的对称性结构，推广至包含高阶微分形式（Killing-Yano形式）所编码的‘隐藏对称性’的更广框架。

Method: 利用Killing-Yano形式（特别是特殊奇KY形式与特殊偶闭CKY形式）构造超代数的奇部，以Killing旋量空间作为偶部；通过明确定义超李括号（包括旋量-形式、形式-形式等混合括号）验证超代数结构。

Result: 证明了Killing旋量与特定Killing-Yano/共形Killing-Yano形式在适当括号下闭合，构成一个非平凡的超代数，该代数是Poincaré超代数的几何推广，编码时空的隐藏对称性。

Conclusion: Killing旋量与特殊Killing-Yano结构共同定义了一个扩展的Poincaré超代数，为研究弯曲时空中的超对称性与可积系统提供了统一的微分几何框架。

Abstract: Isometries of a manifold and special types of spinors called Killing spinors which are related to Killing vector fields constitute a symmetry superalgebra structure corresponding to Poincare superalgebra. The antisymmetric generalization of Killing vector fields to higher degree forms which are called Killing-Yano forms are considered. The odd part of the Poincare superalgebra is extended from the space of Killing vector fields and their rotational representation to the space of special odd Killing-Yano forms and special even closed conformal Killing-Yano forms. The even part is again corresponds to the space of Killing spinors. By defining the relevant brackets, we show that hidden symmetries and Killing spinors together define an extension of the Poincare superalgebra.

</details>


### [177] [Anti-periodic Gravitino and Dirac Effective Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16047)
*Karim Benakli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在保留U(1)_R对称性下，如何在N=1超空间中用有效算符描述引力微子的Dirac质量；通过引入非局域逆达朗贝尔算符耦合的规范不变引力微子曲率超场，结合S^1/Z_2紧致化的五维最小超引力与Scherk-Schwarz扭结诱导的反周期边界条件，导出KK Dirac质量作为曲率超场两点函数的极点，并将物理-规范模分离后的费米子核（16×16矩阵）与两个N=1超空间不变量的费米分量精确匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规避U(1)_R对称性禁止引力微子Majorana质量但允许Dirac质量的限制，构建与Dirac胶微子质量类比的超空间有效描述，以理解高维超引力中自旋-3/2粒子的质量生成机制。

Method: 基于五维N=1超引力在S^1/Z_2上的紧致化，利用Scherk-Schwarz机制实现反周期边界条件；构造引力微子曲率超场，引入含逆达朗贝尔算符的非局域核；通过两点函数极点提取KK Dirac质量；分离物理与规范自由度，导出16×16费米子传播核，并与N=1超空间中两个规范不变量的费米分量（含旋量缩并与归一化）比对。

Result: 得到引力微子Dirac质量对应于曲率超场两点函数的极点；导出的16×16费米子核完全等价于两个N=1超空间不变量的费米分量，验证了该有效算符构造的一致性；该匹配在具有非零径向辅助场的固定背景上成立。

Conclusion: 引力微子的Dirac质量可在N=1超空间中由非局域曲率超场算符一致描述，其动力学本质由Scherk-Schwarz紧致化与U(1)_R保护共同决定，且与低能超对称有效理论中的不变量结构严格兼容。

Abstract: Introducing a preserved $U(1)_R$ symmetry forbids gravitini Majorana masses while allowing a Dirac mass. We investigate how a Dirac mass for gravitini can be described by an effective operator in $N=1$ superspace, in analogy with the well-known Dirac gaugino mass. We find that the corresponding spin-$3/2$ bilinear operator can be expressed in terms of gauge-invariant gravitino curvature superfields coupled through a nonlocal kernel involving an inverse d'Alembertian. We study this construction in minimal five-dimensional supergravity compactified on $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$. The gravitino satisfies anti-periodic boundary conditions induced by a Scherk-Schwarz twist. The physical Kaluza-Klein Dirac masses then appear as poles in the two-point functions of the curvature superfields associated with sources localized at the endpoints. Separation of physical and gauge modes relates these propagators to a quadratic fermionic kernel represented by a $16\times16$ matrix. Its elements match the fermionic components of two invariants written in $N=1$ superspace, including their spinor contractions and normalization. This matching is carried out on a fixed background with a nonzero radion auxiliary field.

</details>


### [178] [Holographic Wilsonian RG of Yang-Mills theory in AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16074)
*Soner Albayrak*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将随机量子化与Wilsonian重整化之间的平衡-演化对偶性推广到反德西特（AdS）空间中的非阿贝尔规范理论，其中AdS的径向坐标扮演随机时间角色；证明了Langevin分布对应于径向演化的Doob变换，并建立了AdS中随机规范固定、径向规范与Zwanziger规范之间的精确对应；同时给出了有限截断下全息Wilsonian作用量中三重迹与四重迹耦合的闭式表达式，其红外极限复现胶子关联函数，总能量极点处恢复平直空间杨-米尔斯振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立非阿贝尔规范理论在弯曲时空（特别是AdS）中随机量子化与Wilsonian重整化之间的严格对偶关系，弥合统计物理、量子场论与全息原理之间的概念鸿沟，并为AdS/CFT中的RG流提供随机过程诠释。

Method: 采用随机微分方程（Langevin方程）描述AdS中径向演化，结合Doob变换提取地面态演化；通过显式计算匹配Zwanziger随机规范固定、Parisi-Wu量子化及特定径向规范条件；利用全息Wilsonian重整化框架导出有限截断下的高阶迹耦合项。

Result: 1) AdS中径向演化等价于Langevin过程的Doob变换；2) 明确识别A_z分量为Zwanziger随机规范固定函数，径向规范对应Parisi-Wu量子化，A_z=α⁻¹∂_i A_i为Zwanziger规范；3) 获得三重迹与四重迹耦合的闭式解；4) 红外极限再现胶子n点函数，总能量极点复原平直空间YM散射振幅。

Conclusion: 随机量子化与Wilsonian RG在AdS非阿贝尔规范理论中存在深层结构性对偶，该对偶不仅统一了不同量子化方案，还为全息RG提供了基于随机过程的微观诠释；结果可推广至dS时空并兼容圈图修正。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the equilibrium-evolution duality between stochastic quantization and Wilsonian renormalization to non-abelian gauge theories in anti-de Sitter space, for which the radial coordinate of AdS plays the role of the stochastic time. We show, to all orders in the fields for bulk theories without explicit radial dependence, that the Langevin distribution at time $t$ is the product of the ultraviolet and infrared radial wavefunctions at the cutoff $\epsilon=t$. We therefore identify this Langevin process as the ground-state (Doob) transform of the radial evolution. By explicit calculation, we also extend the existing dictionary in the stochastic-renormalization duality: the radial component of the bulk field is Zwanziger's stochastic gauge-fixing function; radial gauge is Parisi-Wu quantization; and the bulk gauge $A_z=\alpha^{-1}\partial_iA_i$ is Zwanziger's gauge. We also obtain the expressions for the triple-trace and quadruple-trace couplings of the holographic Wilsonian action at finite cutoff in closed form; in the infrared, they reduce to the known gluon correlators and, at the total-energy pole, to flat-space Yang-Mills amplitudes as expected. Although our explicit results are provided at tree-level and derived in AdS$_4$, we also comment on the de Sitter counterpart of these statements and on the loop corrections.

</details>


### [179] [Multiparticle residues of open-string tachyon amplitudes from DDF sewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16280)
*Dripto Biswas,Qu Cao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过求和所有物理中间态，计算了临界开玻色弦中色序快子盘振幅的最大同时留数；利用立方DDF顶点拼接，将求和简化为高斯收缩，并结合行列式因子与二次型指数的系数提取得到留数；四点情形下用局部映射与应力张量Ward恒等式实现了对Veneziano极点系数的全阶重求和；五点双留数与六点梳状/雪花图也给出显式矩阵，并通过低阶Koba-Nielsen展开验证；最终将该构造与Grunsky算子及高点Koba-Nielsen上形式联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种系统、可计算的框架来精确提取高能弦振幅中多重极点的留数结构，特别是关联到经典Veneziano振幅及其高点推广的解析结构与共形场论表示。

Method: 采用立方DDF顶点的拼接技术，结合高斯收缩、真空拼接因子（横向玻色子行列式）、局部坐标规范一致性处理、应力张量Ward恒等式以及Koba-Nielsen积分表示的比对验证。

Result: 得到了任意n点色序快子振幅最大同时留数的显式构造：包括四点Veneziano系数的全阶重求和证明、五点双留数与六点组合（梳状/雪花）的显式矩阵表达式，并确认其真空拼接因子即为横向玻色子的行列式；建立了与Grunsky算子和Koba-Nielsen上形式的严格对应关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为开弦振幅的极点结构提供了统一的算子-几何构造，揭示了DDF方法在高点留数计算中的强大能力，并架起了顶点算子代数、共形映射理论与散射振幅解析结构之间的桥梁。

Abstract: We compute the maximal simultaneous residues of color-ordered tachyon disk amplitudes in the critical open bosonic string by summing all physical intermediate states. Sewing cubic Del Giudice--Di Vecchia--Fubini (DDF) vertices reduces this sum to Gaussian contractions for any planar cubic tree, with the internal levels specified independently. Each internal level fixes an exact finite mode cutoff. The residue then follows by coefficient extraction in the sewing variables from a determinant prefactor times the exponential of a quadratic form in the sources. With consistent sewing conventions, the local-coordinate scales cancel between the vertices and the sewing metric. At four points, cubic local maps and a one-boson stress-tensor Ward identity give an all-order resummation that recovers the Veneziano pole coefficients. The same construction gives explicit matrices for the five-point double residue and the six-point comb and snowflake, with low-level checks against independent Koba--Nielsen expansions. The determinant is the vacuum sewing factor of the transverse bosons. We formulate the operator, coordinate-map and measure identities needed to relate this construction to Grunsky operators and the full higher-point Koba--Nielsen top forms.

</details>


### [180] [Generalized Kosmann derivative and symmetry operators on Dirac structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16309)
*Ümit Ertem,Özgür Açık,Özgür Kelekçi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了H-扭曲线性Courant胚上度量Dirac结构的旋量对称性框架，建立了正旋子Kosmann-Dorfman作用与内禀Kosmann导数的精确对应，并导出Killing-Yano型可积恒等式；通过特殊KY/CKY形式构造Killing旋子保持算子，且这些形式由虚数Killing数的Killing旋子的厄米双线性给出；在S²×S¹和S³上的具体例子中验证了该框架，揭示了广义等距、隐藏旋量对称性与通量敏感的Lie胚几何之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于H-扭曲线性Courant胚上Dirac结构的旋量对称性理论，以统一处理广义几何中的度量结构、通量背景与自旋几何之间的相互作用，弥补现有广义Killing场理论在旋量层面的缺失。

Method: 基于广义切丛的旋量表示与H-扭曲Courant括号，定义广义度量诱导的Dirac结构内禀Riemannian Lie-代胚结构；引入正旋子丛上的Kosmann–Dorfman作用，并与内禀Kosmann导数比较；利用广义联络无关的旋量微分算子导出KY/CKY型可积条件；通过厄米双线性构造特殊微分形式并分析其对称性提升。

Result: 1) 正旋子Kosmann–Dorfman作用与内禀Kosmann导数严格等价；2) 导出广义KY与CKY可积恒等式；3) 构造由奇特殊KY与偶特殊闭CKY形式生成的Killing旋子保持算子；4) 证明这些形式源于虚数Killing数Killing旋子的厄米双线性；5) 在S²×S¹（非平凡H-通量、秩二锚）与S³（图Dirac结构）上实现显式计算，发现Killing旋子空间为C∞(S¹,ℂ²)，且Hermitian-电流括号的Jacobi恒等式仅差一相位，添加偶相位生成元后形成Lie超代数。

Conclusion: 该框架确立了H-扭曲线性Courant胚上Dirac结构的旋量对称性的内在几何刻画，将广义等距、隐藏旋量守恒律与通量敏感Lie-代胚几何统一于一个旋量微分算子体系；其结果为广义超引力背景下的对称性分析与量子化提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: We develop a spinorial symmetry framework for metric Dirac structures in $H$-twisted exact Courant algebroids. A generalized metric equips a Dirac structure with an intrinsic Riemannian Lie-algebroid geometry and identifies it isometrically with the positive subbundle of the generalized tangent bundle. For sections preserving the generalized metric, we prove an exact intertwining between the positive-spinor Kosmann--Dorfman action and the intrinsic Kosmann derivative. The comparison is independent of the auxiliary compatible generalized connection. Within the intrinsic geometry we derive Killing--Yano (KY) and conformal Killing--Yano (CKY) integrability identities, construct Killing-spinor-preserving operators from odd special KY and even special closed CKY forms, and obtain these forms from Hermitian bilinears of Killing spinors with imaginary Killing number. We illustrate the framework on a global Dirac structure over $S^2\times S^1$ with nontrivial $H$-flux class and rank-two anchor. Its spin connection and symmetry operators are explicit, and its Killing-spinor space is a transverse family $C^\infty(S^1,\mathbb C^2)$. Finally, intrinsic Killing sections act on Killing spinors by a Lie algebra representation. For a graph Dirac structure over $S^3$, the odd Jacobi identity of the Hermitian-current bracket fails by a phase, and adjoining a single even phase generator yields a Lie superalgebra. These results connect generalized isometries, hidden spinorial symmetries, and flux-sensitive Lie-algebroid geometry.

</details>


### [181] [Matching lattice and continuum chiral determinants at finite volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16544)
*Zhengwen Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了在平直四维环面上单代SU(5)模型中，Craig的规范不变重叠测度与Hsu-Witten基于五维η-不变量定相的连续Weyl行列式在光滑能隙路径的连续极限下相对权重一致，需引入特定局域杨-米尔斯反项；并给出了对数变分的一致收敛速率、重构建界及反射正定情形下的相位匹配与模长修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一处理格点手征规范理论中不同费米子权重定义（重叠测度 vs. 重构联络上的Weyl行列式）的等价性问题，尤其在非微扰、有限振幅与边界效应下验证其连续极限一致性。

Method: 运用谱流分析、五维η-不变量的变分公式、真空极化张量的解析计算、重构建界估计及数值验证；结合光滑有界族上的一致能隙条件与反射正定重构（带平直领域）。

Result: 1) 光滑能隙路径下两权重比值在连续极限中一致；2) 确定了必需的局域YM反项系数；3) 给出对数变分的一致收敛速率；4) 证明Craig插值的重构建界；5) 反射正定情形下相位仍匹配，但二次反项系数被轮廓依赖因子增强；6) 在超荷背景中，模长出现对数修正的二次标度。

Conclusion: 两种格点手征费米子构造在物理可观测量（如手征反常、真空极化）层面具有普适等价性，其差异可由局域反项吸收；该结论超越微扰论，适用于有限耦合与几何边界效应。

Abstract: Recent formulations of lattice chiral gauge theories define a complex fermion weight either through Craig's gauge-invariant overlap measure or, following Hsu and Witten, through a continuum Weyl determinant on a reconstructed gauge connection, with its phase fixed by the five-dimensional $\eta$-invariant. For one $SU(5)$ generation on a flat four-torus, we prove that their relative weights agree in the continuum limit along smooth gapped paths, after fixing a normalization and subtracting a local Yang-Mills counterterm, whose coefficient we determine. On bounded smooth families a uniform Dirac gap suffices: the vacuum expansion need not converge. We obtain explicit uniform convergence rates for the logarithmic variations and, for Craig's interpolation, prove the reconstruction bounds that the matching requires, extending Hsu's small-field comparison to finite amplitude. For Hsu's reflection-positive reconstruction with flat collars, the phase variations still match, while the logarithmic coefficient of the quadratic counterterm is enhanced by a calculable, profile-dependent factor. In a hypercharge background on which the vacuum expansion diverges, numerical comparisons at the input gauge field support quadratic convergence of the phase, whereas the modulus acquires a logarithmic correction to quadratic scaling, whose coefficient follows analytically from the free overlap vacuum polarization.

</details>


### [182] [Accelerating solitons in Rindler space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16568)
*Huiquan Li,Jianyong Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了正弦-戈登（sine-Gordon）模型在Rindler时空中的经典孤子解，发现Minkowski时空中的完整孤子在Rindler观者视角下表现为携带部分拓扑荷的‘截断孤子’，其动力学体现为从Rindler视界入射、减速至静止、再反向加速落回视界的运动；能量可在Rindler视界与孤子之间交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相对论性非线性可积场论（如sine-Gordon模型）在加速参考系（Rindler时空）中是否保持可积性，并理解惯性系与加速系间孤子物理图像的对应关系，尤其是视界对拓扑结构和能量流动的影响。

Method: 基于Rindler坐标变换将二维Minkowski时空映射为左右Rindler楔形区域，求解Rindler度规下的sine-Gordon方程经典孤子解，分析其拓扑荷分布、运动轨迹及能量流密度（通过Noether流或应力-能量张量分量）。

Result: 1) Rindler空间中sine-Gordon孤子仍为精确解；2) 每个Rindler观者仅观测到原Minkowski孤子的一部分，该部分具有分数化拓扑荷；3) 孤子在Rindler时间演化中呈现‘视界反射’行为：从视界出射→减速至转向点→加速返回视界；4) 孤子能量随其振幅变化而与Rindler视界交换能量，高能孤子吸能，低能孤子释能。

Conclusion: Rindler时空虽破坏全局洛伦兹对称性，但sine-Gordon模型的可积性得以保持，且孤子的拓扑与动力学展现出视界诱导的类黑洞散射特征，为理解加速系中量子孤子辐射（如Unruh效应推广）提供了经典基础。

Abstract: Relativistic nonlinear field theories that are integrable in two-dimensional Minkowski spacetime remain integrable in Rindler space. We investigate the properties of classical sine-Gordon (SG) solitons in Rindler space. Accelerating observers in each of the two Rindler wedges can see only a portion of the full soliton in Minkowski spacetime; this portion itself forms a soliton carrying a partial topological charge. The soliton solution admits the following interpretation: a soliton incoming from the horizon decelerates to stop at some point, then falls back and accelerates to the speed of light toward the horizon. Energy can flow into and out of the Rindler horizon. The higher energy soliton gains energy from the horizon while the lower one loses energy.

</details>


### [183] [Is Spin Slow in Hot QCD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16684)
*Zi-Hao Liu,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推翻了将自旋作为流体力学变量的常见假设，指出轻夸克自旋在QGP中以g⁴T ln(1/g)速率快速阻尼，与矢量流相当，主因是动量-自旋锁定及软小角度散射主导的helicity守恒旋转；多模态动力学和全局转动的可逆性进一步排除慢自旋模式的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验自旋是否可作为QGP中的慢驰豫流体力学自由度，尤其针对质量压低的helicity翻转在高温QCD中是否足够慢。

Method: 采用全阶领头阶碰撞算符（leading-order collision operator），严格处理轻夸克自旋演化，结合动量-自旋锁定、软小角度散射的动力学分析及多动能模态展开。

Result: 轻夸克自旋阻尼率为g⁴T ln(1/g)，与输运率同量级；空间自旋与动量强锁定；helicity守恒的软散射主导自旋旋转；阻尼过程涉及多个动能模态，非单弛豫时间描述；全局转动仅引入可逆Larmor进动；耦合增强进一步抑制慢自旋模式。

Conclusion: 自旋不能作为QGP中的慢变量纳入流体力学框架，因其弛豫远快于流体力学适用的时间尺度，且缺乏普适的慢模结构。

Abstract: Promoting spin to a hydrodynamic variable requires slow relaxation, as mass-suppressed helicity flips in hot QCD suggest. Using the full leading-order collision operator, we show instead that light-quark spin damps at the transport rate $g^4T\ln(1/g)$, nearly as fast as vector currents. Since spatial spin locks to momentum, helicity-conserving soft, small-angle scatterings readily rotate it. Damping involves multiple kinetic modes beyond a single relaxation time, global rotation adds only reversible precession, and stronger coupling further disfavors a slow spin mode in quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [184] [Discrete anomaly inflow from SymTFT of $U(1)$ global symmetry: the modified Villain resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16696)
*Napat Poovuttikul,K. Sapmee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了具有异常系数k的U(1)全局对称性的格点对称拓扑场论（SymTFT），通过修正的Villain形式化方法，并显式实现奇数k所需的自旋结构（由格点Majorana费米子实现）；进一步从线与结的格点代数导出体和gapped边界的R-、F-符号，证明奇数k时边界F-符号满足Gu-Wen关系，且无需玻色阴影；限制到Z2子群时复现模4反常相与费米子叠堆规则；并应用该SymTFT判别哪些Z_d ⊂ U(1)子群可被规范化及其结果理论的对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从连续对称性的反常流入出发，推导超越群上同调的全局反常结数据，特别是处理U(1)对称性及其离散子群的格点实现与反常特征。

Method: 采用修正的Villain格点公式化构造U(1) SymTFT；引入格点Majorana费米子以显式实现奇k所需的自旋结构；分析对称线与结的格点代数；计算体与gapped边界的R-、F-符号；将U(1)限制至Z2及Z_d子群进行约化分析。

Result: 1. 构造出带自旋结构的U(1)格点SymTFT；2. 证明Majorana费米子位于U(1)对称线交汇处，且其代数自然导出满足Gu-Wen关系的边界F-符号（奇k情形）；3. Z2限制下复现模4反常相与费米子叠堆规则；4. 给出Z_d ⊂ U(1)可规范化的充要条件及规范后理论的剩余对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在格点层面直接实现U(1)对称性的全局反常结构，绕过玻色阴影构造，为系统研究连续对称性反常的拓扑数据（如结、F符号）提供了可计算框架，并揭示了离散子群规范化的对称性保持机制。

Abstract: We construct a lattice SymTFT for a $U(1)$ global symmetry with anomaly coefficient $k$ using the modified Villain formulation, in which the spin structure required for odd $k$ is realised explicitly by lattice Majorana fermions. We demonstrate that the same Majorana fermions sit at the junctions of the symmetry lines, exactly where the fusion of two $U(1)$ elements wraps around the circle. From the lattice algebra of lines and junctions we derive the $R$- and $F$-symbols in the bulk and on the gapped boundary, and show that for odd $k$ the boundary $F$-symbol obeys the Gu-Wen relation for the $U(1)$ symmetry defect, without passing through a bosonic shadow. Restricting to $\mathbb{Z}_2\subset U(1)$, we reproduce the mod-4 anomalous phase from a mapping torus and the fermionic stacking rule. As a simple application of this SymTFT, we also determine which subgroups $\mathbb{Z}_d\subset U(1)$ can be gauged and the symmetry of the resulting theories. This is a first step towards deriving, from the anomaly inflow of a continuous symmetry, the junction data of global anomalies beyond group cohomology.

</details>


### [185] [Finite cyclic monodromy in BMS3 gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16823)
*Evangelos Melas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维渐近平坦BMS引力中，定义在具有缠绕的双曲Virasoro轨道（不含常数超动量）上的诱导数据，以及形式光滑等变截面在全局洛伦兹轨道上的限制；通过关联Hill算子的发展映射重新导出稳定子群结构，发现其在自旋二重覆盖下为一条直线与偶阶循环群的直积；该循环因子的特征标构成一个独立于连续参数的离散诱导标签，区别于质量BMS粒子中由自旋固定的特征标；进一步分析了有限旋转对称点处的循环等变条件、类时投影动量下的庞加莱螺旋度阶梯、投影静止能量非轨道不变性、具双曲洛伦兹单值性的局部平直缠绕BMS几何，并排除了光滑实周期近视界流模型涵盖此类缠绕轨道的可能性；最后给出有限单值性分解下的轨道泛函的选择规则与闭式表达；全文未构造全无穷维轨道上的幺正实现，循环扇区在量子引力中的物理解释仍属猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三维渐近平坦时空下BMS对称性表示论的精细结构，特别是含拓扑缠绕的双曲Virasoro轨道上诱导表示的分类与幺正实现问题，弥补现有BMS粒子分类（如质量/零质量、整数/半整数螺旋度）在非紧、非平凡单值性情形下的缺失。

Method: 运用发展映射（developing map）技术关联Hill算子与双曲Virasoro轨道几何；分析超动量密度的微分几何结构及等变截面空间的限制行为；利用洛伦兹群SL(2,R)及其自旋覆盖Spin(2,1)≡SL(2,C)_R的群表示与轨道理论；结合单值性（monodromy）、稳定子（stabilizer）与循环等变性（cyclic equivariance）刻画离散量子数；引入有限差分方程描述全局分支的幺正动力学。

Result: 1. 双曲缠绕Virasoro轨道的稳定子在Spin(2,1)中为ℝ × ℤ_{2n}（n∈ℕ）；2. 循环因子ℤ_{2n}的特征标χ_k（k=0,…,2n−1）是独立离散诱导标签；3. 类时投影动量下出现庞加莱螺旋度阶梯，但投影静止能量非轨道不变量；4. 构造出具双曲洛伦兹单值性的局部平直缠绕BMS几何；5. 光滑实周期近视界电流模型无法覆盖此类缠绕轨道；6. 获得有限单值性分解下轨道泛函的选择规则与闭式解。

Conclusion: 三维渐近平坦BMS引力中存在一类新型诱导表示，其量子数包含连续参数与独立离散循环标签，源于轨道的全局拓扑（缠绕）与双曲单值性；该结构超越标准BMS粒子分类，暗示量子引力中可能存在由单值性拓扑量子化导致的额外离散自由度；全轨道幺正实现仍未建立，循环扇区的物理诠释（如是否对应某种拓扑荷或边缘态）尚待深入。

Abstract: We investigate inducing data for three dimensional asymptotically flat BMS gravity on hyperbolic Virasoro orbits with winding, which contain no constant supermomentum, and the restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to global Lorentz orbits. Using the developing map of the associated Hill operator we rederive their stabilizer and show that, in the spin double cover, it is the direct product of a line with a cyclic group of even order. The character of the cyclic factor is therefore a discrete inducing label independent of the continuous one, unlike for the massive BMS particles, where it is fixed by the spin. At points with the corresponding finite rotational symmetry, restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to a global Lorentz orbit yields the finite cyclic equivariance condition, while an independently defined unitary global branch realizes the associated finite difference equation. At points with timelike projected momentum we derive the resulting ladder of Poincare helicities, show that the projected rest energy is not an orbit invariant, construct locally flat winding BMS geometries with hyperbolic Lorentz holonomy, prove that the smooth real periodic near horizon current model excludes these winding orbits, and obtain selection rules and closed form expressions for finite monodromy resolved orbital functionals. A unitary realization on the full infinite dimensional orbit is not constructed, and the interpretation of the cyclic sectors in quantum gravity remains conjectural.

</details>


### [186] [Gauge Theory Bootstrap with varying $N_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16824)
*Rafael Cordoba,Yifei He,Martin Kruczenski,Derek Ping,Jignesh Mohanty,Emil Alegria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用规范理论Bootstrap方法研究不同颜色数N_c的渐近自由规范理论，分析了散射长度、有效作用程、低能常数及σ、ρ、f₂共振态随N_c的变化行为，并验证了其与1/N_c展开和手征微扰论的自洽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究渐近自由规范理论在不同颜色数N_c下的强耦合低能动力学行为，检验Gauge Theory Bootstrap方法对非微扰QCD类理论的适用性与可靠性。

Method: 采用Gauge Theory Bootstrap框架，通过色数N_c依赖的求和规则与渐近形式因子调控微观参数，结合色单态强子谱与散射振幅的幺正化约束，提取低能散射参数与共振态性质。

Result: 在3≤N_c≤12范围内：(i) 散射长度、有效作用程及低能常数l̄_i呈现可分辨的N_c依赖；(ii) ρ与f₂质量弱依赖于N_c，宽度按~1/N_c压低，符合qq̄型介子的1/N_c计数律；(iii) σ介子质量与宽度均随N_c增大而增大，与幺正化χPT预言一致。

Conclusion: 结果证实Gauge Theory Bootstrap能系统刻画N_c依赖的非微扰强子物理，为基于第一性原理研究大N_c QCD极限及共振态结构提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We use the Gauge Theory Bootstrap to study asymptotically free gauge theories with a varying number of colors $N_c$. The parameter $N_c$ enters the computation through the sum rules and asymptotic form factors, allowing us to tune this microscopic parameter directly and examine the corresponding low-energy strongly coupled dynamics. For $3\le N_c\le 12$, we extract the scattering lengths, effective ranges, and low-energy constants $\bar{l}_i$, and study their $N_c$ dependence. We also obtain the $\sigma$, $\rho$, and $f_2$ resonances for the same range of $N_c$. The $\rho$ and $f_2$ masses depend only weakly on $N_c$, and their widths decrease as $N_c$ increases, consistent with the $1/N_c$ scaling expected for conventional $q\bar{q}$ mesons. In contrast, the $\sigma$ meson mass and width both increase with $N_c$, in agreement with a previous unitarized $\chi$PT analysis. These results provide a further test of the Gauge Theory Bootstrap for studying asymptotically free gauge theories.

</details>


### [187] [Infinite spin superparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17006)
*Eugenia Boffo,Martin Cederwall*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过在3、4、6和10维超闵可夫斯基背景上的高阶导数超粒子作用量，导出超庞加莱代数的无穷自旋表示，并借助超扭量变换推导第一量子化谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建高维时空中的无穷自旋表示，以拓展对超对称粒子物理与高自旋场论的理解，并探索其与超扭量几何的联系。

Method: 基于高阶导数超粒子作用量，在超庞加莱对称性下构造经典相空间；应用超扭量变换实现相空间变量的重参数化；通过量子化约束系统获得无穷自旋表示的希尔伯特空间结构。

Result: 成功构造了3、4、6、10维超闵可夫斯基时空中的无穷自旋表示；明确了其超庞加莱代数作用下的超多重态结构；给出了对应的第一量子化能谱及超扭量描述。

Conclusion: 无穷自旋表示可由超粒子模型自然实现，且其超扭量表述揭示了高维超对称性与高自旋自由度之间的深层几何关联，为高自旋引力与AdS/CFT中高自旋对偶提供了新路径。

Abstract: We obtain infinite spin representations of the super-Poincaré algebra from a higher-derivative superparticle action on a super-Minkowski background in 3, 4, 6 and 10 dimensions. Super-twistor transforms are performed, and used to deduce first-quantised spectra.

</details>


### [188] [Covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus, non-dissipative transport and the chiral anomaly in inhomogeneous systems with nontrivial momentum-space topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17178)
*M.A.Zubkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾并拓展了非阿贝尔规范场中费米子的协变Wigner-Weyl计算方法，聚焦于实空间非均匀性与动量空间非平凡拓扑共存的体系；核心成果包括：协变符号的规范无关算符刻画、积分反常的序无关性、四维闭流形上手征反常的严格分解（无需同伦假设）、Luttinger-Ward恒等式对重整化速度流的支撑、Fermi面不变量在非椭圆模型中的留数计算（连续/格点）、以及手征磁效应与手征分离效应在非哈密顿体系中的互斥实现机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立统一、自洽且可推广的协变相空间量子化框架，以刻画强关联、非均匀、非平庸拓扑（尤其动量空间）的量子费米系统，解决传统Wigner-Weyl方法在非阿贝尔规范场和非椭圆色散下的适用性与不变量定义问题。

Method: 协变Wigner-Weyl符号演算、非交换几何分析、指标理论（Atiyah-Singer型与指数配对）、留数定理（复分析与伪微分算子迹公式）、Luttinger-Ward泛函方法、主符号纤维度分析、瞬子数与动量空间不变量N₃的拓扑分解。

Result: 1) 给出协变符号规范无关性的充要条件；2) 积分手征反常在任意排序下不变；3) 在无同伦假设下完成四维闭流形上反常的瞬子数×N₃精确分解；4) 建立重整化速度流的Luttinger-Ward本源；5) 用留数公式统一计算非椭圆模型Fermi面不变量；6) 揭示非哈密顿体系中手征磁效应与手征分离效应的拓扑互斥性；7) 将N₃诠释为主符号的纤维度，并给出手征分离不变量的指数配对形式。
