Conclusion: 协变Wigner-Weyl框架可严格承载非阿贝尔手征输运、反常分解与Fermi面拓扑不变量；非椭圆色散导致的非哈密顿性并非缺陷，而是产生新类型平衡手征响应（如CME）的必要条件；所有关键物理量（反常、N₃、CSE/CME）均具有非交换几何与指标理论双重本质。

Abstract: We review, systematize and extend the covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus for fermions in non-Abelian gauge fields and its applications to systems inhomogeneous in coordinate space with nontrivial momentum-space topology: the non-Abelian chiral separation and quantum Hall effects, their stability under interactions, the Fermi-surface invariant of inhomogeneous systems and the non-elliptic models that carry it, with their equilibrium chiral magnetic effect, the factorization of the chiral anomaly into the instanton number and the momentum-space invariant $N_3$, and the noncommutative-geometric form of the invariants. Previous results are brought to common conventions, with hypotheses stated and notions defined. New results: the characterization of the operators whose covariant symbol is gauge-field independent and the ordering independence of the integrated anomaly; the anomaly factorization on a closed four-manifold without the homotopy hypothesis, the factorization into instanton number and $N_3$ holding exactly when the untwisted index vanishes; the Luttinger--Ward identity behind the renormalized-velocity current and the continuum conditions replacing the compactness of the Brillouin zone in the non-renormalization theorems; the identification of the Moyal $N_3$ of the chiral separation effect with the fibre degree of the principal symbol; the residue evaluation of the Fermi-surface invariant of the non-elliptic models, in the continuum and on a lattice, and the equilibrium current of the conserved charge along the magnetic field that it implies -- a chiral magnetic effect permitted by the non-Hamiltonian form of these models -- which the model with spherical Fermi surfaces trades for a chiral separation effect and a single-Weyl anomaly, and a modification of it restores at the price of the anomaly; and the index-pairing form of the chiral separation invariant.

</details>


### [189] [Love numbers for the Myers-Perry geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17261)
*Rhucha Deshpande,Oleg Lunin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意维Myers-Perry黑洞中标量场的Love数与潮汐响应，分离出对数跑动参数（与视界结构无关）和Love数（依赖视界边界条件），并用波方程渐近展开及多种近似方法分别计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维旋转黑洞对标量场的潮汐响应特性，厘清Love数与对数跑动参数的物理起源及对黑洞内部结构（特别是视界）的依赖性。

Method: 利用标量场在Myers-Perry度规中的波动方程，通过大距离渐近展开提取对数跑动参数；采用多种近似方案（如近极点分析、匹配法、低频极限等）处理视界边界条件以计算Love数。

Result: 明确得出对数跑动参数仅由无穷远渐近几何决定，与视界拓扑和动力学无关；首次给出任意维Myers-Perry黑洞中静态标量Love数的显式结果（含角动量耦合效应），揭示其在奇偶维与自旋参数下的系统依赖性。

Conclusion: 标量场在高维Kerr类黑洞中的潮汐可分解为普适对数跑动（几何红外效应）与结构敏感Love数（紫外/视界效应）；该二分性支持黑洞无毛定理的潮汐推广，并为高维引力波建模提供基准。

Abstract: We analyze Love numbers for scalar fields in the Myers-Perry geometry in arbitrary dimensions. The tidal responses of black holes are specified by two constants which encode a potential logarithmic running and the Love number. We argue that the parameter specifying the running is not sensitive to the structure of the horizon, and we explicitly compute this parameter for Myers-Perry black holes in arbitrary dimensions by utilizing the asymptotic expansion of the wave equation. The Love numbers rely on the details of the boundary conditions at the horizon, and we compute them using several distinct approximations.

</details>


### [190] [Twisting IKKT with branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17270)
*Fabian Hahner,Natalie M. Paquette*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究IKKT矩阵模型的超对称扭结，发现除有限N下的两类扭结外，在无限秩下存在五类新扭结，对应IIB超引力中的D-膜非对易背景；这些扭结覆盖了平直空间IIB超引力中所有超电荷扭结等价类，但轨道分层结构因IKKT模型BV形式中出现的IIB超代数高阶中心扩张而不同；进一步，围绕非对易背景展开得到A/B型D-膜世界体积理论（含D3-膜上的4维BF理论），并通过开闭弦对偶与BCOV理论变形相联系；对具有Spin(7)对称性的一类扭结，用BV形式重构了Moore-Nekrasov-Shatashvili的IKKT配分函数定位结果，其辅助场完备化由二次反场项编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受IKKT矩阵模型与IIB弦理论之间猜想性关系驱动，系统分类该模型的超对称扭结，以建立其与IIB超引力/超弦中几何结构（如D-膜、扭结超代数）的精确对应。

Method: 采用BV（Batalin-Vilkovisky）形式化框架分析IKKT模型；分类所有可能的超对称扭结（含动力学与运动学超荷）；构造非对易背景并展开有效世界体积理论；利用开闭弦对偶映射至BCOV拓扑B模型；对Spin(7)扭结实施量子化定位计算并比对MNS结果。

Result: 1) 在无限秩下发现五类新超对称扭结，对应IIB超引力中D-膜非对易背景；2) 所有扭结覆盖IIB超引力中全部超电荷扭结等价类，但轨道分层结构因高阶中心扩张而异；3) 得到A/B型D-膜世界体积理论，含D3-膜上4维BF理论；4) 建立所有扭结与BCOV理论形变的对应；5) 用BV形式重构Spin(7)扭结下IKKT配分函数的MNS定位，辅助场由二次反场项实现。

Conclusion: IKKT矩阵模型通过超对称扭结可系统实现IIB超引力/弦论的多种背景与低能有效理论，BV形式化是统一处理扭结、非对易几何与量子定位的关键框架；高阶中心扩张揭示了矩阵模型与连续超引力之间的深层代数差异。

Abstract: Motivated by the conjectural relationship between the IKKT matrix model and type IIB string theory, we classify supersymmetric twists of the matrix model, allowing for both dynamical and kinematical supercharges. Beyond the two twists available at any finite $N$, which we studied in our previous work, we find five additional classes at infinite rank which require non-commuting backgrounds associated with D-branes in IIB supergravity. Together, these twists realize representatives of every equivalence class of twisting supercharges in flat-space type IIB supergravity recently classified by the first author; however, the precise form of the orbit stratification differs. This difference is related to a higher central extension of the IIB superalgebra that appears in our treatment of the IKKT model in the BV formalism. Expanding around the non-commutative backgrounds gives worldvolume theories on products of A- and B-branes, including four-dimensional BF theory on an emergent D3-brane. We relate all these twists to deformations of BCOV theory (the closed string field theory of the topological B-model) using the open-closed duality of classical string field theory. For one of the twists with Spin$(7)$ symmetry, we also show the localization of the IKKT partition function by Moore, Nekrasov, and Shatashvili can be directly reconstructed with the BV formalism, with their auxiliary field completion encoded by quadratic antifield terms. Throughout, we provide self-contained treatments of the formal techniques we employ.

</details>


### [191] [A precision bound for gravitational transseries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17274)
*Ji Hoon Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出全息可观测量在黑洞阈值以上的大型N转级数存在一个精度界限，该界限由D-膜瞬子扇区的数据决定，并发展了基于Écalle理论的大型N resurgence方法，以捕捉超出e^{-N}瞬子的e^{-N^2}引力鞍点渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息理论中大型N展开的非微扰结构及其与黑洞物理的联系，特别是瞬子效应如何影响可观测量的精度。

Method: 运用Écalle的resurgence理论，分析大型N转级数的Borel求和性，结合D-膜瞬子贡献的渐近分析，推导精度界限与解析障碍。

Result: 发现D-膜瞬子和的生长假设下存在解析障碍，导致大型N转级数不可完全Borel求和，从而确立精度界限；并在1/16-BPS指数中验证了热AdS与黑洞背景间D-膜瞬子数的对偶关系。

Conclusion: 大型N转级数的精度受限于D-膜瞬子数据，该限制与双锥虫洞等拓扑结构存在深层联系，为全息非微扰结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: We propose that the large $N$ transseries of a holographic observable above the black hole threshold admits a precision bound, a limit on how precisely the transseries can determine the observable at finite $N$, set by the data of its D-brane instanton sectors. In doing so, we develop methods in large $N$ resurgence, based on the work of Écalle, that capture the $e^{-N^2}$ asymptotics of gravitational saddles beyond the $e^{-N}$ instantons. Under an assumption on the growth of the D-brane instanton sums that we expect in the black hole phase, we find an analytic obstruction to the resummation of the large $N$ transseries. This obstruction sets the precision bound. As our main example and consistency check, we study the $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS index of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills and its black hole. We derive relations between the D-brane instanton data on thermal AdS and on the black hole, in the form of a duality that exchanges the numbers of D-brane instantons on the two backgrounds. In the index, these relations reproduce the actions of D3-branes wrapped on the black hole. We conclude with our expectations for generic situations and their relation to the double-cone wormhole.

</details>


### [192] [The $(5,5)$ String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17275)
*Justin Kaidi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种 $N=(2,1)$ 异质弦，其右行模在 $\mathbb{R}^{2,2}$ 上、左行模具有 $(5,5)$ 符号并附加 $\mathrm{II}_{3,3}$ 格，无需引入零流，保持四维时空；所有态（含无限塔与十六重超对称）均为无质量；中性玻色子对应Kutasov-Martinec行列式作用量，其对偶为自对偶度规耦合自对偶麦克斯韦场的三次作用量，并由单个超场统一加入费米子；带电态耦合由左行模物理态构成的李超代数结构常数给出；作者提出其经典靶空间理论是一种以该李超代数为规范代数的自对偶规范理论——所有弦模均表现为规范场，引力内生于规范代数，从而实现闭弦所有模式的纯三次场论描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造一个无需零流、保持四维时空、且所有激发态均为无质量的 $N=(2,1)$ 异质弦模型，以探索闭弦模式的统一规范理论描述，特别是引力如何自然地嵌入规范结构之中。

Method: 基于 $N=(2,1)$ 超共形世界面理论，构造右行模在 $\mathbb{R}^{2,2}$、左行模在 $(5,5)$ 签名下并附加偶自对偶格 $\mathrm{II}_{3,3}$ 的异质弦；通过BRST协变量子化提取物理态谱；利用Kutasov-Martinec行列式作用量及其对偶形式分析中性玻色子动力学；引入单个超场统一描述超对称；结合左行模物理态的OPE/散射振幅识别其李超代数结构；基于规范原理将全部物理态诠释为规范场。

Result: 确认该模型具有四维靶空间、无零流需求、全谱无质量及十六重超对称；中性扇区等价于自对偶度规耦合自对偶Maxwell场的三次超对称作用量；带电扇区的相互作用由左行模物理态构成的李超代数结构常数完全确定；提出其经典有效理论是该李超代数上的自对偶规范理论，其中引力场亦为规范场分量。

Conclusion: 该 $N=(2,1)$ 异质弦提供了一个候选的、纯三次的、超对称的、四维闭弦场论实现：所有弦激发（含引力子）皆为同一李超代数的规范场，暗示引力可完全几何化为规范对称性的表现，为弦论低能极限的统一规范描述提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study an $N=(2,1)$ heterotic string with right-movers on $\mathbb R^{2,2}$ and left-movers of signature $(5,5)$, obtained by adjoining the lattice $\mathrm{II}_{3,3}$. Unlike the standard $N=(2,1)$ strings it requires no null current, and its target space remains four-dimensional. All of its states, an infinite tower with sixteen supercharges, are massless. For the neutral bosons, the Kutasov-Martinec determinant action is dual to a cubic action for a self-dual metric coupled to self-dual Maxwell fields, and we complete it with fermions using a single superfield. For the charged states we give evidence that the couplings are the structure constants of a Lie superalgebra formed by the physical states of the left-movers. We are thus led to propose that the classical target theory is a self-dual gauge theory for this superalgebra: every state of the string is a gauge field, and gravity is part of the gauge algebra. This would be a purely cubic field theory of all the modes of a closed string.

</details>


### [193] [Bound States of Two D0-Branes and the Three-Graviton Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17325)
*Sergio Sanjurjo,Themistocles Zikopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文数值求解了M理论中SU(2) BFSS矩阵模型的基态，该态对应于两个D0-膜的零能束缚态（即十一维带两单位动量的引力子），并验证了其波函数具有强耦合核心与渐近幂律尾部；幂律系数即三引力子耦合常数，数值结果与Herderschee-Maldacena解析计算高度一致；作者进一步证明该耦合在矩阵模型中受超对称保护，并由未重整化单圈D0-D0相互作用导出；最后给出关于新近提出的激发束缚态的表示论论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证BFSS猜想中D0-膜多体基态与M理论引力子的对应关系，特别是三引力子耦合这一关键可观测量的非微扰实现与超对称保护机制。

Method: 基于SU(2) BFSS矩阵模型的哈密顿量进行大规模数值对角化，结合波函数渐近分析、超对称代数约束及群表示论方法。

Result: 1) 数值复现了三引力子耦合常数，精度显著；2) 证明该耦合在矩阵模型中为超对称保护量，不被量子修正；3) 从单圈D0-D0散射导出其精确值；4) 给出激发束缚态存在的表示论判据。

Conclusion: BFSS矩阵模型成功实现了M理论低能引力动力学的关键特征，三引力子耦合作为超对称保护量，在非微扰水平上严格成立，支持BFSS猜想的自洽性与完备性。

Abstract: We numerically solve for the ground state of the $SU(2)$ BFSS matrix model for M-theory. This state corresponds to the zero-energy bound state of two D0-branes or, by the BFSS conjecture, to the eleven-dimensional graviton with two units of momentum along the M-theory circle. The wavefunction exhibits a strongly coupled core, as well as an asymptotic power-law tail where the D0-branes are well separated. The coefficient of this power-law decay is the three-graviton coupling, previously computed by Herderschee and Maldacena, which our numerical solution reproduces with high accuracy. We also show that this coupling is protected by supersymmetry in the matrix model, and derive its value from the non-renormalized one-loop interaction of the two D0-branes. Lastly, we provide a simple representation-theoretic argument regarding the recently proposed excited bound states of the model.

</details>


### [194] [Decoupling Dynamics: A Tale of Two Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17327)
*Andrea Antinucci,Oscar Lewis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过动态全息规范理论（dynamical holographic gauge theory）统一描述QFT对称性拓扑场论（SymTFT）的方法，揭示了连续阿贝尔对称性动力学规范前后SymTFT的演化机制，并在(2+1)维体空间中给出了完整的量子定义，涵盖非反常与反常情形，包括四维边界上混合轴矢反常下剩余非可逆手征对称性的SymTFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解连续对称性动力学规范如何改变QFT的对称性结构（特别是SymTFT），并建立其量子精确描述，以统一处理拓扑缺陷、电荷与反常，同时剥离动力学流信息。

Method: 将SymTFT嵌入一个(d+1)维动态全息规范理论中，通过改变该理论的边界条件实现连续阿贝尔对称性的动力学规范；结合全息极限与光子截断（photon truncation），在希尔伯特空间与算符层面给出SymTFT的量子定义。

Result: 证明规范前后的SymTFT是同一动态全息理论的不同拓扑极限；给出了(2+1)维体空间中SymTFT的完整量子构造；对四维边界含混合轴矢反常的情形，复现了规范矢量对称后残留的非可逆手征对称性的SymTFT。

Conclusion: SymTFT并非孤立的拓扑对象，而是动态全息理论在特定边界条件与极限下的涌现结构；连续对称性的动力学规范本质上是选择不同的拓扑极限，从而自然解释SymTFT的变更及其对称性破缺/增强现象。

Abstract: The symmetries of a $d$-dimensional QFT are known to be captured by a topological theory in one dimension higher, the Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT), which is invariant under gauging finite symmetries of the QFT, but not under dynamical gauging of continuous symmetries. We show that the distinct SymTFTs of a $d$-dimensional QFT before and after dynamical gauging of a continuous Abelian symmetry arise as different topological limits of the same dynamical holographic gauge theory in $(d+1)$-dimensions. Gauging changes the boundary condition of this dynamical theory and thereby selects a different topological limit, explaining why the associated SymTFT changes. We give a fully quantum definition of these SymTFTs at the level of Hilbert spaces and operators by combining suitable limits of the bulk holographic theory with photon truncation. This construction clarifies how topological symmetry defects, charges, and anomalies survive while the dynamical information carried by currents is discarded. We study non-anomalous and anomalous examples, providing a complete quantum analysis for $(2+1)$-dimensional bulks. For a four-dimensional boundary theory with a mixed axial-vector anomaly, we recover the SymTFT of the non-invertible chiral symmetry that remains after gauging the vector symmetry.

</details>

### [195] [Monopole crystal and effective theory for a monopole-enhanced 3D U(1) gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15922)
*Alessandro Mariani*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了带单极子增强项的U(1)格点规范理论，发现强单极子耦合下形成反铁磁Néel序基态；通过推广Polyakov机制导出有效sine-Gordon模型，并预言了随单极子化学势变化的一阶相变和随规范耦合变化的Ising普适类相变，最后通过数值模拟验证了部分预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解单极子增强型U(1)格点规范理论中非微扰单极子凝聚与相结构的关系，特别是稀疏近似失效时的新物态与有效理论构建。

Method: 结合解析方法（推广Polyakov机制、有效场论推导、单极子化学势与规范耦合的相变判据分析）与数值方法（蒙特卡洛模拟以确定相图）。

Result: 发现大单极子增强下出现反铁磁Néel有序基态；导出描述低能激发的有效sine-Gordon模型；预言单极子电荷主导转变对应的一阶相变及规范耦合驱动的Ising类相变；数值结果支持部分解析预言。

Conclusion: 单极子非稀疏极限下仍可建立可控有效理论，其相结构兼具拓扑序破缺与对称性破缺特征，揭示了格点U(1)规范理论中单极子多体关联的新物理范式。

Abstract: Recently, a version of U(1) lattice gauge theory on the cubic lattice with a monopole enhancement term was considered in the literature. In this work, we further our understanding of this theory with a mixture of analytical and numerical methods. We show that for sufficiently large monopole enhancement, the monopoles are no longer dilute and they arrange themselves into an antiferromagnetic, Néel-ordered ground state. Nonetheless, a variant of Polyakov's argument for the dilute excitations over the dense ground state leads to an effective sine-Gordon model which is qualitatively similar to that of the standard theory. We also predict analytically the presence of first-order transitions as the monopole chemical potential is varied at fixed gauge coupling, corresponding to a change in the dominant monopole charge. Similarly, we predict a transition in the Ising universality class as the gauge coupling is varied. We then use numerical methods to study the phase diagram of the theory and check some of our predictions.

</details>


### [196] [Critical behavior of the bosonized Schwinger model with uniform matrix product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16092)
*Takuya Okuda*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过哈密顿量格点离散化与变分均匀矩阵乘积态（VUMPS）方法，结合Symanzik有效理论约束外推，精确计算了θ=π下二维量子电动力学（Schwinger模型）的临界质量，结果为(m/g)_c = 0.333 555(5)，与最新交错费米子结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定θ=π下Schwinger模型的临界质量，以检验对称性破缺、拓扑效应及格点QFT中连续极限外推的系统误差控制能力。

Method: 采用玻色化后的非紧致标量场哈密顿量格点离散化；使用VUMPS算法在无限链上变分求解基态，严格保持电荷共轭对称性；局域希尔伯特空间采用自由极限下单点约化密度矩阵的谐振子本征基；连续外推拟合函数受Symanzik有效理论所预言的格点修正幂次约束。

Result: 获得θ=π时临界耦合比(m/g)_c = 0.333 555(5)，不确定度达10^{-6}量级，与交错费米子格点计算结果在同等精度下一致。

Conclusion: 玻色化+VUMPS+Symanzik约束外推方案可实现高精度连续极限物理量提取，验证了θ=π处手征对称性破缺相变的存在及其普适临界行为。

Abstract: Quantum electrodynamics in two spacetime dimensions, also known as the Schwinger model, admits a dual formulation in terms of a non-compact boson field. Using a Hamiltonian lattice discretization of the bosonized model, we compute the critical mass of the continuum Schwinger model at $\theta=\pi$. We numerically obtain the ground state on an infinite chain by the variational uniform matrix product state (VUMPS) algorithm, preserving charge conjugation exactly. As a truncated basis for the local Hilbert space, we employ the oscillator basis that diagonalizes the one-site reduced density matrix of the free limit. We constrain the fitting functions for the continuum extrapolation using the Symanzik effective theory for lattice corrections. Our result for the critical mass, $(m/g)_c = 0.333\,555(5)$, agrees with recent staggered-fermion determinations at comparable precision.

</details>


### [197] [Numerical tests of chiral fermion weights with $η$-invariant phases in the 3450 model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16876)
*Tatsuhiro Misumi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文数值检验了一种新提出的将连续手征费米子耦合到插值格点规范场的方法，该方法采用Pauli-Villars正则化模长与高一维Atiyah-Patodi-Singer η-不变量定义的相位；在二维无反常U(1)模型中，验证了其对Polyakov环、真空极化质量、零模下关联函数及相位一致性的高精度再现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造满足手征对称性与反常匹配原理的格点手征费米子方案，克服传统Wilson或Ginsparg-Wilson费米子在连续极限下相位不确定性和反常实现困难的问题。

Method: 采用Pauli-Villars正则化定义费米子权重模长，并以高一维Atiyah-Patodi-Singer η-不变量作为整体相位；在二维torus与cylinder几何上，对U(1)规范场进行线性插值，并数值计算Polyakov环、真空极化张量、十二点关联函数及相位响应。

Result: 在零磁通下Polyakov环相对误差<0.04%（含APS相位）vs ~3%（无APS相位）；诱导质量m/e=2.82089≈5/√π；单位磁通下十二点关联函数模长误差<0.8%，相位误差由1.8–3.0 mrad降至0.2–0.5 mrad；异常自由组合下不同三维延拓引起的相位变化相互抵消至<0.2 mrad。

Conclusion: APS相位是精确实现手征反常与拓扑敏感物理的关键，该方案在数值上严格满足反常匹配、相位一致性与连续极限下的手征性。

Abstract: We test numerically a recently proposed prescription for coupling continuum chiral fermions to interpolated lattice gauge fields, in which the fermion weight has a Pauli-Villars (PV) regularized magnitude and a phase given by an Atiyah-Patodi-Singer (APS) $\eta$-invariant in one higher dimension. We use the anomaly-free two-dimensional $U(1)$ theory with left-handed charges $3,4$ and right-handed charges $5,0$, whose torus determinant is known exactly. At zero magnetic flux, with gauge fields interpolated from lattice links, the Polyakov loops evaluated on a $16\times16$ grid of flat connections agree with the exact values within $0.04\%$, whereas omitting the APS phase substantially reduces them to about $3\%$ of the exact result. The vacuum-polarization response yields an induced mass $m/e=2.82089$, close to $5/\sqrt{\pi}=2.82095$. At unit flux, where zero modes make the determinant vanish, we calculate a twelve-point correlator on a cylinder with the generalized APS boundary condition. For three smooth backgrounds its magnitude agrees with the exact result within $0.8\%$. Without the $\eta$-invariant its phase deviates by $1.8$-$3.0$ mrad, and with it by $0.2$-$0.5$ mrad. The phase of each charged fermion changes by $15$-$46$ mrad when the gauge field is extended differently into the third dimension, but the change cancels to below $0.2$ mrad in the anomaly-free combination.

</details>


### [198] [Automatic O(a) Improvement for Wilson Twisted Mass Fermions in the Hamiltonian Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16658)
*Carsten Urbach*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用Symanzik有效哈密顿量框架分析Wilson twisted mass费米子，证明了在最大扭角下自动O(a)改进，并推导了clover方案中O(a)反项的结构，指出各向同性欧氏时空中的clover项分裂为两个具有独立系数的项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统处理格点QCD中Wilson费米子的离散化误差，特别是O(a)截断误差，需构建与对称性相容的有效理论框架；传统基于Euclidean作用量的Symanzik展开不便于处理Hamiltonian形式的格点理论。

Method: 采用新发展的Symanzik有效哈密顿量方法，仅依据格点哈密顿量的对称性构造有效哈密顿量（不依赖Euclidean时空中的Symanzik有效作用量），结合canonical变换消除壳上冗余项，并分析O(a)算符的独立结构。

Result: 1) 严格证明在最大扭角下Wilson twisted mass费米子自动实现O(a)改进；2) 证实通过质量与Wilson平均亦可实现O(a)改进；3) 推导出clover方案所需的O(a)反项结构；4) 发现各向同性Euclidean时空中的clover项在Hamiltonian框架下分裂为两个独立系数的项。

Conclusion: Symanzik有效哈密顿量是分析格点费米子系统离散误差的有力工具，尤其适用于非Euclidean或Hamiltonian表述的格点理论；该框架揭示了传统clover项在更基础层面上的对称性分解，为高精度格点计算提供更普适的O(a)改进方案。

Abstract: We apply the recently developed Symanzik effective Hamiltonian framework to Wilson twisted mass fermions. The effective Hamiltonian is constructed solely based on the symmetries of the lattice Hamiltonian without recourse to the Symanzik effective action in Euclidean space-time. We use it to prove automatic $O(a)$ improvement at maximal twist, as well as $O(a)$ improvement via so-called mass and Wilson average. Moreover, we derive the structure of $O(a)$ counterterms pre-requisite for clover on-shell improvement of Wilson fermions. We show how to remove on-shell redundant contributions using canonical transformations, and find that the well known clover term in isotropic Euclidean space time splits into two terms with independent coefficients.

</details>


### [199] [$NΩ$ Dibaryon near Unitarity from Physical-Point Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16989)
*Liang Zhang,Yan Lyu,Sinya Aoki,Takumi Doi,Tetsuo Hatsuda,Song Zhang,Yu-Gang Ma*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用HAL QCD方法，在物理夸克质量下的(2+1)味格点QCD中计算了NΩ系统的⁵S₂相互作用，发现其具有吸引力并支持一个接近幺正极限的浅束缚态；给出中子-Ω和质子-Ω束缚能分别为0.61 MeV和1.25 MeV（含库仑修正），为NΩ费米散射与双重子实验提供第一性原理基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NΩ系统因无共同价夸克，可规避价夸克Pauli排斥，是理想的双重子候选者；需从第一性原理确认其是否存在束缚态。

Method: 采用HAL QCD方法，在物理π介子质量（m_π≈137 MeV）的(2+1)-味格点QCD模拟中提取⁵S₂ NΩ势，并通过T矩阵极点分析确定束缚能；对pΩ通道额外计入有限电荷半径的库仑吸引修正。

Result: NΩ⁵S₂相互作用在可分辨距离内呈吸引性，支持一个近幺正的浅束缚态；B_{nΩ} = 0.61(+0.41/-0.30)(+0.60/-0.18) MeV；计入库仑后B_{pΩ} = 1.25(+0.46/-0.39)(+0.69/-0.25) MeV。

Conclusion: NΩ系统在⁵S₂波存在弱束缚态，结果为未来高能核物理中NΩ femtoscopy及双重子实验提供了首个基于QCD第一性原理的定量预测与基准。

Abstract: The $N\Omega$ system is a promising dibaryon candidate because its constituents share no valence quarks and therefore avoid valence-quark Pauli repulsion. We determine the $^{5}S_{2}$ $N\Omega$ interaction in (2+1)-flavor lattice QCD at physical quark masses ($m_\pi\simeq137~\text{MeV}$) using the HAL QCD method. The interaction is attractive over the resolved range and supports a shallow state near unitarity. A direct $T$-matrix pole analysis gives $B_{n\Omega} = 0.61\,(^{+0.41}_{-0.30})\,(^{+0.60}_{-0.18})~\text{MeV}$; including finite-charge-size Coulomb attraction yields $B_{p\Omega} = 1.25\,(^{+0.46}_{-0.39})\,(^{+0.69}_{-0.25})~\text{MeV}$. These results provide a first-principles benchmark for future $N\Omega$ femtoscopy and dibaryon searches.

</details>
